saṃnidhattāṃ ca me vāṇī bhuvanatrayavanditā

sevitaṃ bhūtasaṃghaiśca bhūtapreta piśācakaiḥ

tadā brahmā samutthāya praṇamya stutavānprabhum

prasīda jagatāṃ nātha ! prasīdānandadāvyaya !

sahasrapādayuktāya śarvāya paramātmane

huṃkārāya namastubhyaṃ vaṣaṭkārāya te namaḥ

sarvajñāya śaraṇyāya sadyo jātāya te namaḥ

anantāsana saṃyuktamanantāyānta kāriṇe

yogapīṭhāntarasthāya yogine yogadāyine

parāya parameśāya nirguṇāya namo'stute

adhvānadāyine tubhyaṃ ṣaḍadhvāya namo'stute

pratyāhārasthitānāṃ tu pratiṣṭhānāṃ sthitāya ca

dhāraṇābhyāsa yuktānāṃ purastātsaṃsthitāya ca

dhyeyāya dhyeyagamyāya dhyeyādhyeyāya te namaḥ

samādhinā'dhigamyāya samādhānāya te namaḥ

sahasrākṣaradevāya ayutākṣararūpiṇe

* * * * * * * ṇi tvayāntamapurā prabho

pitāmaha mahāprājña ! mayā nigaditā śṛṇu

aṣṭāṣṭau ca sthānāni śṛṇu ca tapodhana

naimiśe devadevaśa gayāyāṃ prapitāmaha

puṣkare jaraje gandhiṃ viśvantavimaleśvaram

ujjayinyāṃ mahākālyaṃ mahākāṭe madotkaṭam

rudrakoṭyāṃ mahāyogī mahāliṃgaṃ sthaleśvare

svarṇākṣe sahasrākṣaṃ vṛṣe ca vṛṣabhadhvajam

sūryaṃ kanakhale caiva bhadrakarṇe sadāśivam

ūrdhvaretāstri siddhāyāṃ kāraṇḍe tu kapardinam

dhyāne śuddheśvaraṃ caiva gāyatryā muktakeśvara

samaliṃgaṃ virasthāṇuṃ kapāliṃ karavīrake

dīpte marukeśvaro jñeyaṃ nepāle tu paśoḥ patim

gaṃgāsāre amara * * * * mokāramare

jaṭilaṃ vāmeśvaraṃ caiva saumyaṃ ca kakudeśvarau

japyeśvare triśūlaṃ ca śrīśaile tripurāntakam

hemakūṭe virūpākṣaṃ bhūbhuvaṃ gandhamādane

dānavānāṃ vadhārthāya vārāhaṃ vinyakeśvare

laṃkāyāmardhadevaisaṃ pātāle hāṭakeśvaram

aṣṭaṣaṣṭiṃ tu nāmāni mayā proktāni te'nagha

iti vīratantre aṣṭaṣaṣṭisthāna (kathanaṃ nāma) prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ

tantrāṇāmudbhavaṃ kiṃcittatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

tanoti vipulānarthāntatvamantra samāśritān

kāmikaṃ yogajaṃ cintyaṃ kāraṇaṃ cājitaṃ tathā

vijayaṃ caiva niśvāsaṃ svāyaṃbhuvaṃ tathānalam

candrajñānaṃ ca bimbaṃ ca prodgītaṃ lalitaṃ tathā

kiraṇaṃ vātulaṃ caiva aṣṭāviṃśatisaṃhitāḥ

kāmikaṃ caiva viprendra trividhaṃ bhedamucyate

cintyaṃ ca ṣaḍvidhaṃ proktaṃ saptadhā kāraṇaṃ bhavet

suprabhedasya vikhyātaṃ bhedamanyanna vidyate

vimalaṃ ca viśeṣeṇa aṃśumānsuprabhedakam

vīraṃ ca rauravaṃ caiva makuṭaṃ vimalaṃ tathā

svāyaṃbhuve viśeṣeṇa tadvidhena pravakṣyate

vīratantraṃ ca viprendra trayodaśaṃ bhedamucyate

makuṭākhyaṃ mahātantraṃ dvividhenābhidhīyate

candrajñānaṃ ca bimbaṃ ca caturdaśavidhaṃ bhavet

prodgītākhyaṃ mahātantraṃ ṣoḍaśaṃ bhedamucyate

siddhatantraṃ ca viprendra caturvidhena cocyate

śarvoktaṃ tu mahātantraṃ paṃcabhedena cocyate

kiraṇākhyaṃ mahātantraṃ navadhā bhedamucyate

tantrakāyaṃ pravakṣyāmi tava snehādviśeṣataḥ

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ devavanmukhamucyate

mukhaṃ prati triṇetraṃ syātsarvāṃgalakṣaṇairyutaḥ

tatra kāyaṃ mahāproktaṃ sarvakāmārthasādhanam

caturdaśāṃgulaṃ vāpi paṃcadaśāṃgulaṃ tu vā

aṣṭādaśāṃgulaṃ vāpi navadaśāṃgulaṃ tu vā

dvāviṃśatirvā viprendra kārayeddeśikottamaḥ

utsedhaṃ cāṣṭadhā hitvā ekāṃgulaṃ caiva vistaram

tribhāgaṃ tu caturbhāgaṃ madhye suṣiramucyate

śeṣamāyamiti proktaṃ guṇaireva vivarjayet

padaṃ pratiguṇe vṛddhiraṣṭabhirhrāsayettataḥ

saptaviṃśatibhirhrāsaṃ labdhamiṣṭamiti smṛtam

triṃśadbhirhrāsayettāni śeṣaṃ tithirihocyate

śeṣaṃ vāramiti proktaṃ āyaṃ vyayaṃ ca yoni ca

āyādhikaṃ vyayaṃ hīnaṃ saṃpatkaramihocyate

tasmātsarvaprayatnena āyahīnaṃ na kārayet

dhū?mraśvā gardabhaṃ kāko doṣānyetāni varjayet

evaṃ parīkṣya bahudhā kartavyaṃ pustakaṃ param

viśvāmitro'tha vālmīkiḥ kāśyapaścāṅgirāstathā

caturdaśāṃgulādīni kramādvinyasya dehinaḥ

jagatī śatvarī caiva atiśatvari eva ca

brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca īśvaraḥ śiva eva ca

caturdaśāṃgulādīni devānāmihacocyate

ṣaṭpaṃktirvātha paṃcairvā lekhayetpustakaṃ tataḥ

paṃktirevaṃ samākhyātaṃ tattannāmādhidevatā

ṛṣīṇāṃ bhūrjapatre tu lekhayettu dvijottama

dvau pārśvakampa saṃyuktau pustakāyamatatsamam

śivāgre maṇḍapaṃ kuryādīśāne dakṣiṇe'pi vā

vedībāhye tu paritaḥ paṃca kuṇḍāni kārayet

sthaṇḍilaṃ vā prakartavyaṃ lakṣaṇena samanvitam

yathāṃkurārpaṇaṃ kuryātpaṃcāhe vā tryahepi vā

sa hiraṇyaṃ sakūrcaṃ ca somarājādhidaivatam

vinyasettu viśeṣeṇa pālikāścāṣṭadikṣu ca

hṛdayena tu mantreṇa bījāni vinyasettataḥ

suptadeśe samutthāpya pālikāḥ sthāpayettataḥ

svarṇena tantunā vipra kautukaṃ bandhayeddhṛdā

lepayetpustakaṃ tatra śayane śāyayettataḥ

śālyardhāṃstaṇḍulānnyasya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilānnyaset

tattadāgamabījena śāyayecchayanopari

ādyakṣarasamāyukta manusvārasamanvitam

vastreṇāveṣṭayettatra pustakaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

sarasvatīṃ nyasettatra pustakasya tu madhyame

śuddhodakena saṃpūrya sa kūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam

mūlamantreṇa vinyasya arcayettu viśeṣataḥ

sa vastraṃ sahiraṇyaṃ ca sakūrcādīni vinyaset

balavikariṇīṃ balapramathinīṃ sarvabhūta damanīm

hṛdayādīṣasaṃghaiśca pūjayettu vidhānataḥ

pālāśaḥ pūrvatobhāge aśvatthaṃ dakṣiṇe tathā

udumbaraṃ tathaiśānyāṃ pālāśairvāpi hūyate

samidhaṃ sadyamantreṇa īśānenājyameva ca

sarṣapānsadyamantreṇa netreṇa tu yavāṃstathā

sahasraṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vāpi hūyate

dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā pustakaṃ sparśayeddhṛdā

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāttadanantare

tattadāgamabījena prokṣayedbardhanīṃ tataḥ

havirnivedayetpaścāttāmbūlaṃ dāpayettataḥ

brāhmaṇānpūjayettatra saptakanyāśca pūjayet

kāpālaṃ paṃcarātraṃ ca ṣaḍete samayāḥ smṛtāḥ

tantrasaṃkaradoṣeṇa rājarāṣṭraṃ vinaśyati

aṣṭāviṃśatibhedena śaivabhedaṃ vidhīyate

dīkṣitasya subīrasya śivabhaktiratasya ca

adīkṣitaṃ na śrotavyaṃ na deyaṃ yasya kasya cit

tantrāvatāramevoktaṃ paṃcākṣaraṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre ṣaṭsahastrikāyāṃ tantrāvatāro dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

śrīpaṃcākṣarakalpaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ vada me prabho !

namaskāreṇa saṃyuktaṃ śiva ityakṣaradvayam

tadvargāntaṃ saptamaṃ syātpaṃcākṣaramiti smṛtam

caturthyā tu samāyuktaṃ paṃcākṣaramiti smṛtam

yakāreṇa samāyuktaṃ paṃcākṣaramihocyate

ṛgvedo jāyate vipra nakārākṣaramucyate

atharvavede vākāraṃ yakāramitihāsake

śikāraṃ taijasamāyuktaṃ vākāraṃ vāyumeva ca

nakāraṃ śvetavarṇaṃ tu īśvareṇa samāyutam

sthānaṃ sadyomukhaṃ caiva nakārākṣaramucyate

anuṣṭhubhchandasāyuktaṃ mantriṇā ca samāyutam

vākāraṃ raktavarṇaṃ tu skandastatrādhidaivatam

puruṣasya mukhaṃ sthānaṃ vākārākṣaramucyate

sāvitrī chandasā yuktaṃ bhāradvājena saṃyutam

paṃcākṣaramiti proktaṃ praṇavaṃ śṛṇu tatvataḥ

akāraṃ brahmadaivatyamukāraṃ viṣṇudaivatam

ṛgvedastu akāraḥ syādyajurvedamukārakam

akāraṃ sātvikaṃ vidyādukāraṃ rājasaṃ bhavet

ukāraṃ pauṣṭikaṃ vidyānmakāramābhicārakam

makāraṃ pītavarṇaṃ syādevameva krameṇa tu

oṃkāreṇa samāyuktaṃ ṣaḍakṣaramihocyate

aṃgulyā prajapedekaṃ paṃcadhā mṛṇmayaṃ bhavet

śatasaṃkhyaistu vijñeyaṃ pravālaistu sahasrakam

padmākṣaiḥ koṭiguṇitaṃ sauvarṇairdaśakoṭikam

nyāsaṃ kuryāttato mantrī paṃcākṣaramihocyate

kaniṣṭhādyaṃguṣṭhāntaṃ saṃhāranyāsamucyate

karanyāsaṃ purākṛtvā aṃganyāsaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

śikāraṃ karṇadeśe tu makāraṃ hṛdaye nyaset

sarvāṃgeṣu ca viprendra oṃkāraṃ vinyasettataḥ

paṃcāśatpaṃcaviṃśairvā japaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ

japaṃ kuryāttu viprendra praṇavena samāyutam

nadītīre śataṃ vidyātsahasraṃ devatālaye

paṃcaviṃśantato lakṣairvaiṣṇavaṃ padamāpnuyāt

praviśecchivamīśānaṃ * * saṃsāra sāgarāt

iti vīratantre ṣaṭsahasrasaṃhitāyāṃ śrīpaṃcākṣarakalpastṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

sarvapāpa(praṇāśā)rthaṃ mantroddhāraḥ kathaṃ bhavet

mananaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ trāṇaṃ saṃsārasāgarāt

gaṃgākūle samudre vā prabhāse vā viśeṣataḥ

anyeṣu śuddhadeśeṣu mantramuddhārayetkramāt

snānaṃ kṛtvā tu viprendra tatsthāne mantramuddharet

ācāryastu śucirbhūtvā kṛtopavāsasaṃyutam

ratnena * * * vipra akṣaraṃ lekhayeddhṛdā

bindornādassamutpannastasmācchaktirajāyata

kalāyā vyaṃjanaṃ jātaṃ vyaṃjanā * * mantrakaḥ

avargaṃ ca kavargaṃ ca cavargaṃ ca ṭavargakam

ādivargeṣu ṛ-ṛca caturakṣaraṃ varjayet

pūrvaṃ hṛsvaṃ samākhyātaṃ paraṃ dīrghamihocyate

sadyojātaṃ tu gokṣīra sadṛśaṃ varṇamucyate

puruṣaṃ kuṃkumaprakhyaṃ īśānaṃ sphaṭikaṃ bhavet

dvinetrāṃ cāruvadanāṃ sustanāṃ ca suyauvanām

ṣadaṃgaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi hṛdayaṃ ca śirastathā

indudhūmraṃ suvarṇaṃ ca kiṃciddhūmraṃ tathaiva ca

ṣaḍaṃgānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mantraṃ varṇaṃ vicintayet

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktāṃ yajñopavītasaṃyutām

strīrūpaṃ bhāvayedbrahmā ṣaḍaṃgaṃ puruṣo bhavet

atasīpuṣpasaṃkāśaṃ padmapuṣpasamaṃ bhavet

raktapaṃkajasaṃyuktaṃ bhinnāñjanasamaprabham

akṣarasyādisaṃyuktaṃ binduṃ caiva samāyutam

śivaviṣṇusamārūḍhaṃ prathamaṃ cāṣṭavargake

prathamasyākādasaṃgṛhya śivaviṣṇusamāyutam

paṃcamasya prathamaṃ syādvitīyaṃ saptamasya ca

aṣṭamasyādi samāyuktaṃ māyānalasamanvitam

prathamasya dvitīyaṃ tu bindunādasamanvitam

gaṇāmbikā ca caṇḍeśo nandīśaśca tathaiva ca

bhṛṃgīśaśca mahāsenaḥ svanāmādyakṣarairyutam

vāyussomastatheśāno brahmā viṣṇuśca saṃyutam

vajraṃ śaktiṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ pāśaṃ tathāṃkuśam

svanāmādyena bījena bindunādasamanvitam

śrīvatsaṃ svastikaṃ śaṃkhaṃ dīpo devāṣṭamaṃgalam

ṣaṣṭhavargāddvitīyaṃ tu caturthādyena saṃyutam

visargeṇa samāyuktaṃ pāśupatāstramucyate

bindunādasamāyuktamācāryaṃ bījamucyate

mātrāṇāṃ rākṣasānāṃ ca * * * gaṇameva ca

mūrtipānāṃ ca śiṣyāṇāmevameva krameṇa tu

yakārādidvayaṃ vargaṃ caturakṣaramucyate

anantāsanaṃ tadviprendra yogāsanaṃ tathaiva ca

vimalāsanaṃ tu viprendra svanāmādyakṣarairyutam

āgneyamaṇḍalaṃ caiva vinyasettu viśeṣataḥ

tatvāmaṇḍalamevoktaṃ svanāmādyakṣarairyutam

vyomavyāpi mahāmantraṃ ṣaṇṇavatyakṣarairyutam

dvādaśasya padasya bahiśyadvivitathā punam

mantramuccāryate vipra sarvakāmānavāpnuyāt

śivaviṣṇusamārūḍhaṃ śivamantramihocyate

vyomaśca rūpāśca vaścaivaṃ rūpāyaśvata eva ca

taśca yaścaiva dhvāyaśca pukamaśvadṛdṛmaśca ca

daśca pāśca vineraśca neśca miśca na eva ca

evaṃ krameṇa viprendra vinyasedastramantravit

niścayaśca gīpaścaiva tumaguśca jātaśca bhāśca ta

vyoścaścaiva ye yośca omaśca dhaśca eva ca

viśca sarva oṃ bhraśca jaścedīriśca vāyaniśca

evaṃ krameṇa vinyāsaṃ sarvakāmānavāpnuyāt

agastyena samāyuktaṃ bṛhatīcchandasā yutam

vasiṣṭhena samāyuktaṃ prakṛticchandasā yutam

ācāryasya tu bījasya ṛṣicchando devatā

atriṇā ca samāyuktaṃ paṃcavaktreṣu dīkṣitāḥ

jagatī śakvarī caiva anuṣṭubtriṣṭhubheva ca

āgameva tu bījāni pravakṣyāmyanupūrvaśaḥ

* * * * dvitīyaṃ tu bindunādavibhūṣitam

saptamasyādisaṃyuktaṃ trayodaśa samanvitam

tṛtīyasyādimaṃ gṛhya saptamasya tṛtīyakam

cintyākṣaramidaṃ proktaṃ dvitīyasyādisaṃyutam

kāraṇākhyamidaṃ proktaṃ prathamasyādi saṃyutam

bindunādasamāyuktamajitākṣaramucyate

dvādaśākṣarasaṃyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitam

dvādaśa svara saṃyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitam

saptamasya dvitīyaṃ tu māyābījena saṃyutam

antyākṣarasamāyuktamādyakṣarasamanvitam

aṣṭavargāttṛtīyaṃ tu analena samāyutaḥ

suprabhedākṣaraṃ proktaṃ saptavargasya cādikam

vijayaṃ caiva viprendra paṃcamasyāntasaṃyutam

niśvāsatantra mevoktamaṣṭavargatṛtīyakam

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ vīratantramihocyate

akṣarāṇāṃ paraśreṣṭhaṃ rauravaṃ procyate mayā

māyānalasamāyuktaṃ makuṭākṣaramucyate

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ vimalaṃ caiva kathyate

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ candrajñānamihocyate

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ bimbasyākṣaramucyate

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ prodgītaṃ ca mayocyate

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ lalitasya tu mantrakam

analena samāyuktaṃ siddhatantramihocyate

saptamasya dvitīyena bindunādasamanvitam

dvādaśasvarasaṃyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitam

māyānalasamāyuktaṃ bindunāda samanvitam

dvādaśasvarasaṃyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitam

māyānalasamāyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitam

ṛṣicchandodaivatānvakṣye āgamānāṃ viśeṣataḥ

tavarūpeṇa saṃyuktaṃ kāmikādīni sapta ca

jagatīcchandasāyuktaṃ viṣṇustatrādhidaivatam

adīkṣitaṃ na śrotavyaṃ na deyaṃ na prakāśataḥ

aṣṭavargatṛtīyāntā ādyakṣarasamāyutam

śaktibījamidaṃ proktaṃ sarvakarmārthasādhanam

śuddhasphaṭika saṃkāśaṃ tryakṣaṃ daśabhujairyutam

divyāmbaradharaṃ devaṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam

pāśaṃ cāṃkuśaghaṇṭāṃ ca nāgavahniṃ ca vāmake

etatsakalamākhyātaṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadam

iti vīratantre ṣaṭsahasra saṃhitāyāṃ mantroddhāraścaturthaḥ paṭalaḥ

viśeṣaṃ śrotumicchāmi taṃ me brūhi maheśvara

ayogyāni ca puṣpāṇāṃ patrāṇāṃ ca tapodhana

karavīra sahasrebhyo bilvapatraṃ viśiṣyate

padmapatra sahasrebhyaḥ puṇḍarīkaṃ viśiṣyate

śatapatrasahasrebhyaḥ sahasraparṇaṃ viśiṣyate

bakapuṣpasahasrebhyo dhurdhūrakaṃ viśiṣyate

droṇapuṣpasahasrebhyo apāmārgaṃ viśiṣyate

kuśapuṣpasahasrebhyaḥ śamīpuṣpaṃ viśiṣyate

vyāghātakasahasrebhyaḥ śaṃkhapuṣpaṃ viśiṣyate

vyāghrapuṣpasahasrebhyaḥ sadābhadraṃ viśiṣyate

* * * * * * dvipra bṛhatpuṣpaṃ viśiṣyate

śvetārkasya sahasrebhyo bṛhaddroṇaṃ viśiṣyate

svarṇadhurdhūrapuṣpaṃ tu nīlotpalasamodbhavaiḥ

punaḥ pūjāḥ prakartavyāḥ sarvalokahitāya ca

droṇapuṣpa sahasrebhyo jñeyaṃ viṣṇukrāntiṃ ca pastathā

bilvapatrasamaṃ jñeyaṃ * * * * * * * *

padmapuṣpasamaṃ jñeyaṃ vakulaṃ pa * * * *

śvetaṃkusuma * raktotpalamapāmārgaṃ bhavet

gandhapuṣpī gandhapuṣpā padmapuṣpasamaṃ bhavet

vedasī se kā cotpalaṃ caiva kalhāraṃ kṛntakaṃ tathā

musalī palāśapuṣpaṃ ca droṇapuṣpasamaṃ bhavet

sūryāvartaṃ ca nāgaṃ ca * * * * * meva ca

vajraṃ ca padmarāgaṃ ca marakatamauktikāni ca

nīlotpalasamā jñeyā sarvaratnāni caiva hi

aṃkolasaṃkaraścaiva priyaṃguścampakeva ca

pālāśapuṣpaṃ viprendra raviraśmisamāyutam

yavagodhūmanīvāra brīhivaiṇavakāni ca

aṃkurāṇi prakartavyaṃ pūjayecchivamādarāt

kadambārjuna sindūraṃ kuḍumaṃ kusumāni ca

kadalīpuṣpapunnāga nāganandī tathaiva ca

madhūka dhūrtapuṣpaṃ ca karapāddhirdhavāni vā

girirāgakakau girikakaca dhurdhūraka samaṃ bhavet

puṣpāṇāmapyalābhe tu phalānāṃ pūjayettataḥ

mūlānāmapyabhāve tu bhaktyā caiva tu pūjayet

ketakīmādhavīpadmaṃ madhudhī pūjayetsadā

nīlakaṇṭhī ca mevaśca talapoṭakameva ca

evamevaṃ krameṇaiva yo naraḥ pūjayetsadā

puṣpādhyāyamidaṃ proktaṃ karṣaṇasya vidhiṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre ṣaṭsahasrasaṃhitāyāṃ puṣpalakṣaṇaṃ paṃcamaḥ paṭalaḥ

etatsarvaṃ samāsena brūhi me parameśvara

puṣyamāsādiṣaṇmāsaṃ devatānāṃ divā bhavet

divāmāsaṃ ca gṛhṇīyādrātrimāsaṃ ca varjayet

maitrādityamakhāsvātī tvaṣṭārvāśravaṇāśśubhā

dvitīyā ca tṛtīyā ca paṃcamī ṣaṣṭhisaptamī

pratipatpaurṇamāsyāṃ vā kārayettu śivadvijaḥ

somabhārgavasaumyānāṃ vārā śreṣṭhatamāḥ smṛtāḥ

aṣṭamasthā grahāḥ sarve kartuḥ kurvantu susthitam

grahaṇe viṣuve caiva ayane vyatipātake

evaṃ parīkṣā kartavyā sarvasaṃpatkarāya ca

ūṣaraṃ kukumimiṣṭarja? valmīkāḍhyaṃ ca varjayet

pītā vaiśyā iti proktā kṛṣṇā śūdrā iti smṛtāḥ

adhikaṃ cottamaṃ jñeyamadhamaṃ nyūnameva ca

evaṃ parīkṣya bahudhā kuryātkarṣaṇamuttamam

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyukto valavānmadhyamovaraḥ

kṣīravṛkṣayugaṃ badhvā gocarmeṇaiva bandhayet

ācāryaḥ sakalīkṛtya navavastreṇa veṣṭayet

tīkṣṇa śṛṃgāya vidmahe vedapādāya dhīmahi (tanno vṛṣaḥ) pracodayāt

prāgudakkarṣayettatra ācāryaṃ pūjayettataḥ

ācāryāya pradātavyaṃ sahagau vṛṣabhau tathā

śalyaṃ vimocayettatra lakṣaṇena samanvitam

pūjānte sparśayankartāyidaṃgastannirīkṣayet

asthiśalyaṃ śirasparśāntadadhastaṃ ca dvayamānataḥ

trihaste śṛṃgalāśalyamaṃgha sparśāttu deśikaḥ

aṃgārakatrihaste tu bāhusparśanamucyate

kaniṣṭhāṃ guṣṭayoḥ sparśātkarārdhaikāṃgamucyate

nābhikorparaṃ haste jānusparśameva ca

kughnaṃsatvā tu viprendra gomayenānulepayet

aśvatthodumbaro vātha śaṃkuṃ grāhayettataḥ

tatra madhye sthitaṃ śaṃkuṃ tanmānena tu bhrāmayet

pūrvāhne ca parāhne ca śaṃkucchāyāṃ viloñcayet

vāstu devānsamarbhyarcya puruṣaṃ pūjayettataḥ

mahātapasi saṃbhūtaṃ bhūtākāraṃ mahābalam

prāgeva tattanmānena madhyasthenaiva kārayet

paṃcagavyena saṃprokṣya ṣaṇṇavatyena mantrataḥ

daśasūtrāṃśca prāgagrānudagagrāṃstathaiva ca

madhye navapadaṃ kṛtvā caturdikṣu ca vinyaset

dakṣiṇe tu vivasvāṃśca ṣaṭpadaṃ caiva bhujyate

uttare ṣaṭpadaṃ bhuṃkte pṛthivīdhara eva ca

sāvitra savitāścaiva āgneyyāṃ tu padadvayam

rudraradrojayaścaiva vāyavyāṃ tu padadvaye

ādityassatyakabhraṃśa antarikṣaśca pūrvaśaḥ

gandharvo bhṛṃgarāgaśca bhṛṃgaścaiva tu dakṣiṇe

puṣpadantaśca varuṇa apsaraśca tathaiva ca

uttareṣu tathā rogānnāgo mukhyastathaiva ca

pūtanā nair-ṛte caiva pāparākṣasi vāyave

evaṃ vinyasya devāṃśca puruṣaḥ procyate mayā

āpāpavatsake vipra vāmabāhuriti smṛtaḥ

rudro rudrodayaścaiva vāmapādaṃ prakīrtitam

mitraścaiva tu liṃgasya vṛṣaṇaṃ pṛṣṭhameva ca

māse bhādrapadeṣvādi pūrvaśīrṣakamucyate

phalgunyāditrayo māsāḥ paścimaṃ śira ucyate

pūrvaśīrṣaṃ tu madhyāhne sāye dakṣiṇaśīrṣakam

uṣaḥkāle tu sarveṣu uttare śira ucyate

vakṣye balividhiṃ yaśca tāvacchṛṇu tapodhana

pradadya phalabhakṣyaṃ tu samaryāke viśeṣataḥ

pūṣṇe ca dadhimitre ca mahiṣakṣīrameva ca

mahendre piṣṭamevaṃ tu * * * madhurucyate

kakvānaścata * * the bimbāna ca grahakṣate

samudramatsyaṃ viprendra bhṛṃgarājabaliṃ tataḥ

iṣṭeva śatapiṣṭe dauvāriśca gravake

asure madhukaṃ vidyācchoṣe ca tilataṇḍulaiḥ

mukhye'pi dhānyacūrṇaṃ tu dadhi saṃyutameva ca

ṛgvedave gulaṃ caivaditidaivastu modakaiḥ

āpe tathā pavatse ca ajamāṃsaṃ ca matsyakam

indre balistathā dadyādindrajaye tu mudgakam

carakīghṛtamenaṃ tu pitāri lavaṇaṃ tathā

sphaṭitaḥ kṛtopavāsaṃ ca evameva baliṃ dadet

ācāryaśca śucirbhūtvā upavāsasamanvitam

praṇavādyena saṃyuktaṃ baliṃ dadyācchivadvijaḥ

evameva mahāśāntissarvasaṃpatkarāya ca

śivalokamavāpnoti śivavanmodate sadā

karṣaṇasya vidhiḥ prokta ācāryalakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre ṣaṭsahasrasaṃhitāyāṃ karṣaṇa vidhiḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ

kasminkule tu saṃjātāstatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

samāsātkathayiṣyāmi tanme nigadataḥ śṛṇu

sarveṣāṃ ca guruścaiva gururityabhidhīyate

apramatto bhavennityaṃ nityaṃ vā gurupūjakaḥ

śaivāssaptavidhā jñeyāsteṣāṃ bhedaṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

tṛtīyastu mahāśaivāścaturthaścānuśaivakaḥ

saptamo vā nyaśaivastu śaivānāṃ bheda ucyate

mahāśaivaḥ kevalo vipraḥ kṣatriyo vaiśya eva ca

savarṇāmbaṣṭha madhyasthau kulālapāraśavāstathā

anyeṣāmihajātī nāmanya śaiva mihocyate

kauśikaḥ kāśyapaścaiva bhāradvājo trireva ca

śivena dīkṣitāścaiva śivabrāhmaṇa ucyate

kauśikaścottaramukhaḥ kāśyapaścaiva dakṣiṇe

atrirūrdhvamukhaṃ jñeyaṃ evameteṣu dīkṣitāḥ

kauśikaḥ śivabhaktastu kāśyapastvīśvareṇa tu

atressadāśivaścaiva umādhūrtastu dīkṣitaḥ

śivadīkṣātparaṃ jātaḥ śivabrāhmaṇa ucyate

gokarṇe tu bharadvājo nāgarikastu gautamaḥ

śaivaṃ pāśupataṃ somaṃ lākulaṃ ca caturvidham

eteṣāmapi tantrāṇāṃ navatvaikasya tu saṃkaraḥ

tantrasaṃkaradoṣeṇa rājarāṣṭraṃ vinaśyati

kāmikaṃ yogajaṃ cintyaṃ kāraṇaṃ cājitaṃ tathā

vijayaṃ caiva niśvāsaṃ svāyaṃbhuvamathānalam

candrajñānaṃ ca bimbaṃ ca prodgītaṃ lalitaṃ tathā

kiraṇaṃ vā tulaṃ tvetāstvaṣṭāviṃśati saṃhitāḥ

ācāryaṃ śivamevāhuḥ śiva ācārya ucyate

saubhadro viṣayākhyātā kauśalā māgadhāstathā

keralaścāryadeśaśca mālavaṃ ca marūṇi ca

jonakāstuluvāścaiva karṇāṭakaśca deśataḥ

kumārī himavanmadhye * * * * * * * *

haihalaṃ bagharaṃ jonyaṃ varjayelliṃgakarmaṇi

ādiśaivakule jātassa tu vaidika ucyate

dīkṣitasya kule jātassarvāgamaviśāradaḥ

sarvāyavasaṃpūrṇaḥ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutaḥ

kriyādi jñānaparyantatantrajñāna viśāradaḥ

sanābhiḥ kunakho * * bālo vṛddhastathaiva ca

atidīrgho'tihṛsvaśca atisthūlaḥ kṛśastathā

nagnaprabhā tathā kṣībaśmaśruromādivarjitaḥ

anabadyāśca viprendra gaṇakāśca tathaiva ca

vyasanāḥ krūrakarmāṇaḥ pāradārika corakāḥ

kuṣṭhinaścakṣuhīnāśca badhirāḥ śrutidūṣakāḥ

rājarāṣṭrāpi naśyanti tasmindeśe na varṣati

ādiśaivena kartavyaṃ pratiṣṭhādyarcanāntakam

sa gotreṇa tu sambandhaṃ na kartavyaṃ kadācana

eṣāmṛṣīṇāṃ viprendra jātānāṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ

upanayana kālānte maṇḍale pūjayecchivam

sthiraliṃgaṃ tu śūdrāṇāṃ spṛśyate tvabhicārataḥ

ātmārthaṃ pūjayennityaṃ sarveṣāṃ jātireva ca

ācāryahastayordadyācchiva lokamavāpnuyāt

śivadvijānāṃ sarveṣāṃ na doṣāya prakalpayet

ācāryahastayordadyācchiva lokamavāpnuyāt

patitaṃ cānucāṇḍāla dhūpadeśevikaṃ śivim

grāmakukkuṭamevaṃ tu saṃspṛṣṭvāgniṃ tathaiva ca

snānaṃ kṛtvā tu viprenda sa prāsādena saṃyutam

prayaṃ prātastrayaṃ sāyaṃ * * madyādayācitam

ṣoḍaśānāṃ kriyāṇāṃ ca pūrvamevācarettathā

ācāryalakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ dīkṣā(yā) maṇḍalaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīra tantre ṣaṭsahasrasaṃhitāyāṃ ācārya lakṣaṇaṃ saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ

maṇḍalaṃ kīdṛśaṃ deva tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana

udyāne caiva goṣṭhe vā mahātaṭākatīrake vā

atha śuddhagṛhe vāpi kārayenmaṇḍapaṃ tataḥ

śrutihastaṃ paṃcahastaṃ vā ṣaḍhastamadhamatrayam

daśaikādaśahastaṃ tu dvādaśaṃ cottamaṃ bhavet

ṣoḍaśastambha saṃyuktaṃ paṃktimānamathocchraye

kramukaṃ veṇudaṇḍaṃ vā vakulī ketakī tathā

nālikerasya patreṇa vidheyamācchādanaṃ tataḥ

digdhvajaiśca samāyuktaṃ vitānopariśobhitam

evameva krameṇaiva pūrvaṃ bhūmiṃ parīkṣayet

vaiśyasya vasudhā pītā kṛṣṇā śūdrā iti smṛtā

vaiśyasya ṣaḍrasā jñeyā śūdrastu kaṭukā smṛtā

mūtragandhā tu vaiśyasya śūdrā viṣṭhānugandhinī

hastamātraṃ khanedbhūmiṃ pūrayettena pāṃsunā

hīnācāpyadhamaṃ jñeyamadhamaṃ ca vivarjayet

paṃcagavyena saṃprokṣya śivamantreṇa deśikaḥ

pāṃsubhiśchāyayitvā tu dhiṣṇyaṃ samyak prakalpayet

pūrvāgraṃ saptahastaṃ syādudagagraṃ tathaiva ca

īśo jayanta ādityā satyakaśca bhṛśastathā

bhṛṃgaśca pitṛ sugrīvo varuṇaścāsurastathā

ardhārdhadvipadaṃ bhuṃkte pūrve tu samarīcakaḥ

ardhārdhadvipadaṃ bhuṃkte uttare pṛthivīdharaḥ

rudrajayaśca viprendra īśānaika padasthitāḥ

carakī vidārī caiva pūtanā pāparākṣasī

pururṣaṃ pūrvaṃ saṃkalpya * * rīke śiro bhavet

sāvitrīṃ candra ca viprendra dakṣīmabahurucyate

indrajayastathaiva syāddakṣiṇaṃ pādamucyate

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca varuṇaścaiva pūjitam

pūrve catuṣpade caiva aṣṭādaśa tu bhājayet

evameva krameṇaiva caturdikṣu ca bhrāmayet

bāhyakārṇaṃ pade cārdho vartayedvṛttameva ca

prativāraṇarekhā ca ekāṃgulena kārayet

aṃgulenaiva bhūmitvā * * * * * * * *

aṃgule naiva viprendra upaśobhāṃ ca vartayet

kesaraṃ tu tribhāgaikaṃ karṇikāṃ caiva vartayet

mokṣadaṃ maṇḍalaṃ proktaṃ śāntikaṃ ca śṛṇuṣvatha

nair-ṛte śānakoṇe vā vāyavyāgneyameva vā

khādirāḥ śaṃkavaḥ kāryā bilvacūtakadambakaiḥ

aṣṭāṃgulasamutsedhaṃ tadardhaṃ nāhamucyate

dalāyāmaṃ ca viprendra caturdaśāṃgulaṃ bhavet

śobhā vapyupaśobhāṃ ca vartayedaṃgulāntarā

maṇḍalaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi muktācūrṇena saṃyutam

gairikaiṣṭaka cūrṇaiśca indranīlasya cūrṇakaiḥ

haridrā cūrṇasaṃyuktaṃ maṇḍale vinyasettataḥ

madhyame pītavarṇaṃ tu agre śvetamihocyate

āgneyyāṃ raktavarṇaṃ ca yāmye kṛṣṇamihocyate

tadante rajanīṃ nyasya śālipiṣṭaṃ tadantake

ekahastaṃ dvihastaṃ vā caturhastamathāpi vā

hastaṃ ca triṃśatirvāpi ita urdhvaṃ na kārayet

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi dalasaṃkhyā vidhīyate

puruṣaṃ pūrvadale nyasya aghoraṃ dakṣiṇe dale

āgneyyāṃ hṛdaye nyasya śiramīśānake dale

kavacaṃ vāyudeśe tu netramīśāna deśake

daityake tu pade caiva śiṣyādhivāsanaṃ bhavet

brāhmīke maṇḍale kuryādevameva krameṇa tu

prokṣayedambhasā caiva puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ

āvāhayettato devaṃ sadyo jātena mantrataḥ

sāṃnidhyaṃ puruṣeṇaiva īśānena tu pūjayet

nivedaccaraṇaṃ tasmātsarvopadaṃśa saṃyutam

pātraśeṣaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ taṃ tacchiṣyāya pradāpayet

ācamya vidhinānena dantenāspṛśya prāśayet

dantakāṣṭhaṃ tataḥ kuryāddvādaśāṃguladairghyakam

kṣīravṛkṣamapāmārgaṃ dantadhāvanamucyate

śodhayitvā malaṃ vipra ācamya ca vidhānataḥ

aghoreṇa tu mantreṇa bhasma śirasi vinyaset

śivāṃgairbrahmabhiryuktaṃ japitvā paṃcaprāśayet

śiṣyaṃ cādhivasenmantrī maṇḍalasya tu dakṣiṇe

agnikuṇḍaṃ prakartavyaṃ dvātriṃśatṣoḍaśaṃ tu vā

vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā sarvakuṇḍāni kārayet

pālāśakhadirāśvattha plakṣanyagrodhameva ca

samidājya caruṃ lājānbaliṃ ca sarṣapaṃ tathā

samidhaṃ sadyamantreṇa ājyaṃ hṛdayameva ca

śilānyanneyamaṃ caiva śālīṃ ca puruṣeṇa tu

śivāya daśahotavyaṃ vidyeśādviguṇaṃ bhavet

triguṇaṃ lokapālāstraṃ homayeddeśikottamaḥ

sa vastrānsāpidhānāṃśca samagre hemasaṃyutān

dhānyopari vidhātavyaṃ candanodakapūritam

puruṣaṃ pūrvataḥ sthāpya aghoraṃ dakṣiṇena tu

aiśānyāmīśamantreṇa āgneyyāṃ hṛdayena tu

tattanmantreṇa saṃyuktaṃ tattatkumbheṣu deśikaḥ

hṛdayena tu mantreṇa naivedyaṃ dāpayecchive

śubhāśubhāni svapnāni yatnena tu parīkṣayet

viṣṭhānulepaṃ rudhirasrutiṃ ca svapneṣu gamyaṃ gamanaṃ ca dhānyam

paśyate piśajaṃ svapne mahā(na)rthamavāpnuyāt

arpitaṃ pilitaṃ vāpi pakvaṃ vā śubhavṛkṣake

śālikṣetre mahaṃ pakvaṃ dṛśyate śālilābhakam

dṛśyate puṣpamālāyā arthalābhaṃ vinirdiśet

svapnepi dṛśyate vipra dhanalābhaṃ vinirdiśet

śārikāṃ vā bhayaṃ vāpi dṛśyet kuṭumbavardhanam

patraṃ vā cārthalābhaṃ vā labhate'tra saṃśayaḥ

dātavyamapi svapnepi (tu) duḥkhanāśaṃ bhavanti vai

janaṃ vā vānaraṃ vāpi sarpaṃ vāpi ca dṛśyate

prāsādārohaṇaṃ vāpi samudraṃ dṛśyate naraḥ

ādityamaṇḍalaṃ svapne darśanādrājyamāpnuyāt

arthalābhamavāpnoti darśanādarthamāpnuyāt

vidyālābhaṃ dvijānāṃ tu itarastu dhanaṃ labhet

amedhyaṃ darśanaṃ bhakṣye arthalābhaṃ vinirdiśet

raktapāne mahāvyādhirājyaṃ ca dadhibhojanam

mahatsukhamavāpnoti māsā maraṇamāpnuyāt

mūḍho vā līyate pakṣe māsānmaraṇamāpnuyāt

nigalaṃ cātra dṛśye tu mahadvyādhimavāpnuyāt

pādadeśe sthitā nārī vyādhitairmaraṇaṃ bhavet

māsānmaraṇamāpnoti svapnānte dṛśyate yadi

saṃnyāsinaṃ kṣapaṇaṃ boddhaṃ dṛśyate maraṇaṃ bhavet

mahāstriyamavāpnoti svapnānte dṛśyate yadi

kiṃśukaṃ raktapuṣpāṇi karavīraṃ tu varjayet

narāṇāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ śreṣṭho rājānāṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet

dhenuṃ (ca) vṛṣabhaṃ caiva siṃhaṃ caiva mṛgaṃ tathā

kulasthaṃ sarṣapaṃ mudgaṃ dṛśyate vyādhimāpnuyāt

eteṣāṃ tu phalaṃ dṛṣṭvā dhanadhānyaṃ mavāpnuyāt

yastu paśyati svapnānte dhanamāyuryaśo bhavet

māṃsaṃ vāpi ca matsyaṃ vā labdho śubhadaṃ bhavet

rājā pīḍāmavāpnoti pratārairvyādhimādiśet

raṇḍāṃ ca muṇḍāṃ vāpi nagnastrīṃ vāpi paśyati

purīṣaṃ vāpi mūtraṃ vā na svapne darśayettataḥ

śālyannaṃ bhuṃjate yastu āyuḥ śrīkīrtivardhanam

śubhe tu praviśeddīkṣāmaśubhe homamācaret

sarvaṃ haratu me pāpaṃ dūrvā duḥsvapnanāśinī

svapnaṃ tu prathame yāme vatsaratraya pākanam

caturthe viśvayāme ca ṣaṇmāsātsidhyate tataḥ

aruṇodayavelāyāṃ saptāhātsiddhimāpnuyāt

svapnaṃ parīkṣya bahudhā prabhāte snapanaṃ bhavet

dīkṣāmaṇḍalataḥ proktadīkṣāmārgamataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre ṣaṭsahasrasaṃhitāyāṃ dīkṣāmaṇḍalavidhiraṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ

śaivāssaptavidhājñeyā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana

tathostu saṃghaṭīkṛtya sā dīkṣā iti kathyate

anādi śaivaṃ prathamamādiśaivaṃ dvitīyakam

avāntarastu śaivaṃ tu paṃcamaṃ tu vidurbudhāḥ

śaivāssaptavidhāḥ proktāsteṣāṃ bhedaṃ śṛṇuṣva ca

śivadvijāśca viprendra ādiśaivāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

gautamaścaiva paṃcaiva paṃcavaktreṣu dīkṣitāḥ

śivadīkṣā na kartavyā kartavyā cetsuniṣphalā

snapanaṃ cāṃgaśudhyarthaṃ kārayettu śivadvijān

adīkṣitaścaturvedī śivapūjāṃ na kārayet

saṇmāsātpatanaṃ yānti yajeddevalakāḥ smṛtāḥ

pūjayedyadi viprastu sa vai devalako bhavet

sthiraliṃgeṣu yatpūjānnakurvanti kadācana

śūdro vāpyantaro vāpi antare śaiva cocyate

pravaraśaivamiti khyātaṃ idamanyacchṛṇuṣva ca

* * * ādiśaivena pratiṣṭhābhyarcanāntakam

śaivabhedā mayākhyātā dīkṣāvidhimataḥ śṛṇu

puṣyaṃ śatabhiṣak caiva dīkṣānakṣatramucyate

mithunaṃ vāpi kanyā vā dhanurvā mīnameva vā

dvitīyā paṃcamī vāpi ṣaṣṭhī vāpi viśeṣataḥ

ete hi tithayaḥ proktā vārāṃścaiva śṛṇuṣva ca

bṛhaspatyudayaṃ vāpi śukrodayamathāpi vā

aṣṭamasthā grahāścaiva paścācchiṣyānupoṣitān

pādaprakṣālanaṃ kuryādācamya vidhinā tataḥ

dukūlapaṭṭasaṃyuktaṃ kārpāsikamathāpi vā

adhamaṃ navahastaṃ tu catustālaṃ tu vistṛtam

sadaśāhatavastreṇa netrabandhaṃ tu kārayet

vyomavyāpi japettatra tatpuruṣeṇa samāyutam

uttamaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ tu aṣṭaniṣkaṃ tu madhyamam

daridrāṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ yathālābhena kārayet

īśānena tu mantreṇa puṣpaṃ haste nidhāpayet

dhyātvā tu parameśānaṃ śivamantreṇa buddhimān

dhyātvā tu parameśānaṃ śivamantreṇa buddhimān

tanmantraṃ sidhyate tasya kuruto vāpyanugraham

nair-ṛtyāṃ śrīkaraṃ proktaṃ anāyuṣyaṃ tu vāyu ca

karṇike patite puṣpe sukhaṃ caiva bhavettadā

udakena samāyuktaṃ bhasma śirasi nikṣipet

śivāgre caiva tu kuṃbhaṃ tu śālyopari ca vinyaset

śivamantramanuddhṛtya sthāpayetkuṃbhameva ca

īśānaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya mukhe tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset

sadyojātaṃ nyasetpādau vinyasecchiṣyadehake

śikhāyāṃ tu śikhāṃ nyasya kavacaṃ hastamadhyataḥ

devasya dakṣiṇe pārśve palāśapatraṃ ca saṃyutam

āsanasthā tu śiṣyāṇāṃ pūrvābhimukhameva ca

pādāṃguṣṭhātsamārabhya dahedyāvacchirontakam

candramaṇḍalasaṃkāśamakāraṃ cātmasaṃbhavam

omityekena malaṃ yogapīṭhamadhomukham

tena plāvitamātmānamārya * ntaṃ vicintayet

na tasya jāyate manyuratra śāstrasya niścayaḥ

mantrādhvā ca padādhvā ca tatvādhvā bhuvanātmakam

satvo rajastamaścaiva ātma trayameva ca

paṃcabrahmaśivāṃgāṃ ca prāsādaṃ tu sadāśivam

tattanmantramanusmṛtya yojayecchiṣyadehake

tatvādhvā hṛdaye nyasya nābhau tu bhuvanātmakam

pādādhvā tu punaryojya pādārabhya śirontakam

aghoramapi cāṣṭau tu tatpuruṣaṃ tathaiva ca

vidyeśvarāṇāmaṣṭau tu gaṇeśānāṃ catuṣṭayam

evaṃ padādhvā vinyasya tatvādhvā ca punarnyaset

śrotraṃ cakṣuśca jihvāśca ghrāṇaṃ tvakcaiva paṃcamaḥ

śabdasparśorūparaso gandhaścāpi ca paṃcamam

rāgo māyā ca vidyā ca kalā niyatimeva ca

śuddhavidyā śuddhakalā sadāśivaḥ śivaḥ param

bhuvanādhvā tato nyasya kalānāṃ tu śivāntakam

adhastāddhṛdayādīni kalpayetkalpavittamaḥ

rauravaṃ kumbhīpākaṃ ca mahārauravameva ca

uṣṇasaṃtāpanaṃ caiva lohataptatapastathā

dhanurnirayamādhānaṃ kṣurikānīyameva ca

talaṃ ca vitalaṃ caiva nitalaṃ ca talātalam

parahalokaśca bhuvarlokaḥ svarlokaśca tathaiva ca

tapolokaṃ satyalokaṃ gandharvākṣayalokakam

īśānaṃ devalokaṃ ca brahmalokaṃ tathaiva ca

anante satyalokaṃ tu sūkṣmalokamataḥ param

ekarudrastrimūrtiśca śrīkaṇṭhaśca śikhaṇḍi ca

golokaṃ śivalokaṃ ca kramameṣāṃ prakīrtitam

nābhimūlātsamārabhya yāvaddhṛdayadeśakam

akāraṃ mūrdhnideśe tu ākāraṃ tu lalāṭake

ḷkāraṃ dakṣiṇe gaṇḍe ḹkāraṃ vāmagaṇḍake

dantapaṃktidvaye nyasya aṃ aḥ ityakṣaradvayam

ṭavargaṃ dakṣiṇe pāde tavargaṃ vāmapādake

makāraṃ hṛdaye nyasya yakāraṃ caiva vinyaset

lakāraṃ mede vinyasya śakāraṃ cādhideśake

hakāraṃ prāṇamityāhu lakāraṃ meḍhrameva ca

navatriṃśatkalā vipra kādipādāntavinyaset

nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathaiva ca

tato mohau kṣudhānidrā mṛtyumāyajarā mayā

dhṛtiḥ kāryā ca rātrī ca bhrāmaṇī mohinī tathā

navatriṃśatkalāvyātā devadevena dhīmahi

ākāśaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya paṃcatatvānkramānnyaset

tatvā vaiśyaghoreṇa bhuvanādhvā puruṣeṇa tu

evameva krameṇaiva śiṣyadehe nyasettataḥ

hṛdaye brahmaṇaḥ proktaṃ aśvinyāṃ hastayornyaset

deśikaścātmanā vipra śivastatraiva tiṣṭhati

yaddravyaṃ pātraśeṣādi śivasya parameṣṭhinaḥ

śivamuddiśya sarvaṃ tu vastradhānyādikāñcanam

dīkṣāvidhiriti proktaṃ abhiṣekamataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre dīkṣāvidhiḥ navamaḥ paṭalaḥ

abhiṣekaṃ kathaṃ deva tatsarvaṃ vada me prabho

sumuhūrte sulagne ca yoge ca karaṇānvite

athāṃkurārpaṇaṃ kuryāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

nīvāraṃ yavaśālī ca tilaṃ vaiṇavameva ca

kalamāśāliyavoktaṃ vā sarṣapaṃ śimbameva vā

payasā kṣālayedvipra ! prācyādīnāṃ tu bāpayet

savastraṃ sahiraṇyaṃ ca sakūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam

bījāni vāthayettatra tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettataḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi paṃcāśatpaṃcaviṃśatiḥ

sūtrapātraṃ tu kartavyaṃ śālinā vikirettataḥ

taṇḍulārdhatilairlājādarbhaiścaiva paristaret

sa sūtrānsāpidhānāṃśca sa vastrānhemasaṃyutān

savyādīśānaparyantaṃ sthāpayedaṣṭakumbhakān

ananteśaśca sūkṣmaśca śivottamaścaikanetrakaḥ

adhidevāssamākhyātā dravyāṇāṃ ca śṛṇuṣva ca

vardhanīṃ gauribījena suvarṇaṃ tatra nikṣipet

ārakūṭaṃ ca vipulaṃ kāṃsyaṃ pūrvaṃ nyasettataḥ

haritālaṃ ca * * * * * * * canātilām

rasena navakaṃ caiva vinyasettu viśeṣataḥ

pālāśaṃ cādi khadiraṃ tārkṣya nyagrodhameva ca

nair-ṛte sthāpite kumbhe puṣpāṇāṃ navakaṃ nyaset

* * * devaśirau ca nivedyāṃ ca tu sāgare

paṃkajaṃ caṃpakaṃ caiva kumudaṃ nīlotpalaṃ tu vā

nandyāvartaṃ ca ghātaṃ ca navapuṣpamihocyate

candanaṃ kuṃkumaṃ nīlotpalaṃ khagajānaśca vānaram

saumyāyāṃ sthāpite kumbhe navabījāni vinyaset

godhūmayavamudgāṃśca sarṣapaṃ ca yathākramam

lakṣmī ca sahādevī ca viṣṇukrāntiṃ trayaṃbalā

vinyasettu viśeṣeṇa svasvanāmānumantrataḥ

aṣṭadikṣu ca homaḥ syāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

palāśakhadirāśvattha plakṣanyagrodha bilvakaiḥ

samidājyacarunlājānyavaśālitilānapi

yāvadvai kumbhasaṃkhyā tu tāvatsaṃkhyā tu homayet

yāgasthānasya vāruṇyāṃ mahādevasya * * * *

adhyayanaṃ ca kartavyaṃ caturvedaiścaturdiśi

yāgasthānasya vāruṇyāṃ mahādevasya pūrvataḥ

brahmairaṃgaiśca saṃyukta vyomavyā(pi)samāyutam

gandhapuṣpādinā pūjya prāsādena tu mantrataḥ

sā varṇaka * atrādya * nta siṃhahani bhojanam

* * * * * * * * * * parvatavāhanam

ācāryāya pradātavyaṃ mānaṃ ca nivedayet

sagulaṃ goghṛtairyuktaṃ kadalīphalasaṃyutam

ācāryaḥśiṣyasaṃyuktaṃ bhojayedvidhipūrvakam

lājānta saṃyuktamapūvaṃ ghṛtasaṃyutam

catuṣpathe pathe caiva dvipathekai ca vidhirave

evamevakrameṇaiva abhiṣekaṃ tu kārayet

vidhinā kārayedyastu tasya mṛtyurna vidyate

abhiṣekaṃ mayā proktaṃ śaivācāraṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre ṣaṭsahasrasaṃhitāyāṃ abhiṣeko nāmadaśamaḥ paṭalaḥ

kīdṛśānīha sarvaṃ tadvaktuṃ sarvamihārhasi

niṣekādi sapiṇḍāntaṃ śrūyatāṃ kamalāsana

śucau deśe samaṃ kṛtvā sitānvinyasettataḥ

hastamātreṇa viprendra kuryādvai sthaṇḍilaṃ tataḥ

vyapoṣya kalaśaṃ tatra hastau prākṣālayettataḥ

vyāhṛtyāvānayedagniṃ ṣolkālayettataḥ

paristīrya tato darbhairhastamātraiścaturdiśi

dakṣiṇānuttarānagrānadhastādvinyasettataḥ

parīdhīnhastamātreṇa * * * * * caiva hi

hṛdayena tu darbhāṃśca paridhī śikhayā saha

darbhoparinyasedvipra śarāvaṃ samidhā saha

prokṣayedambhasā caiva gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

sthaṇḍilādakṣiṇe bhāge brāhmaṇānbhojayettataḥ

dvedarbhāgrasamāyuktaṃ tiraḥ pavitraṃ vinyaset

darbhānekera viprendra ājyādhiśravaṇaṃ bhavet

sruvaṃ caiva tu darbhāṃśca mārjayeddeśikottamaḥ

pariṣecanaṃ tataḥ kuryāddhṛdayena samanvitam

prajāpataye svāheti sruveṇaiva tu homayet

athājyabhāgaṃ hotavyaṃ agnesvāheti mantrataḥ

yuktau vahādi ṣaḍbhiśca bhūyate kramaśodhanam

ṛtusnātānāṃ strīṇāṃ ca ṛṭusaṃgamanaṃ śṛṇu

tatra cāgnimukhaṃ kāryaṃ ūrdhvavaktraṃ caturbhujam

hṛdayena tu mantreṇa hūyate kramaśodhanam

evameva prakāreṇa rākṣasānāṃ tu tṛptaye

ayantayonirityāśca agnayerājñayā tanu

praṇītasyoṣṭha prakṣālya prokṣiṇyāmudakena tu

vardhanīṃ vyāpayettatra prokṣayedambhasā tataḥ

śayane śāyayettatra daṃpatibhyāṃ samanvite

viṣṇuryoniti mantreṇa mūlaliṃgaṃ bhavanti vai

yonisaṃgamanaṃ kuryāduktareta sa saṃyutam

somābhivardha ityuktaṃ tadasyāṃgaṃ ca tiṣyati

niṣekametaduddiṣṭaṃ tava snehādviśeṣataḥ

iti vīratantre ṛtu saṃgamanavidhiḥ ekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

rohiṇī revatīhastā śravaṇaṃ cānanāyakam

saumya jīvasitendūnāṃ drekkāṇāvasāṃśakam

puruṣaṃ jīvanāmeti pradakṣiṇamathācaret

sadyena homayettatra paṃcaviṃśatirāhutiḥ

tataḥ paṃkti viprendra sakalīkṛtavigrahaḥ

puruṣeṇodasparśa garbhādhānaṃ bhavanti vai

iti vīratantre garbhādhānavidhiḥ dvādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

māsadvayānte kartavyaṃ sarvālaṃkāra saṃyutam

guruśukrabudhajñānāṃ udayaṃ śastameva ca

aṃkurāṇīha kartavyaṃ puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ

agnimukhaṃ samāstīrya yukto vāhādi bhūyate

garbhau agnau vidhānaṃ tu ājyāhutimathācaret

nyagrodhaudumbarāśvattha palāśaṃ khādirā saha

dakṣiṇe caiva nāsā ca asmai devāna mantrataḥ

prācīnaṃ vāpyudīcīnaṃ cchedadarśapaṃ tataḥ

dhruvāyetatyarundhatyāmiti bruvandarśayettataḥ

stridharmasyā tu strīṇāṃ tu kurute nātra saṃśayaḥ

tīrthānāṃ tu nakhākośa vānādireva ca

antarvartnyāḥ patiḥ kuryānna prajā bhavati śruvam

iti vīratantre puṃsavanavidhiḥ trayodaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

māsāccatuṣṭaye prāpte ṣaṇmāse vāpi kārayet

rohiṇyāṃ caiva puṇye ca uttarā ca punarvasu

sīmantonnayanaṃ kuryācchubhāsye śubhalagnake

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra agnimukhaṃ ca kārayet

ājyā hutiṃ kariṣyāmi * * * * * * * *

kāmayediti mantreṇa rāvikhareṇakhayet

sinībalī khamantreṇa audumbareṇa homayet

vanavāsī cāsīva pāśvetikeśārvanilaṃ badhyate tataḥ

śeṣaṃ puṃsavanādeva kartavyaṃ syātsurottama

iti vīratantre sīmantavidhiḥ caturdaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvā tu viṣṇumabhyarcayettataḥ

agnimukhaṃ ca saṃkalpya pūrvavallakṣaṇānvitam

keśavaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ caiva govindaṃ mādhavaṃ tathā

śrīdharaṃ ca hṛṣīkeśaṃ padmanābhaṃ tathaiva ca

carubhojanaṃ ca kartavyaṃ yāddhṛdavasānake

ājyāhutistathaivaṃ syātpuṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ

aṣṭānāṃ brahmacārīṇāṃ bhojayettu viśeṣataḥ

iti vīratantre viṣṇubalividhiḥ paṃcadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

abhijātaṃ kumāraṃ ca darśayed hṛdayena tu

adbhyassaṃbhūtamantreṇa snāpayedvidhi pūrvakam

īśānādimantrāṇāṃ mūrdhni jighratimucyate

aśvābhava paraśubhaṃ japtvā caiva viśeṣataḥ

brūhitāntena saṃprārcyāṃ hiraṇyena samāyutam

āyurārogyamedhāvīti prāśayet

yastestenemimantreṇa mātussavyastanetreṇa mātustanaṃ dadet

sa caivaṃ sthāpayitvā tu agnimukhaṃ ca kalpayet

hariharaṃ ca mucca * * pa * * tathaiva ca

sadyaṃ ca kamanāya aśvatthāparṇasaṃyutam

* * * * * taṃ ca gau tilasarṣapahomayet

āveśanāvyatra khīphalīkaraṇa homayet

iti vīratantre jātakarmavidhiḥ ṣoḍaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

uttarārevatīhastā śatabhiṣaṅmūlapuṣyakāḥ

śraviṣṭhā conati nakṣatrātpraśastā nāmakarmaṇi

īśānaṃ prājāpatye nāmakaraṇaṃ ca bhūyate

mārgasīrṣe tu puṣyeśaṃ śaṃbhuriti smṛtaḥ

caitre tu sthāṇu nāmaṃ ca vaiśākhe tu śivastathā

śravaṇe sarvanāmaṃ tu bhādramāse triyambakam

eteṣāṃ nāmamuccārya gurornāmastu vācayet

nāmakaraṇaṃ ca mevaṃ tu kartavyaṃ syātsurottama

iti vīratantre nāmakaraṇavidhiḥ saptadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

aśvinī somadaivatyaṃ revatī ca punarvasu

citrādiruttarāścaiva śatabhiṣagrohiṇī tathā

daśamasthā grahāścaiva neṣṭāssarvaprayatnataḥ

īśānena tu mantreṇa svasyātreṇa juhoti ca

sikataiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā aśvatthaṃ sthāpayettataḥ

svānnigrahānnāmayantīti evameva vidhīyate

iti vīratantre upaniṣkramaṇavidhiraṣṭādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

ṣaṣṭhame māsi saṃprāpte annaprāśanameva ca

śravaṇaṃ ca śraviṣṭhā ca hastasvātyanurādhakaḥ

puṣyaṃ caiva tu nakṣatrā praśastāścānna bhojane

pūrvedyurbhojayedviprānyugmaṃ nādimukhaṃ tataḥ

śubhaṃ saṃpūjayettatra citreṇacchādayettathā

putra ca mūrdhni madhye tu atijighratpratipadmajaḥ

paṃcabrahmasamāyuktaṃ gomaye sapta cāhutiḥ

yāmāta oṣadhīsteti madhuno madhunopya ca

brāhmaṇānbhojayettatra ācāryaṃ pūjayettataḥ

iti vīratantre annaprāśanavidhirekonaviṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

* * * trīṇi saṃprokte śubhanakṣatra saṃyutam

puṣyā punarvasū caiva mṛgaśīrṣakameva ca

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra kuru brāhmaṇa bhojanam

eke sino'tinaktaṃ ca iti hutvā hutiṃ tataḥ

īśānena tu mantreṇa paṃcāśajjuhuyāttataḥ

piṇḍaṃ sākṣata saṃyuktaṃ śarāve sādhayettataḥ

keśāśca secayettatra kuṃśena prokṣayettataḥ

karmamadhye śikhāṃsthāpya gavāmabhimukhaṃ tataḥ

kumāraṃ snāpayettatra prokṣayeddhṛdayena tu

svasti vācakasaṃyuktaṃ dadyādācāryadakṣiṇām

iti vīratantre caulavidhirviṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ

garbhāṣṭameṣu viprāṇāṃ rājanyaikādaśaṃ tataḥ

ṣoḍaśābdāvidhiṃścaiva brāhmaṇānbhojayettataḥ

svātī mṛgaśirāścaiva punarvasuḥ prakīrtitaḥ

snānaṃ kṛtvā tu viprenda sarvaṃ cābhi pradakṣiṇam

agnimukhaṃ ca saṃkalpya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

yāvatkuntantatāvatyā vastrayugmaṃ tu veṣṭayet

āpañca ācamitrasya kṛṣṇājinaṃ dadettataḥ

devasyatveti savyena pakvāhutimathācaret

brahmasūktena hutvā tu hutvā vyāhṛti nāmataḥ

catasṝḥ palāśasamidho hotavyaṃ nīcajīvinā

āditya vratapate caiva vratānāṃ vratapatepi ca

agnerdakṣiṇataḥ sthāpya astreṇābhyukṣayettataḥ

brahmacāryasyābhimukhaṃ sāvitrīṃ ca japettataḥ

tatkāle maṇḍalaṃ likhya yathā vidhipurassaram

maṇḍalasyottare bhāge kalaśāṃsthāpayettataḥ

īśānādivyāhṛtibhirnavasamidhā samāyutam

ācāryaṃ pūjanaṃ kuryādgobhūmīkāṃcanādibhiḥ

arghyeṇaiva tu māyāṃśca dadyānmāṇavake tataḥ

prātaḥ snātvā'pa ācamya sūryaśceti jalaṃ pibet

agniśceti ca sānneti kāle kāle pibettataḥ

añjalyodakamādāya vyāhṛtyā sūryābhimukhaṃ sṛjet

* * * sahāraṃ * * * * * * * praviśet

prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā sāvitryāṣṭaśataṃ japet

mitrassametya nayaści siddhasandhyopasthānamācaret

agniṃ caiva paristīrya pariṣecanamācaret

evamevakrameṇaiva sāyaṃ prātaśca homayet

kṣāraṃ ca madhumāṃsādi lavaṇa caiva varjayet

evameva krameṇaiva balaṃ prāyeṇa naktakam

dve striyau samalaṃkṛtya śraddhā medhā prayāmiti

evameva kramaṃ kuryādāyuṣyaṃ balamāpnuyāt

agnimukhaṃ ca saṃkalpya sruksruve ceti mantrataḥ

catasrasamidhā caiva pūrvavaddhūpayettataḥ

palāśamūle daṇḍaṃ ca ajinaṃ mauñjīmaṇi vā sakhā

apa ācamya viprendra jayādiṃ hūyate tataḥ

brāhmaṇānbhojayettatra śivadīkṣāsamanvitān

iti vīratantre upanayanavidhiḥ ekaviṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ

nadītaṭāke gatvā tu navarṣīṃstarpayettataḥ

agnimukhaṃ ca saṃkalpya ṛṣīṇāṃ ca hūyate

punaḥ pariṣecanaṃ kṛtvā yathā vidhi ca hūyate

sahasrasamidhāṃ ca hūyate kramaśastataḥ

iti vīratantre upākarmavidhiḥ dvāviṃśati paṭalaḥ

vayetsadā | surabhimudrābhavedeṣā sarvāriṣṭavināśinī

dvau hastau muṣṭisaṃbadhvā ekaikena tu tā yet

prasārya dakṣiṇe haste aṃguṣṭhamūrdhvasaṃyutam

śiro mudrā bhavedeṣā sarvakāmārthasādhinī

śeṣānparasparaṃ kṛtvā kavaceyaṃ prakīrtitam

uttānau tu karau kṛtvā maṇibandhaṃ tu kārayet

adhamaṃ tāmrapātraṃ syādalābhe mṛṇmayaṃ bhavet

etadāgneyamityuktaṃ mārutaṃ ca śṛṇuṣvatha

mārutākhyamiti proktaṃ śaivalakṣaṇamucyate

bhogadaṃ ca pravakṣyāmi kulālānāṃ dahepyataḥ

catvāri bheda saṃyuktaṃ bhasmanā ca viśeṣataḥ

vyoma vyāpi samāyuktaṃ brahmamaṃgena saṃyutam

mukhaṃ tatpuruṣeṇaiva svītau caiva kaṭī tathā

sadyo jātena mantreṇa uddhūlayedviśeṣataḥ

tripuṇṭraṃ kārayettatra nṛpāṇāṃ vaiśyameva ca

madhyahātprakṛjalairyuktaṃ toyaṃ tamana varjayet

karṇau ca cakṣuṣī caiva nāsikau cāsya eva ca

hṛdi nābhau ca meḍhre ca puyūruku ca jānunī

dvātriṃśatsthānamevoktaṃ bhasmanā lepayettataḥ

hṛdaye jaṭhare caiva nābhau pārśve tu pṛṣṭhake

mūrdhni caiva lalāṭe ca kaṇṭhe bāhu tathaiva ca

aṣṭasthāneṣu viprendra vinyasedbhasmameva ca

vasundharā ca pitarau adhidevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

brāhmaṇādīnāṃ varṇānāṃ vistārasi vivistaraṃ bhavet

jñānāsūcandanaṃ lipya vaiśyārthaṃ pūrṇameva ca

deśikānāṃ tu sarveṣāmanāmikā madhyamatarjanī

dusvapne durjanasparśe āturāṇāṃ tu sarvataḥ

bhojane tuṣakeśaṃ ca citāri nakhānyapi

dadhikṣīra ghṛtetaila miśrite tu viśeṣataḥ

pūrvāhne bhojanekāle gamane'pi tathaiva ca

evameva krameṇaiva bhasma dadhyāttu bhaktitaḥ

bhasmasnānaṃ mayā proktaṃ mantranyāsaṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

iti vīratantre bhasmasnānavidhiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

mantranyāsaṃ kathaṃ deva tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

tavasnehādahaṃ vakṣye śrūyatāṃ kamalāsana

maṇḍapaṃ caiva viprendra gomayenānulepayet

kūrcaṃ caiva tu nikṣipya pūrvanā(tha) vidhipūrvakam

sadyena prokṣayettatra kuśakūrcena vāmataḥ

ghṛtāśikhāgre dvādaśāṃgulye śivatatvaṃ vicintayet

trikoṇamaṇḍale vidyādadvitīyaṃ vicintayet

bhasmabhūtaḥ śivaṃ dhyātvā amṛtaṃ plāvanaṃ kuru

śvetapadmo pariṣṭāttu candrabimbamadhomukham

cintayedamṛtaṃ toyaṃ suṣumnābhinnamastakam

mṛtyunāśanamevaṃ tu iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ

recakaṃ pūrakaṃ caiva kumbhakaṃ trividhaṃ matam

āpūrṇakumbhavattiṣṭhedacalasya tu kumbhakam

hastābhyāṃ saṃspṛśetpūrvaṃ kādipādāntameva ca

divi dikṣu ca dātavyaṃ ūrdhvādhaścaiva vinyaset

galāddve tejasaṃśuddhi lalāṭādvāyu śudhyati

prathamaṃ sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ tu sthitinyāsaṃ dvitīyakam

gṛhasthaḥ sṛṣṭimārgeṇa brahmacārī sthitirbhavet

aṃguṣṭhādi kaniṣṭhāntaṃ sṛṣṭinyāsamudāhṛtam

īśānaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya mukhe tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset

sadyojāte nyasetpādau punaraṃgāni vinyaset

brahmāṇaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya lalāṭe viṣṇurucyate

sarasvatīṃ tu vaśvāre hṛdaye īśvaraṃ tathā

subrahmaṇyasya liṃge ca pārśvayorambikā tathā

akāraṃ mūrdhni vinyasya ākāraṃ tu lalāṭake

ukāraṃ dakṣiṇe nāse ūkāraṃ vāmanāsike

ḷkāraṃ dakṣiṇe gaṇḍe ḹkāraṃ vāmagaṇḍake

dantapaṃktidvaye nyasya aṃaḥ parikīrtitau

ṭavargaṃ dakṣiṇe pāde tavargaṃ vāmapādake

makāraṃ hṛdaye nyasya yakāraṃ tvaci vinyaset

vakāraṃ medodeśe tu śakāraṃ cāsthideśake

hakāraṃ prāṇa ityāhuḥ kṣakāraṃ meḍhrameva ca

sṛṣṭinyāsamiti proktaṃ nyāsaṃ sthitimataḥ śṛṇu

īśānaṃ madhyame caiva anāmikena puruṣaṃ nyaset

sadyojātaṃ tu tarjanyāṃ vinyasetpṛṣṭhameva ca

śikhāṃ kaniṣṭhike nyasya kavacāṃguṣṭhake nyaset

evaṃ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ vinyasya vidyeśānvinyasettataḥ

śivottamaṃ mukhe nyasya ekanetraṃ tu karṇayoḥ

liṃge śrīkaṇṭhamevoktaṃ śikhaṇḍiṃ pādayornyaset

śikhāyāṃ tu śikhāṃ nyasya kavacaṃ stanamadhyataḥ

hastau tu maṇibandhaṃ tu īśabrahmāṇimeva ca

sadyojātaṃ kaniṣṭhe tu vāmadevena nāmikā

īśamaṃguṣṭhake nyasya ṣaḍaṃgānvinyasettataḥ

śikhāmantraṃ tu madhyaṃ syāt kavacaṃ

pādayoḥ sadyamantreṇa kukṣau vāmaṃ nyasettataḥ

īśānaṃ mūrdhni vinyasya pādayorhṛdayaṃ nyaset

kavacaṃ hṛdaye nyasya netraṃ bāhudvayornyaset

indraṃ tu pādayornyasya meḍhre tu pāvake nyaset

bāhvau varuṇadaivatyaṃ kaṇṭhe vāyuriti smṛtaḥ

evameva krame jñātvā vinyaseddeśikastathā

sṛṣṭau sthitiṃ ca saṃhāra nyāsaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca pādyaiśca svadehaṃ pūjayeddhṛdā

pūrve tu dvārapālaṃ tu nandikālau tathaiva ca

paścimadvārapālau tu bhṛṃgiṭirvijayastathā

nandinaṃ dakṣiṇe sthāpya śyāmavarṇasamanvitam

kālaṃ kuṃkumavarṇāmaṃ uttare tu pratiṣṭhitam

daṇḍiṃ pūrve tu vinyasya indragopasya saṃnibham

muṇḍī tu paścime nyasya śuddhasphaṭikasaṃnibham

bhṛṃgiṭiṃ dakṣiṇe sthāpya mayūragrīvasaṃnibham

vijayaṃ cottare sthāpya raktavarṇa samāyutam

anantaṃ paścime sthāpya kanakābhaṃ tathaiva ca

paśupatiṃ pūrvataḥ sthāpya nīlarūpasamanvitam

evameva kramaiḥ sthāpya etairmantraiśca pūjayet

sarve ṛjvāgatāḥ sthityā krūradṛṣṭibhayāvahāḥ

nānābharaṇasaṃyuktā ghorabhayāvahāḥ

evameva krameṇaiva pūjayeddeśikassadā

iti vīratantre mantranyāsaḥ saptatriṃśatpaṭalaḥ

śivārcanaṃ kathaṃ deva tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

nārudraḥ saṃsmaredrudraṃ nārudro rudramarcayet

sarvapāpaharaṃ caiva sarvatīrthaphalaṃ bhavet

dīkṣitaścāpi yo vipro bhṛtyarthaṃ na prapūjayet

sandhisvadharmānuṣṭhānaṃ na doṣāya prakalpayet

tatkāle vāpyakāle vā evameva krameṇa tu

tṛtīyaṃ dravyaśuddhistu caturthaṃ liṃgaśodhanam

pūrvoktena vidhānena ātmaśuddhiṃ ca kārayet

huṃkāreṇa samāyuktaṃ phaṭkāreṇa samāyutam

gomayenopa lipyātha prokṣayed hṛdayena tu

arghyapātraṃ puṣpapātraṃ dhūpadīpasya pātrakam

hṛdena kṣālayitvā tu astreṇa śodhayettataḥ

paṃcabrahmeṇa mantreṇa udakaṃ secayettataḥ

dravyāṇi prokṣayettatra hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

hṛdayenaiva mantreṇa ghaṇṭāṃ caiva tu tāḍayet

pūjāparyaṣitānāṃ tu hṛdayena visarjayet

apanīya ca nirmālyaṃ caṇḍeśāya pradāpayet

liṃgaṃ prakṣālayettatra hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

evamevakrameṇaiva liṃgaśuddhimathācaret

paṃcabrahmaśivāṃgaiśca prāsādena samanvitam

gaṇeśānlokapālāṃśca lokapālāstrameva ca

mantraśuddhirbhavedevaṃ kārayeddeśikottamaḥ

āsanaṃ kalpayedvidvān anantāntānyathākramam

siṃhāsanaṃ tṛtīyaṃ syād yogāsanaṃ caturthakam

śaktyakṣarasamāyuktaṃ navamāṃkurasaṃyutam

madhye nālasamāyuktaṃ paṃcāśatkarṇikāyutam

anantaṃ pūrvato bhāge vāsukiṃ cāgnigocare

śaṃkhapālaṃ ca vāruṇyāṃ gulike vāyugocare

anantāsanaṃ tu pradadyāt kūrmasyopari saṃśritāḥ

ādhāraṃ cāpyate vipra devānāṃ ca śṛṇuṣvatha

ekarudrastrimūrtiśca śrīkaṇṭhaśca śikhaṇḍikaḥ

śeṣaścaiva tu nāgendrā gulikāśca caturdiśi

caturaśraṃ tu yogaṃ syāt śeṣasyopari vinyaset

devadattadhanaṃjayau madhyame ca vikīryate

praṇavaṃ madhyadeśe tu ākāraṃ pūrvadeśake

adhaścāthordhvacchadanaṃ yogāsanamiti smṛtam

āgneyyāṃ dharma vinyasya nair-ṛtyāṃ jñānameva ca

catuṣkoṇe mahāsiṃhaṃ āsanaṃ pādanirmitam

adharmaṃ pūrvato bhāge ajñānaṃ dakṣiṇe tathā

kṛṣṇavarṇa samāyuktaṃ ilikāṃ tatra vinyaset

mahādikṣu nyasedenaṃ madhyapādastu keśavaḥ

evaṃ siṃhāsanaṃ proktaṃ padmāsanamathocyate

tejorūpaṃ tu dakṣiṇyāṃ vāyurūpaṃ tu nair-ṛte

satvaṃ vāyavyake nyasya rajovinyasya somake

tatvavarṇasamāyuktaṃ ādhāraṃ cocyate mayā

aghoraṃ dalamevāhu statpuruṣaṃ guhyadeśake

devatāḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi kālīṃ pūrve tu vinyaset

balavikaraṇīṃ nair-ṛtyāṃ vāmāṃ paścimato nyaset

sarvabhūtadamanīmīśānye manonmanīṃ tu madhyame

caturvedasvarūpaṃ tu vinyased vyāghracarmavat

ambikāṃ pūrvataḥ sthāpya gaṇāmbikāṃ ca dakṣiṇe

vimalāsanamidaṃ proktaṃ idamanyacchṛṇuṣvatha

vimalamarcaneṣūktaṃ bhojyāsanaṃ tu yogataḥ

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca dīpaiścaiva tu pūjayet

satvaṃ rajastamaścaiva uparyupari vinyaset

candrabimbaṃ nyasetpaścā ātmānaṃ cāntarātmakam

dakṣiṇe hyātmatatvaṃ tu vidyātatvaṃ tu cottare

evaṃ nyastvā tu vidhivacchivaṃ saṃpūjayettataḥ

tryakṣaṃ daśabhujaṃ caiva candrārdhakṛtaśekharam

paṃcavaktraṃ ca deveśaṃ paṃcamūrdhasamanvitam

makuṭaṃ kuṇḍalaṃ caiva keyūrakaṭakaṃ tataḥ

divyāmbaradharaṃ devaṃ divyagandhānulepitam

vāmāṃkuśaṃ caivāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ nāgaṃ ca vahni ca

evaṃ sadāśivaṃ dhyātvā sarvalokasukhāvaham

sthāpayedvāmadevena aghoreṇa nirodhayet

pūrve dvāragehe tu dakṣiṇe vā viśeṣataḥ

niṣkale sakalaṃ dhyātvā āsanaṃ caiva kalpayet

dvārabhedeṣubhedānāṃ pūjābhedaṃ na vidyate

aghoraṃ hṛdaye nyasya guhye vāmaṃ prakalpayet

hṛdaye hṛdayaṃ nyasya śiraśśirasi vinyaset

astraṃ hastapradeśe tu netraṃ netreṣu vinyaset

pādyaṃ pādau pradātavyamarghyaṃ śirasi vinyaset

uśīraṃ candanopetaṃ pādyadravyamudāhṛtam

ācamanīyamiti proktaṃ mukhe dadyādviśeṣataḥ

tilaissiddhārthakaiścaiva arghyamaṣṭāṃgamucyate

kṣurikāṃ kalpayettatra dakṣiṇekadireva ca

śivasya dakṣiṇe pārśve nyasetpāśupatāstrakam

evaṃ nyasya vidhānena paścādarcanamārabhet

ādityodayapūrve tu paṃcasnānaṃ tu kārayet

paṃcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgaiśca vyomavyāpi japettataḥ

jalabhāṇḍe vinikṣipya abhiṣekaṃ tu kārayet

dvātriṃśadvā catustriṃśatpaṃcāśatpaṃcaviṃśatiḥ

āḍhakena tu saṃpūrṇaṃ kumbhalakṣaṇamucyate

tāmraṃ vā mṛṇmayaṃ vāpi abhiṣekaṃ tu kalpayet

nālikerasya salilaṃ ikṣukāṇḍasamanvitam

nṛttageyasamāyuktaṃ stotraśabdasamāyutam

tasyānte pūjako vipra ācamanīyaṃ tu kārayet

niryāsaṃ triphalaṃ caiva uśīraṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

hṛdayena tu gandhaṃ syād vastraṃ vāmena dāpayet

nānāvidhaiśca puṣpaiśca pūjayetparameśvaram

pīṭhapūjā prakartavyā lakṣaṇena samanvitā

vistāraṃ tāvadevasyānmṛdenaiva tu lepayet

puṣpairnānāvidhairyuktaṃ nālikerasya pātrakam

sarvadvayaṃ ca pīṭhaṃ ca gopuraṃ maṇḍapaṃ tathā

darpaṇaṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ ca vṛṣabhaṃ yugmacāmaram

vajraṃ śaktiṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca khaḍgaṃpāśaṃ tathāṃkuśam

dinaṃ prati nyasedekaṃ triṃśaddināntameva ca

pīṭhapūjāṃ ca viprenda hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

māse māse prakartavyaṃ evameva punaḥ punaḥ

dhūpaṃ caiva tu viprendra dhūpapātraṃ viśeṣataḥ

ekabhāgaṃ tu karpūraṃ lohaṃ caiva catuṣṭayam

madhunā secayeddhūpaṃ pātre tu pari nikṣipet

śrīphalaṃ cājyasaṃmiśraṃ dhūpaṃ dadyādviśeṣataḥ

dakṣiṇe dhūpapātraṃ tu hṛdayena tu dāpayet

prokṣayedambhasā caiva dīpaṃ dadyāttu sadyataḥ

rakṣādīpaṃ tu kartavyaṃ tatkāle tu viśeṣataḥ

taddīpaṃ balipīṭhe tu strībhiścaiva tu dhārayet

āḍhakaṃ vā śivaṃ vāpi droṇaṃ vā taṇḍulaṃ pacet

ājyena dadhisaṃyuktaṃ pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

pātraśeṣaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ deśikāya pradāpayet

nirmālyaṃ caiva viprendra caṇḍeśāya pradāpayet

padmaṃ śūlaṃ ca paraśuṃ khaḍgaṃ pāśaṃ tathāṃkuśam

surabhimudrāṃ liṃgamudrāṃ darśayettu vidhānataḥ

puruṣaṃ pūrvataḥ pūjya aghoraṃ dakṣiṇe yajet

īśānaṃ karṇikāyāṃ tu pūjayeddeśikassadā

nair-ṛtyāṃ tu śikhāṃ pūjya vāyavyāṃ kavacaṃ yajet

madhyame śivatatvaṃ tu ātmatatvaṃ tu dakṣiṇe

madhyame tu yajeddevaṃ śivaṃ paramakāraṇam

maheśvaraṃ yajenmadhye kiṃcidāgneyamācaret

paraṃ śivamayaṃ jñeyaṃ śivātparataraṃ na ca

anantaṃ pūrvataḥ pūjya sūkṣmaṃ vai dakṣiṇena tu

ekarudraṃ tu īśānyāṃ trimūrtistvagnigocare

vidyeśāvaraṇaṃ hyetad gaṇeśāvaraṇaṃ śṛṇu

caṇḍeśamīśadigbhāge nandīśaṃ pūrvato yajet

vighneśaṃ nir-ṛte caiva bhṛṃgīśaṃ vāruṇe yajet

gaṇeśāvaraṇaṃ hyetat lokapālānśṛṇuṣvatha

yāmyāyāṃ tu yamaṃ pūjya nair-ṛtyāṃ nir-ṛtiṃ yajet

saumyāyāṃ somaṃ saṃpūjya aiśānyāmīśameva ca

lokeśāvaraṇaṃ hyetat lokapālāstramucyate

yāmye daṇḍaṃ yajettatra khaḍgaṃ nir-ṛtigocare

gadāyudhaṃ cottare pūjya śūlaṃ cīśānagocare

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca dīpaiścaiva tu pūjayet

āsanasthāṃśca devāṃśca śivasyābhimukhaḥ sthitaḥ

vidyeśānāṃ gaṇeśānāṃ liṃgamudrāṃ ca darśayet

tatkāle tāḍayed ghaṇṭāṃ paṭaṃ caiva visarjayet

prokṣayedambhasā caiva dīpaṃ dadyācchikhāsaha

paṃcavarṇahavirdadyāt paṃcavaktreṣu deśikaḥ

uśīraṃ caiva kuṣṭhaṃ ca elaṃ candana saṃyutam

mukhavāsaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ karpūraṃ triphalairyutam

apūpaṃ pṛthukaṃ caiva phaṭkulī ca samanvitam

nivedayettu śarvāya kṣetrapālāṃśca pūjayet

samudraghoṣo vāyughoṣo analākṣaḥ pramardanaḥ

nīlajīmūta saṃkāśa ūrdhvakeśasamanvitam

evameva krameṇaiva pūjayeddeśikottamaḥ

śiro hastau dvijānvośca cibukaṃ bāhuyuk tathā

śiro hastau dvijānū ca paṃcāṃgamihocyate

paṃcāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ ekāvaraṇamucyate

vimalāsanaṃ tṛtīyaṃ syād āsanaṃ trīṇi kalpayet

ekāvaraṇamevoktaṃ pūjayeddeśikassadā

agnikāryaṃ tataḥ kuryāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

gandhapuṣpādinā caiva agnidevaṃ samarcayet

prāsādena tu mantreṇa śive daśāhutiṃ hunet

paṃcabrahmaśivāṃgānāṃ ekaikaṃ homayettadā

ekāhutiṃ ca hotavyaṃ tattatsvanāmamantrataḥ

pustakārādhanaṃ tatra gandhapuṣpādinā saha

uktānuktaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ hṛdayena tu dāpayet

praviśya vigṛhaṃ paścād gṛhadevānsamarcayet

tasmātsarvaprayatnena pūjayecchivamādarāt

ātmārthaṃ pūjayed nityaṃ calaliṃgaṃ viśeṣataḥ

ābhicāraṃ kariṣyāmi ekadhā daśadhā tataḥ

evameva krameṇaiva arcayeddeśikassadā

arcanāyā vidhiḥ proktaḥ agnikāryaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre arcanāvidhiḥ aṣṭatriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

kīdṛśaṃ cāgnikāryaṃ ca tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

samāsena pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

pratiṣṭhā cotsave caiva śatrūṇāṃ ca pradarśane

dīkṣākāle tu vā kuryācchāntike pauṣṭike'pi vā

cauke vā copanayane samāvartanake'pi vā

sīmantopariniṣkrāme agniṣṭomādiyāgake

kuṇḍaṃ vā sthaṇḍilaṃ vāpi kārayeddeśikastathā

vṛttaṃ * * * * kuryān nityakarma ihocyate

trimekhalā samāyuktaṃ nābhiyonisamanvitam

khananaṃ hastamātraṃ tu darpaṇodaravattataḥ

śoṣitaṃ pācitaṃ caiva śoṣaṇaṃ pācanaṃ tathā

praṇītā spṛśyapātraṃ tu sudhāparidhayaḥ sruvāḥ

vastraṃ coṣṇīṣamevaṃ tu arghyaṃ gandhaṃ ca puṣpakam

ājyaṃ caiva caruṃ caiva homadravyaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ

sādhayeddhṛdayenaiva prokṣayedambhasā tataḥ

tisṝḥ pūrvamukhā rekhā udagrekhāstathaiva ca

arcayedgandhapuṣpāṃśca hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

śrotriyasyāgnihotrācca janayejjātavetasam

vistāraṃ tu ṣaḍaṃgulyaṃ viṣkambhaṃ tu triyaṃgulam

mathitvā paṃcabrahmeṇa pātre vinyasya deśikaḥ

hṛdā cāgniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya agnyādhānaṃ tu kārayet

pātrāṇāṃ sādanaṃ caiva prokṣaṇaṃ prokṣaṇī tathā

ūrdhva praveśanaṃ cāpi tattanmantreṇa deśikaḥ

agnau kāṣṭhaṃ vinikṣipya śivamantreṇa deśikaḥ

kuśenaiva paristīryaḥ pūrve tu cottarāgrajāt

uttare pūrvamūrdhvaṃ tu vinyaseddeśikastathā

ūrdhvaṃ saṃsthādvayaṃ caiva āgneyyāmīśadeśataḥ

ṛtusnānaṃ ca saṃkalpya yonimārgeṇa jāyate

tatpuruṣeṇa mantreṇa puṃsavanaṃ bhavanti vai

vāmadevena mantreṇa jātakarma ca hūyate

catuḥśṛṃgasamāyuktaṃ ṣaṇṇetreṇa samanvitam

jaṭāmauṃji samāyuktaṃ kṛṣṇājinasamāyutam

atiraktā bahurūpā saptajihvāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

uttarā raktamākhyātaṃ yāmye kṛṣṇā iti smṛtā

bahurūpā caiva jihvā syāt saptajihvāḥ prakīrtitāḥ

jātakarma mayā proktaṃ nāmaṃ tu hṛdayena tu

caulamantreṇa kartavyaṃ upanayanaṃ hṛdayena tu

hṛdayena tu mantreṇa samāvartanavivāhakam

dakṣiṇe brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ darbhāsanopari nyaset

viṣṇustatrādhidaivatyaṃ gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

kuśenaiva tu viprendra udakopavanaṃ kuru

utplāvanaṃ tataḥ kuryāt praṇave naiva nikṣipet

agneruttarabhāge tu aṃgārakopari nyaset

ājyādhiśrayaṇaṃ kāryaṃ darbhānande vinikṣipet

sruvaṃ caiva tu viprendra kṣālayeddhṛdayena tu

madhyaṃ madhyena saṃspṛśya mūlaṃ mūlena saṃspṛśet

somāya svāheti mantreṇa dakṣiṇārdhapūrvārdhake

raktapaṃkajasaṃkāśaṃ kiṃśukasya samaprabham

āgneyyāmagnidagdhaṃ ca mṛtyuṃ caiva tu yāmyake

īśānyāṃ jñānalābhaṃ ca śikhāniṣkrāntalakṣaṇam

ulūkadhvanite vahnau kārāgṛhamavāpnuyāt

vṛṣabhadhvanite vahnau agamyāgamanaṃ bhavet

bhāṇḍavad dhvanite vahnau rogo bhavati niścayam

kapiladhvanite vahnau surāpānamavāpnuyāt

laśunagandhaṃ bhavedagnau kṣayarogamavāpnuyāt

atigandhaṃ bhavedagnau mahājvaramavāpnuyāt

mūtragandhaṃ bhavedagnau pāṇḍurogamavāpnuyāt

romagandhaṃ bhavedvipra kukṣirogamavāpnuyāt

śubhāśubhaṃ mayā proktaṃ agnidevasya padmaja

pariṣecanaṃ tu kuryāttu taddoṣaśamanāya ca

samidājyacaruṃ lājān sarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

hṛdayenājyahomaṃ tu caruṃ ca śikhayā hunet

aghoreṇa yavaṃ hutvā kavacena tilānapi

sarvadravyasamāyuktaṃ havyavāhaṃ samuccaran

īśānyāṃ caiva hotavyaṃ kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile'pi vā

indradhanuśca dṛṣṭvā ca taddoṣaśamanāya ca

ācāryapūjanaṃ kṛtvā vastrāṃgulīyakādibhiḥ

agnikāryaṃ mayā proktaṃ nityotsava mataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre agnikāryavidhiḥ ekonacatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

nityotsavaṃ kathaṃ deva tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

sarvapāpaharaṃ puṇyaṃ sarvalokasukhāvaham

nityaṃ nityārcanānte tu nityotsavamathācaret

ucchiṣṭāścaiva vetālā kūṣmāṇḍārhatathāgatam

nityotsavasya śabdāṃśca śrutvāgarjanti tatra vai

dvitīyaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ caiva viṣṇuprītikarāya ca

pūrvāhne ca tava prīti puṣpaliṃgasamanvitam

sāye taṇḍulaliṃgaṃ syād nārāyaṇapriyaṃ tathā

calaliṃgeṣu sarveṣu śrībaliṃ ca na kārayet

evameva krameṇaiva nityotsavaṃ tu kārayet

balipākaṃ tu kartavyaṃ lakṣaṇena samanvitam

ekatālena hīnaṃ tu madhyamaṃ pātramucyate

tripātraṃ ca tridhāharmyaṃ madhye padmaṃ tu kārayet

padmasyotsedhamānaṃ tu ekāṃgulamihocyate

evameva krameṇaiva balipātraṃ tu kārayet

dviprasthamadhamaṃ proktaṃ āḍhakaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet

atyuṣṇamatiśītaṃ ca āmlaṃ lavaṇavarjitam

karṇakambusamāyuktaṃ māraṇaṃ tu vinirdiśet

adhamaṃ liṃgamityuktaṃ madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ śṛṇu

agramaṣṭāṃgulaṃnāhaṃ uttamaṃ śṛṇu tatvataḥ

agraṃ daśāṃgulaṃ nāhaṃ annaliṃgamihocyate

dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ * * * vāmahastaṃ kaṭispṛśam

evaṃ svarūpakaṃ dhyātvā baliliṃge tu vinyaset

hastamātraṃ bhaveddaṇḍaṃ agre cakradvayaṃ bhavet

prokṣayedambhasā caiva hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

aghoraṃ hṛdaye nyasya guhye vāmaṃ prakalpayet

hṛdayaṃ hṛdaye nyasya śiraśśirasi vinyaset

astraṃ hastapradeśe tu netraṃ netre tu vinyaset

sthalikāṃ caiva coddhṛtya hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

japetpāśupatāstraṃ ca śanairgacchetpradakṣiṇam

purastātpratimaṃ gacchelliṃgaṃ tu tadanu vrajet

śivasyābhimukhaṃ caiva gomayenānulepayet

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya vādyakaṃ pūjayettataḥ

suṣiraṃ gānavaṃ caiva śabdabhedaṃ tathaiva ca

evaṃ vidhisamāyuktaṃ vādyakānvādayettataḥ

dvitīyaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ kuryād aṣṭatāla samanvitam

dakṣiṇe kaduvādyaṃ syān nair-ṛtyāṃ saṃkarī tathā

naṭaprahāraṃ saumyāyāṃ aiśānyāṃ mallatālakam

bāhyapīṭhe kramāvṛtya prāsādaṃ tripradakṣiṇam

paścime vā trisaṃdhyāyāṃ pradakṣiṇamathācaret

sāyāhne paścime caiva balimevaṃ visarjayet

sarvadvāre tu kartavyaṃ pūrve tu balipīṭhakam

yamasya bhṛṃgiṇī tālaṃ nair-ṛtyāṃ mallatālakam

saumye ca koṭikāḥ proktā īśānyaṇḍaṃkarī tathā

gāyaṃ gāndhārapūrvaṃ syād dakṣiṇe kauśikaṃ tathā

āgneyyāṃ caiva kollī ca nair-ṛtyāṃ nāṭṭabhāṣayā

dvāradeśamanuprāpya vṛṣabhasyādhidaivatam

nirmālyaṃ ca visṛjyātha jale caivābhiṣecayet

arcayetpādukau vidvān * * * * * * dvayoḥ

pṛthivīṃ paścime nyasya medinīṃ cottare nyaset

balavikariṇī balapramathinī sarvabhūtamanonmanī

manonmanī nyasenmadhye arcayedgandhapuṣpakaiḥ

gaṇikāṃ snāpayettatra gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

aṣṭottaraśataṃ nṛttaṃ bhāvayedgaṇikā tataḥ

agnimukhīkaraṇaṃ yattu tadā vāhanamucyate

iti vīratantre nityotsava vidhiścatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

parivāraṃ kathaṃ deva tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

parivāraṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāvidhi purassaram

purato vṛṣabhasthānaṃ sthāpayedvṛṣabhaṃ tataḥ

īśānyāṃ śaiva viprendra bhāskaraṃ vinyasettataḥ

idamanyaprakāreṇa vakṣyehaṃ caturānana !

dakṣiṇe mātṛdevāṃśca vighneśaṃ nair-ṛte tathā

jyeṣṭhāṃ vāyavyadeśe tu viṣṇuṃ cottaradeśake

uttarāyāṃ diśi caiva durgāṃ devīṃ ca vinyaset

pūrve tu bhārgavasthānaṃ kumāraṃ * * * * *

āgneyyāṃ gaṇapatiṃsthāpya vinyasettu daivatān

dakṣiṇadvāragehasya dakṣiṇe vṛṣabhālayam

jyeṣṭhāṃ ca vāyudeśe tu (uttare) viṣṇurucyate

durgāmāgneyadigbhāge kṣetrapālaṃ tu vinyaset

maryādā vīradigbhāge yāmye mātṛgaṇāḥ smṛtā

vāyavyāṃ vinyasejjyeṣṭhāṃ viśeṣeṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ

ṣoḍaśadvāradevāṃśca vakṣyāmi munisattama

yamanair-ṛtayormadhye vasavo'ṣṭau ca vinyaset

varuṇa vāyavyayormadhye ṛṣīṇāṃ sthānamucyate

soma īśānayormadhye aṣṭanāgaṃ prakalpayet

evameva prakāreṇa maryādau ca vinyaset

rakṣādīpaṃ kathametat tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

rakṣādīpaṃ viśeṣeṇa trividhaṃ cocyate mayā

sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ vāpi tāmrajaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ tu vā

pūrvaṃ ca dakṣiṇaṃ caiva paścimaṃ cottaraṃ tathā

aṣṭāṃgulasamutsedhaṃ * * * * * * bhavet

pādaṃ caiva daśāṃgulyaṃ utsedhaṃ tu vidhīyate

vistāraṃ tu caturviṃśad aṃgulyā sthalitā bhavet

etatpauṣṭikamityuktaṃ kāmadaṃ ca tataḥ śṛṇu

saptapātrā * * * * ke teṣāmūrdhve tantathā

ninnityāne kuryāt tasyānnaṃdīpamucyate

nityāgni kuṇḍe viprendra ! * * * * * dīpayet

sthalikāṃ vinyasettatra śikhāmantreṇa saṃyutam

vartiṃ tatraiva * * * * * * dīpayettataḥ

gaṇikā (vātha) dāsī vā dhārayeddīpamuttamam

* * * * samāyuktaṃ śaṃkhadhvani samāyutam

gaccha pradakṣiṇaṃ tatra ninayecchivasaṃnidhau

īśānena tu mantreṇa arcayeddīpameva ca

ekaṃ vāpi trayaṃ vāpi pradakṣiṇamathācaret

vinyasetsthalikāṃ tatra hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

hṛdayena tu mantreṇa dīpaṃ dadyācchivasya tu

* * japettato mantrī bhasmaṃ śirasi nikṣipet

evaṃ pauṣṭikamevā tu kāmadaṃ śṛṇu tatvataḥ

saptapātre tu viprendra dīpaṃ saṃyojayettataḥ

śive dīpaṃ susaṃyojya visarjayettu vā mataḥ

uttamaṃ dīpamākhyātaṃ saptāṃgulasamucchrayam

uttamaṃ goghṛtaṃ caiva madhyamaṃ ja saṃbhavam

dūrvākṣataiśca saṃyuktaṃ darpaṇaṃ bhasmameva ca

dīpasya darśanādūrdhvaṃ śive dadyādviśeṣataḥ

yāntenaiva samāyuktaṃ īkārasvarasaṃyutam

etanmantraṃ paraṃ guhyaṃ tava snehātprakāśitam

karaṇḍamakuṭopetaṃ saumyarūpaṃ sulocanam

evaṃ vidhasvarūpaṃ tu dhyātvā vai pūjayettataḥ

balavikaraṇī balapramathinī sarvabhūtadamanyatha

madhye manonmanīṃ nyasya pūjayeddevi bāhyataḥ

rāhuketuṃ ca viprendra tadbāhye pūjayettataḥ

someśānasamāyuktaṃ tādbāhye pūjayettataḥ

bhasma dadyācchive tatra dadyāccheṣaṃ janāya ca

pariṣecanakāle tu rakṣādīpaṃ karoti yaḥ

rakṣādīpaṃ mayā proktamaṃkurārpaṇakaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre rakṣādīpavidhidvicatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

aṃkurārpaṇakaṃ kiṃ vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

utsavāṃkuramevoktaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ

athavātryaṃkuraṃ vidyād dhvajāṃkuraṃ tu cocyate

sthāpane prokṣaṇe yāge utsave snapane'pi ca

śrīkaraṃ śrīnivāsaṃ ca śrīpradaṃ ca tridhā bhavet

kṛtvā navapadaṃ tatra daivatāṃśca śṛṇuṣvatha

dakṣiṇe tu yajurvedaṃ ekāraṃ caiva nir-ṛte

vāyavye pavanaścaiva atharvaṃ somake pade

madhyame tu pade caiva somakumbhaṃ pratiṣṭhitam

etacchrīkaramityuktaṃ śrīnivāsamataḥ śṛṇu

ṣoḍaśānpadamevāhuḥ devapādāṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

sugrīvo varuṇaḥ śoṣa mukhya somāditistathā

īśānogniśca sugrīvo mukhyasya tu padāni ca

jayanto vitathaścaiva varuṇassoma eva ca

ādityaśca yamaścaiva śoṣaścaiva diti sthitaḥ

etadvai śrīnivāsaṃ tu śrīpradaṃ ca śṛṇuṣvatha

paṃcaviṃśatpadaṃ kṛtvā pūjayetkramaśastathā

svabhṛṃgaśca sugrīva varuṇāḥ śoṣamārutau

āpavatsārya sāvitrī vivasvānindramitrakā

brahmaṇaśca pade caiva sthāpayetsomakumbhakam

pitṛdauvārikau caivaṃ marutaścaiva mukhyakaḥ

ādityaśca prathaścaiva yamaścaiva tu bhṛṃgarāṭ

śarāvānvinyasettatra ghaṭikāṃśca śṛṇuṣvatha

lakṣaṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva caturānana !

tatrāṃgulena kartavyaṃ pālikādīni caiva hi

ṣoḍaśāṃgulaṃ madhyaṃ syād adhamaṃ ca daśāṃgulam

paritoṣṭau ca viprendra ṣaḍbhāgaikamiti smṛtam

ghaṭasyotsedhamānaṃ tu pālikāsamameva ca

vaktraṃ caiva ṣaḍaṃgulyaṃ paṃcavaktra samanvitam

vaktraṃ caiva ṣaḍaṃgulyaṃ paṃcavaktra samanvitam

tadardhaṃ pāda vistāraṃ tripādaṃ vā samucyate

ghaṭikā brahmadaivatyaṃ pālikā viṣṇurucyate

caturviṃśati saṃkhyā tu uttamaṃ tu vinirdiśet

pālikāṣṭakasaṃyuktaṃ adhamaṃ tadihocyate

navame saptame vāpi triśūlena samanvitam

tadante pālikādīni dhārayecchivabhaktakān

suśuddhabhūtalaṃ prāpya prokṣayecchuddhavāriṇā

kundālīṃ caiva saṃpūjya hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

mṛdaṃ saṃgrāhya vāmena vāmena mṛdamarcayet

prāsādasyāgrato vāpi īśāne dakṣiṇe'tha vā

aṣṭadroṇena śālisyātsthaṇḍilaṃ kārayettataḥ

madhyame somakumbhaṃ tu vinyasetsomabījakaiḥ

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya naivedyaṃ dāpayettataḥ

darbheṇa suṣiraṃ nyasya mṛdaṃ tatraiva pūrayet

puṇyāhaṃ tatra kurvīta tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettataḥ

niṣpāvaṃ śimbasaṃyuktaṃ apāmārgaṃ ca sarṣapam

payasā kṣālayettatra hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

vāpayecchivamantreṇa śivabhaktāśca vāpayet

somādhidevatānkumbhātpariṣicya tato jalam

suguptadeśe saṃsthāpya somakuṃbhena saṃyutam

havirnivedayettatra tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettataḥ

homayeccaiva viprendra apūpāni tathaiva ca

aṃkurārpaṇahīne tu rājarāṣṭrasya doṣakṛt

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

aṃkurārpaṇamevoktaṃ utsavasya vidhiṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre aṃkurārpaṇavidhistrayaścatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

utsavasya vidhiḥ ko vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

udgataṃ tu savoṃ yasmāt tasmādutsavamucyate

jvaramāryā prakartantu śūtrūṇāṃ pīḍane'pi vā

sarveṣāmiṣṭakāmyārthaṃ sarvavyādhivināśanam

dvividhamutsavaṃ caiva tayorbhedaṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

puṣyamāse tu puṣyāntaṃ māghamāse maghāntakam

vaiśākhe tu viśākhāntaṃ jyeṣṭhāntaṃ jyeṣṭhamāsake

pūrvāṣāḍhe tu mevoktaṃ pūrvabhadreti māsakam

kārtike kṛttikāntā tu kārayettu śivotsavam

rājñāṃ janmadinānte vā grāmanakṣatrameva vā

śaivaṃ gauṇaṃ vibhūtiṃ ca bhauvanaṃ daivikaṃ tathā

eteṣāṃ bhedakaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

triyahena samāyuktaṃ gauṇamityabhidhīyate

eteṣāṃ tu kramāṇāṃ tu vakṣyate vidhinā'dhunā

prātaḥkāle tu tīrthaṃ syādekāhe tu balidvayam

sāyaṃkāle samārabhya tadrātrau cādhivāsayet

balimekaṃ mayā proktaṃ ekāhe cotsave tathā

evameva krameṇaiva vividhaṃ cocyate mayā

prātaḥkāle tṛtīye syādbalirvipra ṣaḍeva tu

tṛtīye tu dine caiva balireko vidhīyate

cūrṇotsavānte tīrthaṃ syāddvividhaṃ cocyate mayā

sāyaṃkāle samārabhya tatpūrvaṃ homamācaret

ṣaṣṭhāhe tu ca pūrvāhne cūrṇotsavaṃ tu kārayet

sāyaṃkāle samārabhya tadrātrau cādhivāsayet

dvitīye tu dine caiva balireko vidhīyate

cūrṇātsavānte tīrthaṃ syād dvividhaṃ cocyate mayā

sāyaṃkāle samārabhya tatpūrvaṃ dinamārabhet

ṣaṣṭhāhe tu ca pūrvāhne cūrṇotsavaṃ tu kārayet

sāyaṃkāle samārabhya paṃcāhaṃ syāddine tathā

dvitīye ca tṛtīye ca caturthe ca balidvayam

kuryādvai tīrthakarmaṃ tu dvividhaṃ cocyate mayā

aṣṭāhe caiva sāyāhne adhivāsanamārabhet

caturdaśabaliṃ dadyāccūrṇotsavaṃ ca kārayet

saptāhe sāyaṃmārabhya adhivāsya tato budhaḥ

dvitīye divase caiva (tṛtīye ca caturthake)

saptāhe balirevaṃ syād balyante cūrṇamucyate

saptāhe dvividhaṃ proktaṃ navāhaṃ ca śṛṇuṣvatha

homaṃ kuryāttu tadrātrau baliṃ caiva tu dāpayet

cūrṇotsavaṃ tataḥ kuryāt tīrthaṃ kuryāttato budhaḥ

navāhe caiva sāyāhne adhivāsanamārabhet

dvitīye divase caiva tṛtīye tu caturthake

navāhe'pi ca viprendra balinā dvitayaṃ bhavet

navāhaṃ dvividhaṃ caiva procyate kamalāsana !

sūryavāraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra havirnivedayettataḥ

nityotsavasya kāle tu kuryādgrāma pradakṣiṇam

praviśedālayaṃ tatra pādukārcanamācaret

liṃge vā pratimāyāṃ vā tailābhyaṃgamihocyate

havirnivedayetpaścāllokapālabaliṃ kṣipet

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt sarvātodya samanvitam

sāyaṃkāle samārabhya prātaḥkāle baliṃ dadet

maṇṭapāddakṣiṇe pārśve sthāpayetkalaśānnava

samidājyacaruṃ caiva hutvā vai paṃcaviṃśatiḥ

baliṃ dadyāttu viprendra sāyaṃkāle viśeṣataḥ

tīrthaṃ kuryāttato bidvānpraviśedālayaṃ prati

mārtāṇḍīyamidaṃ proktaṃ * * rambhaṃ śṛṇu dvija !

bherītāḍanapūrvaṃ ca trividhaṃ cocyate mayā

bherīṃ tu tāḍayitvā tu aṃkurārpaṇa pūrvakam

evameva krameṇaiva trividhaṃ cocyate mayā

dviguṇīkṛtya vā kuryād dhvajamāropayettataḥ

bhauvane daivike kuryād anyeṣu ca vivarjayet

yatpakṣe yaddine vāre astaṃ yāti divākaraḥ

anyeṣāṃ tithi na spṛṣṭaṃ tasmātpūrvaṃ vidhīyate

itareṣāṃ ca viprendra tannadīṣu na kārayet

yatpakṣe vāre astaṃ yāti divākaraḥ

śivasya ca vrataṃ kuryāt snapanaṃ tadanantaram

utsavārambhamevoktaṃ vṛṣayāgamataḥ śṛṇu

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryān maṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam

darbhamālābhirāveṣṭya puṣpamālā valambitam

lokapālādhidaivatyaṃ svasvarūpaṃ tu lāṃcchayet

vitānenordhvamācchādya muktādāmairalaṃkṛtam

mudgānnaṃ caiva dātavyaṃ upadaṃśaissamanvitam

aṣṭadroṇena śāliṃ tu tadardhaṃ taṇḍulairapi

tasyopari nyasettatra kalaśānnavasaṃkhyayā

pūrayedgandhatoyena suvarṇaṃ tatra nikṣipet

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ vinyasedaṣṭakumbhakān

ācamanaṃ dakṣiṇenyasya śuddhodakaṃ tu nair-ṛte

uttare paṃcagavyaṃ tu īśāne ratnodakaṃ nyaset

vṛṣabhaṃ madhyame nyasya ukṣāṇaṃ pūrvato nyaset

nair-ṛtyāṃ nandinaṃ caiva paścime tu paśoḥ patim

mahodakaṃ tathaiśānyāṃ vinyasetkalaśeṣu ca

aṣṭapadmaṃ samālikhya darbhaiścaiva paristaret

uttamaṃ daśahastaṃ tu madhyamaṃ navahastakam

ardhādhikatritālaṃ tu dhvajadairghyamihocyate

ardhādhika tritālaṃ tu kanyasaṃ tviha cocyate

brāhmaṇasya bhavecchvetaṃ kṣatriyasya ca raktakam

vistāreṇa samaṃ pucchaṃ pucchārdhaṃ śikharaṃ bhavet

vṛṣabhaṃ śvetavarṇaṃ tu raktavarṇaṃ tu śṛṃgakau

netraṃ śvetamayī caiva madhyaṃ raktamayī tathā

pārśvau dīpadvayau nyasya citramūrdhve tu kārayet

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ vṛṣamūrtiṃ nyasettataḥ

prokṣayedambhasā caiva hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ vṛṣamūrtiṃ nyasettataḥ

tadbāhye pūjayettatra lokapālāstrameva ca

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhya darbhaiścaiva paristaret

vāmādīnāṃ tu śaktīnāṃ śūlaṃ mūle pratiṣṭhitaḥ

taṭī caivāgnidaivatyaṃ sarasvatīṃ caiva daṇḍake

gandhapuṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca ṣaṇṇavatyena pūjayet

tato homaṃ prakurvīta sthaṇḍilaṃ caturaśrakam

samidājyacaruṃ lājān sarṣapaṃ caiva hūyate

pratyekaṃ śatamardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vāpi hūyate

dhvajārohaṇamevaṃ tu vakṣyāmi munisattama

varṇaṃ vai raktavarṇaṃ tu stambhanaṃ pītavarṇakam

dhūmraṃ vyādhipradaṃ caiva evameva krameṇa tu

varṣārdhaṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ tu kṣīrābhaṃ sarvaśāntikam

pūrvāhne brāhmaṇaprītira madhyāhne rājavṛddhidam

dhvajasthānamahaṃ vakṣye śrūyatāṃ kamalāsana !

pīṭhāgrevā'tha kuryāttu dhvajasthānaṃ vidhīyate

campako devadāruṃ ca stambako bilvakairapi

prāsādasya samaṃ vāpi prāsādagrīvameva vā

mūlāntaṃ tu kulāntaṃ vā daṇḍasyotsedhamucyate

dvādaśaṃ daśahastaṃ vā dhvajasyotsedhamucyate

dvāviṃśatyaṃgulaṃ vātha nāhastasya vidhīyate

ekapūrvaṃ vināśārthaṃ dvipūrveṇa bhayaṃkaram

paṃcaparvadvayaṃ loke ṣaṭparvaṃ śriyamāvahet

navaparvaṃ kulakṣīṇaṃ daśa vai sarvakāmadam

ita ūrdhvaṃ na kartavyaṃ hastamānaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

ādhāraphalakādīni yājñikairvṛkṣasaṃbhavaiḥ

suṣiradvayasaṃyuktaṃ kramukaṃ veṇunā saha

paṃcaṣaṭsaptamāṃgulyaṃ veṇudaṇḍasya nāhakam

kaniṣṭhāṃgulīpramāṇena tantunā rajjurucyate

māṇavakaḥ śucirbhūtvā prokṣayedambhasā tataḥ

* * anyadhvajaṃbandha kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam

nānādhvajasamāyuktaṃ nānāpiṃchasamāyutam

nānācchatra samāyuktaṃ brahmaghoṣa samanvitam

agrataḥ pṛṣṭhato gatvā ācāryo yajamānakaḥ

avaṭaṃ khānayettatra hastadvayapramāṇataḥ

ṣaṭtālamadhyena vinyasya suvarṇaṃ kūrmameva ca

hṛdayena tu mantreṇa kṣālayed dhvajadaṇḍakam

vṛṣagāyatrīmantreṇa prokṣayetkalaśodakaiḥ

vālukena hṛdā caiva pūrayedavaṭaṃ tataḥ

ṣaṭtālaṃ paṃcatālaṃ vā catustālamathāpi vā

tadūrdhve padmaṃ saṃkalpya aṣṭapatraṃ suśobhanam

utsedhaṃ tu tridhā bhajya ekaikaṃ mekhalocchrayam

śrīdevīṃ madhyame pūjya tṛtīye ca sarasvatīm

ekamekhalayā kuryād bhūmidevīṃ nyasettataḥ

yāgasthāne tathā devaṃ snāpayetkalaśena tu

vṛṣagāyatrimantreṇa paścātsūtre tu sūtrayet

rajju yantreṇa vā kuryād dhvajamāropayettataḥ

śūlahastaḥ pinākī ca aṣṭanāḍī daṇḍamuṇḍiyaḥ

pūjayedgandhapuṣpeṇa dhūpadīpaiḥ samanvitam

puṇyāhaṃ kārayettatra kalaśānnava vinyaset

indrādīśānaparyantaṃ vinyasetkalaśāṃstataḥ

sthāpayitvā yathānyāyaṃ vṛṣagāyatrimantrataḥ

tatkāle tu prajārthīnāṃ piṇḍān (vai) bhojayettataḥ

vṛṣagāyatrimantreṇa piṇḍaṃ dadyāttu deśikaḥ

* * * * * kartavyaṃ dhvajamūle samāśritaḥ

antyajātistu sarveṣāṃ pratilomaṃ ca kārayet

tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettasmai janānsarvānviśeṣataḥ

iti vīratantre dhvajārohaṇa vidhiścatuścatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

prātarmadhyāhnasāyāhne dhvajamāropayettataḥ

lakṣaṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

vyāghravṛkṣeṇa vā kuryādbherīṃ vā paṭahaṃ tu vā

paṃcatālaṃ tu madhyaṃ syāt ṣaḍaṃgulyavihīnakam

āyāmasya samaṃ nāhaṃ madhye paṭahamucyate

evameva krameṇaiva kuryātpaṭahamuttamam

mardalairhāhalaiścaiva haṃsadhvanisamāyutam

anyaiśca sarvavādyaiśca yathā lābhena tāḍayet

maṇḍapasya tu madhye tu gomayenānulepayet

aṣṭadroṇena śāli syāt sthaṇḍilau dvau tu kārayet

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulairyuktaṃ prokṣayedambhasā tataḥ

paṭahasya tadākāraṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ kārayettataḥ

tato'gre sthāpayecchalaṃ astramantreṇa deśikaḥ

evaṃ vai pūjayecchūktaṃ paścādbherīṃ samarcayet

āpohiṣṭheti mantreṇa prokṣayitvā kuśāmbhasā

dakṣiṇe rudramabhyarcya vāme viṣṇuriti smṛtam

vāsukī carmasūtre tu valayau ravi candramāḥ

kauṇadaṇḍena saṃyuktaṃ pūjayedastramantrataḥ

śuklavastra paridhānaṃ gandhamālyaṃ tu lepanam

arcayitvā yathā nyāyaṃ gandhapuṣpādinā tataḥ

ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra vādyakaṃ pūjayettataḥ

tāḍayecchivamantreṇa ācāryau mantravigrahaḥ

kulodgato'pi dīnāśca tāḍayettālameva ca

indrasya samatālaṃ tu agnau badhvā vaṇaṃ bhavet

tatogrāmabaliṃ dadyāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

dhūpadīpasamāyuktaṃ nānāpiṃcha samanvitam

triśūlena samāyuktaṃ annaliṃga samāyutam

vimāna pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryācchivabhakta samanvitam

brahmādyaiśānaparyantaṃ grāme caiva baliṃ dadet

sarveṣāṃ tu gaṇānāṃ tu bhūtānāṃ tu piśācakaiḥ

dhvajamūle tu cāvāhya baliṃ tatraiva nikṣipet

bherītāḍanamevoktaṃ yāgasya maṇḍapaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre bherītāḍanavidhiḥ paṃcacatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

pratiṣṭhā cotsave caiva dīkṣāyāṃ prokṣaṇe'pi ca

pūrvameva mayā proktaṃ bhūmisaṃgrahaṇaṃ dvija

indra īśānayormadhye (madhye) cānalaśakrayoḥ

kārayenmaṇḍapaṃ tatra lakṣaṇena samanvitam

saptaṣaṭpaṃcahastaṃ vā sapta * * * cocyate

ekāśīti padaṃ kṛtvā prokṣayedgandhavāriṇāṃ

veditāratnicotsedhaṃ darpaṇodaravatkṛtam

madhyame tu pade caiva homakuṇḍaṃ vidhīyate

uttare caiva viprendra kuṇḍaṃ syāccaturaśrakam

sarveṣāṃ caturaśraṃ vā sarveṣāṃ vṛttameva vā

īśāna indrayormadhye vṛttakuṇḍamiti smṛtam

trimekhalā samāyuktamekamekhalameva vā

catustoraṇa saṃyuktaṃ caturdvāreṣu deśikaḥ

trihastatālasaṃyuktamadhamaṃ tviti kathyate

palatraya samāyuktaṃ śūlaṃ tālasamaṃ bhavet

uttamaṃ saptadarbhaiśca hastamātra pralambitam

vitānoparisaṃcchannaṃ muktāsragdāma saṃyutam

pūrvāyāṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ tu āgneyyāṃ raktavarṇakam

paścime pītavarṇaṃ tu vāyavyāṃ nīlameva ca

lokapālasya rūpaṃ tu dhvajānāṃ madhyame nyaset

śrīvatsaṃ svastikaṃ śaṃkhaṃ dīpo devāṣṭamaṃgalam

gadā triśūlaṃ cakraṃ ca padmaṃ ceti daśāyudham

hastamātra pramāṇena kārayedaṣṭamaṃgalam

kṣālayettu mṛdā caiva śuddhatoyena saṃyutam

vedibāhye nyasecchāli tatsamaṃ samalaṃkṛtam

darbhopari nyaseccaiva kūrcenaiva pidhāya ca

evameva nyasedvidvān aṣṭame navameva ca

vajrādīnarcayettatra svaiḥ svairnāmnā ca mantrataḥ

tato homaṃ prakartavyaṃ ekaṃ vā paṃcame'pi vā

ācāryapūjanaṃ kṛtvā pādāthīnpūjayettataḥ

iti vīratantre maṇḍapalakṣaṇaṃ ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

utsavasyādirātro tu ārabhedadhivāsanam

vinyasetpālikādīni madhyame somakumbhakam

suguptadeśe saṃsthāpya pālikādīnyathākramam

pratimāṃ vinyasettatra astraṃ caiva tu vinyaset

kārpāsasūtraṃ vā kuryāt prokṣayeddhṛdayena tu

tāmbūlaṃ dāpayetpaścājjanānsarvānviśeṣataḥ

taṇḍulaistala saṃyuktaṃ darbhaiścaiva paristaret

sasūtrānsāpidhānāṃśca savastrānvāripūritān

ratnāni vinyaseddhemaṃ gaurībījena vinyaset

nānā vādyasamāyuktaṃ nānāpiṃcha samāyutam

vimānaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt praviśedyāgamaṇṭapam

ācāryaśca śucirbhūtvā mantradehasamanvitaḥ

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

yajñamūrtiṃ pravakṣyāmi ekavaktra samanvitam

padmamadhye sukhāsīnaṃ jaṭāmakuṭa saṃyutam

varadābhayahastaṃ ca divyāmbaradharaṃ haram

dvinetrāṃ cāruvadanāṃ dvibhujāṃ ca suyauvanām

sarvābharaṇa saṃyuktāṃ vardhanīmadhyame nyaset

dvārasyābhimukhaṃ caiva vinyasedyāgamūrtinam

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ aṣṭakuṃbhānkramānnyaset

vedikāścaiva digbhāge śūlaṃ vai sthāpayettataḥ

arcayedgandhapuṣpādyaiḥ svasvanāmnā viśeṣataḥ

gadā triśūlaṃ padmaṃ ca cakraṃ ceti daśāyudham

padmāstraṃ nair-ṛte bhāge cakraṃ caiśānagocare

śrīvatsaṃ svastikaṃ śaṃkhaṃ dīpo devāṣṭa maṃgalam

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya tattatsvanāmamantrataḥ

tato homaṃ prakurvīta lakṣaṇena tu deśikaḥ

samidājya caruṃ lājān sarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

samidhaḥ sadyamantreṇa ājyaṃ vāmena hūyate

sarṣapānīśamantreṇa yavānvā yamamantrataḥ

netreṇa mudgaṃ hutvātha śālīnastreṇa homayet

hutvā caiva viśeṣeṇa dravyaṃ prati viśeṣataḥ

sarvadravya samāyuktaṃ havyavāhaṃ samuccaran

jayādirabhyadhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛcca kramāddhunet

kuryādadhyayanaṃ tatra caturovedapāragān

sāyaṃ prātarbaliṃ dadyād homānte tu viśeṣataḥ

annaliṃgaṃ prakartavyaṃ pūrvavallakṣaṇānvitam

pāśaṃ vāmakare caiva abhayaṃ dakṣiṇe kare

evaṃ pāśupataṃ dhyātvā baliliṃgaṃ suyojayet

prathamaṃ vighnarātraṃ tu dvitīyaṃ bhūtarātrakam

paṃcamaṃ tvindrarātraṃ syāt ṣaṣṭhaṃ vai ṛṣirātrakam

navamaṃ śaivamityāhu rityete rātridevatāḥ

paṃcāhe tu prakartavyaṃ indrarātrādhivardhayet

ekāhamutsave caiva śivarātramiti smṛtam

śuddhānnaṃ dadhisaṃyuktaṃ apūpaṃ laḍḍukānvitam

padmapuṣpaṃ ca rajanī lājapāyasasaṃyutam

padmamūlaṃ kuśāgraṃ ca śālyannaṃ ghṛtamiśrakam

haridrānnaṃ sa sarpiśca nālikeraphalairyutam

indravallī haridrānnaṃ priyaṃgughṛtasaṃyutam

gulodanaṃ sasarpiśca kadalīpanasāyutam

gulodanaṃ ghṛtaṃ yuktaṃ bṛhatīphalasaṃyutam

māṣānnaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ māṃsaṃ caṃpa samanvitam

śuddhānnaṃ dadhisaṃyuktaṃ lājāpūpasamanvitam

navame'hani dātavyaṃ śivamantramanusmaran

brahmādīśānaparyantaṃ balipīṭhe baliṃ kṣipet

mātrāṃgulena kartavyaṃ vistārāyāma tatsamam

mekhaloccaṃ dvayāṃgulyaṃ veśanaṃ tāvadeva tu

hastamātraṃ tu vistāraṃ utsedhaṃ tālamātrakam

vistāraṃ tu tridhā kṛtvā ekaikaṃ dvidvihastakam

raṃgeṣvārūḍhato vāpi rathedyāmau vrajettataḥ

prathame tu patākāśca dvitīye vādyakāstathā

paṃcame vānnaliṃgaṃ tu vitānavastra saṃyutam

dhūpadīpasamāyuktaṃ ghaṇṭārava samanvitam

caṇḍarudrāya gosthāne vyāpya * * darāya ca

kṣetreṣu kṣetrapālaṃ syāt sabhāsthāne sarasvatī

evameva baliṃ kuryād balipīṭhe vinikṣipet

kautukaṃ balidānānte kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam

nānājanapadairyuktaṃ nānādhvaja samanvitam

prathame ca patākāśca dvitīye vādyakastathā

paṃcame nṛtayantrāśca tadā yaṣṭitaṭātakān

navame tu * * * * vṛṣabhaṃ daśame tataḥ

bhikṣāṭanaṃ tadante tu ardhanārīṃ caturdaśa

ṣoḍaśe harirardhaṃ tu tadante kālahā vrajet

bimbakordhve tu viprendra umāskandahitaṃ vrajet

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā balidānasya deśikaḥ

ālayaṃ praviśedvidvān pariveṣamathocyate

dakṣiṇābhimukho vāpi pūrvābhimukha eva vā

mahāmaryādau maryādau kuryānnoda maṇḍapam

snānaṃ kuryāttu viprendraṃ gandhapuṣpādinā'rcayet

uttare nṛpatiṃ vidyād dvau pārśvau yoṣito nyaset

dakṣiṇena tu śaivānāṃ uttare samayānsmitān

tatkāle caiva viprendra pūrvoktavidhinā saha

arcayedgandhapuṣpādyaiḥ dadyādvai dhūpadīpakam

pṛthak pātre vinikṣipya gaṇikā dhārayetkramāt

evameva prakāreṇa dinaṃ prati samācaret

iti vīratantre adhivāsanavidhiḥ saptacatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

rākṣasānāṃ tu divase vanayātrāṃ samārabhet

tripurāntakarūpaṃ gacchedāyudhakaṃ dharet

keciddhanurdharāścaiva kecitkhaḍgadharāstathā

evameva divā kuryād rātrau kautukabandhanam

kautukaṃ caiva śūlaṃ ca vardhayetkautukaṃ hṛdā

sthalikāṃ vinyasettatra taṇḍulānupari nyaset

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya prāsādena tu mantrataḥ

nānādīpa samāyuktaṃ nānāvādya samanvitam

pūrvoktena tu mantreṇa śūlaṃ vai bandhayettataḥ

tīrthasaṃgrahaṇaṃ kuryācchivāgre maṇṭapaṃ tataḥ

madhyame vinyasetkumbhaṃ savastraṃ sāpidhānakam

hemaṃ tatraiva nikṣipya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

tīre samatalaṃ kṛtvā sthāpayet * * * tataḥ

digbandhanaṃ tu kartavyaṃ astramantreṇa deśikaḥ

sādhitaiḥ kalaśaistatra śūlaṃ vai snāpayettataḥ

iti vīratantre yātrā kautukavidhiraṣṭacatvāriṃśaḥ paṭalaḥ

kautukaṃ balidānānte kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam

kautukasyāgrato vāpi cūrṇamabhyukṣya lūkhale

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā sthaṇḍilaṃ śālinā kuru

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ nyasya darbhaiścaiva paristaret

plakṣodumbaramaśvatthaṃ padaṃvai na tu lūkhalam

kṣālayedastramantreṇa sthāpayeddhṛdayena tu

lūkhale * * vinyasya madhyena hṛdayena tu

* * * vyajanairyuktaṃ gaṇikā cūrṇayettataḥ

takkolaṃ caiva jātī ca gaṇikā cūrṇayettataḥ

savastra hemasaṃyuktaṃ gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

mūlabimbe nyasettatra cūrṇaṃ caiva tu tailakam

bhaktānāṃ parivārāṇāṃ cūrṇaṃ dadyādviśeṣataḥ

indrādi lokapālānāṃ cūrṇaṃ dadyāttu deśikaḥ

gehaṃ praveśayettatra tatastīrthaṃ samācaret

iti vīratantre cūṇotsavavidhirekonapaṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

sthaṇḍilaṃ śālinākuryāt taṇḍulaistilasaṃyutam

gaurībījena mantreṇa vardhanīṃ tasya vāmataḥ

svasvanāmnā ca mantreṇa pūjayettu viśeṣataḥ

kautukaṃ śivasaṃyuktaṃ kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam

tīre samatalaṃ kṛtvā śālinā sthaṇḍilaṃ kuru

arcayitvā tu gandhādyaiḥ śūlaṃ kalaśasaṃyutam

kautukaṃ ca visṛjyaiva pratimāṃ śūlameva ca

sahasnānaṃ tu yomartyaḥ sarvapāpādvimucyate

pūrṇāhutiṃ ca kṛtvā tu agniṃ caiva visarjayet

snapanaṃ kārayettatra lakṣaṇena samanvitam

tadrātrau caiva viprendra baliṃ dadyādviśeṣataḥ

vāpayetsṛtayenāpi piṇyākena samāyutam

sandhau catuṣpathe caiva devālaye śmaśānake

baliṃ dadyādviśeṣeṇa rājarāṣṭrasukhāvaham

iti vīratantre tīrthavidhiḥ paṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

tīrthāhe vā tṛtīye vā paṃcāhe saptame'pi vā

tīrthāhe vā tṛtīye vā paṃcāhe saptame'pi vā

saptāhātparamaṃ caiva snapanaṃ tatra kārayet

caṇḍeśvarāgrataścaiva gomayenānulepayet

tilānvinyasya viprendra darbhaiścaiva paristaret

navakumbhānsamādāya sūtreṇa pariveṣṭayet

snāpayeccaiva caṇḍeśaṃ caṇḍamantreṇa buddhimān

caṇḍeśasyākṛtiṃ kṛtvā brahmacārī śucirbhavet

puṣpamālyairalaṃkṛtya gandhacandanakādibhiḥ

ṭaṃkahastasamāyuktaṃ bhasmoddhūlitavigraham

śibikordhve nyasettatra dhvajasthāne praveśayet

dhvajāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā sthāpayetkalaśānnava

vṛṣamantramanusmṛtya dhvajaṃ vai sthāpayettataḥ

diśāṃ baliṃ tataḥ kṛtvā dhvajaṃ caiva visarjayet

ṭaṃkena cchedayettatra dhvajavedīṃ viśeṣataḥ

lājasakthu samāyuktaṃ haridrānnaṃ dadhisaṃyutam

sandhisthaṃ pīṭhamudvāsya ṭaṃkenaiva tu chedayet

ācāryaścaivaṃ viprendra ! pādaprakṣālanaṃ kuru

ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra vastradhānyādikāṃcanaiḥ

gaṇikānāṃ ca dāsīnāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayettataḥ

kulaikaviṃśamuddhṛtya śivalokaṃ sa gacchati

gacchecchivapuraṃ divyaṃ yāvanto dhṛti saṃplavam

utsavasya vidhiḥ proktaḥ prāyaścittavidhiṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre utsavavidhirekapaṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

prāyaścittaṃ kathaṃ deva tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

vināśasya tu saṃdhānaṃ prāyaścittamiti smṛtam

akṛtānāṃ vinaṣṭānāṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate

(prāsādaṃ pūrvavatkṛtvā jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kuru)

āgneyyāṃ vahninā dagdhaṃ dakṣiṇe maraṇaṃ bhavet

vāyavyāṃ bandhuvṛddhiḥ syād uttare putravardhanam

ravātvaṃ vyapohya sarvaṃ tu sakalikenna bandhayet

prāsādaṃ naṣṭamevaṃ syāt prāsādaṃ caiva kārayet

pūrvasthānagataṃ liṃgaṃ anyasthānagataṃ bhavet

pūrvadeśe calaṃ liṃgaṃ kiṃcideva tu saṃsthitam
