liṃgaṃ vā pīṭhamevaṃ vā sphuṭite dagdhameva vā

vyapohyaiva tu talliṃgaṃ sthāpayennavaliṃgakam

talliṃgaṃ sthāpayedanyaddeśilakṣaṇa saṃyutam

jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kuryāllakṣaṇanena samanvitam

gulaṃ sākṣānmadhūcchiṣṭaṃ sajjarasamanvitam

jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt samīpe snapanaṃ bhavet

* * * * * * * * śāntihomaṃ tu kārayet

pratimasyāṃgahīnaṃ tu śilpinā kārayettataḥ

pratimāṃ caiva deveśaṃ parito viśvarairyutam

hutvā pratiṣṭhāmārgeṇa sthāpayettadvadeva ca

yojayetpratimāṃ viprasnapanaṃ tatra kārayet

bhinne ca sphuṭite caiva pramādācchedane'pi ca

jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā śāntihomaṃ tu kārayet

sadyogandhādināpūjya śivamaṣṭaśataṃ japet

pūrvavatparimanyā vā saṃprokṣaṇādi kārayet

jvarādipīḍanaṃ kuryād upaśāntiṃ karoti ca

catudikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

samidājyacaruṃ lājā saktuṃ caiva tu hūyate

aṣṭottaraśataṃ caivaṃ homayeddeśikottamaḥ

evameva krameṇaiva saptāhaṃ kārayeddvijaḥ

śaivāṃśca bhojayettatra savyaṣṭiparicārakān

dinānte caiva viprendra ! rātrau grāmabaliṃ dadet

brahmādīśānaparyantaṃ svasvanāmnā vinikṣipet

nityārcanā vihīne tu śāntihomaṃ tu kārayet

sandhyāṃ ca dvitrayo hīne paṃcagavyābhiṣecanam

ekāhe tritaye vāpi paṃcāhe vāpi hīnake

diśāhomaṃ cārdhamāse tu snapanaṃ caiva kārayet

sthitaliṃgamidaṃ proktaṃ calaliṃgamataḥ śṛṇu

triṃśāttu divasādūrdhvaṃ snapanaṃ kārayettataḥ

nityāgnau ca vihīne tu dviguṇaṃ homamucyate

parivāre tu vicchinne sthāpayetparivārakān

garbhagehe dīpahīnaṃ puṇyāhaṃ tatra kārayet

nityotsave dīpahīne tu puṇyāhaṃ tatra kārayet

śuddhanṛttavihīne tu snapanaṃ tatra kārayet

śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kuryānnavaliṃgaṃ prakalpayet

pratime patite vāpi snapanaṃ tatra kārayet

jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā paścānnityotsavaṃ kuru

śvānakākādibhiḥ spṛṣṭe liṃge vā pratime'pi vā

kāle cotsavahīne tu snapanaṃ tatra kārayet

paṃcāhe balihīne tu diśāhomaṃ tu kārayet

balidravyavihīne tu dviguṇaṃ ca prayojayet

bhinne vāṃgavihīne tu pratiśūlaṃ tathaiva ca

āvāhya śūlaṃ tatraiva baliśeṣaṃ samācaret

aṣṭamaṃgalarūpe vā āyudhe vā vihīnake

tatsvarūpaṃ punaḥ kṛtvā prokṣayetpūrvavatkramāt

snapanaṃ tatra kurvīta caṇḍayāgaṃ tu kārayet

caṇḍayāgavihīne tu snapanaṃ tatra kārayet

yāgaśālāvihīne tu śāntihomaṃ hunettataḥ

dīpaṃ tatraiva saṃyojya taddoṣaśamanāya ca

gandhapuṣpavihīne tu dhūpahīne tathaiva ca

ācāryastu viprendra ! pramādāvyādhisaṃbhave

putrapautrādinā caitacchiṣyeṇaiva tu kārayet

vṛṣabhaṃ tatra saṃsthāpya sthāpayedvidhinā saha

śilpinā kārayetpīṭhaṃ sthāpayellakṣaṇairyutam

punaḥ pūjāṃ samārabhya taddoṣaśamanāya ca

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra punaḥ karmasamārabhet

snapanaṃ tatra kurvīta punarvārotsavaṃ kuru

śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kuryādamāvāsyāṃ punaḥ kuru

snapanaṃ tatra kurvīta punarmāsotsavaṃ kuru

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra punaḥ snapana mācaret

khanitvā cāstramantreṇa valmīkaiḥ pūrayettataḥ

prākāre maṇṭape vāpi prāsādasya samīpake

tatkāle paṃcahavyaṃ tu tadevaṃ prokṣayettataḥ

bhūtavalyaṃ ca viprendra ! dīpahīnaṃ bhavettataḥ

agnikuṇḍavihīne tu śāntihomaṃ tu kārayet

homagarbhe pratiṣṭhāyāṃ prokṣaṇe cotsave'pi vā

śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā snapanaṃ tatra kārayet

traivarṇikakule jātā strīṇāmārtava saṃyutam

prākāre gopure caiva paṃcagavyena śudhyati

snapanaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ taddoṣaśamanāya ca

mantrahīne kriyāhīne dravyahīnasya kālake

upadaṃśādi vicchinne puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ

pīṭhapūjā vihīne tu puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ

śuddhanṛttavihīne tu kuryātsnapanamuttamam

śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kuryācchuddhanṛttaṃ tu kārayet

gaṇikābhṛtyavarge vā rajodṛṣṭena saṃbhave

diśāhomaṃ tataḥ kuryān mahāhavirnivedayet

puṇyāhaṃ kārayettatra śāntihomaṃ ca kārayet

iti vīratantre prāyaścittavidhiḥ dvipaṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

etatsarvaṃ samāsena vaktumarhasi me prabho !

anāvṛṣṭyādikāleṣu jvaramārī pravartane

śatrūṇāṃ pīḍane kāle kṣīrasnānaṃ vidhīyate

adhamaṃ raktajaṃ proktaṃ snāpayeddeśikottamaḥ

paṃcagāvasamutpannā lokānugrahakāmyayā

kṣīrasnānaṃ tridhā proktaḥ muttamaṃ madhyamādhamam

adhamaṃ caitaduddiṣṭaṃ madhyamaṃ ca tataḥ śṛṇu

śivasya purato vipra ! gomayenānulepayet

sūtrapātaṃ prakartavyaṃ śāstradṛṣṭena tantunā

uttarāgraṃ tathā sūtraṃ vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ

tadbāhyāvaraṇādbāhye padamekantu hrāsayet

madhyamena pade caivaṃ śālinā vikirettataḥ

paristarettu darbhaiśca prokṣayed hṛdayena tu

kalaśāngrāhayettena sūtreṇa veṣṭayettataḥ

madhyame śivakumbhaṃ tu kṣīreṇaivābhipūrayet

śivabījena mantreṇa sthāpayecchivakumbhakam

savastraṃ sa hiraṇyaṃ ca sakūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam

prathamāvaraṇe caiva kalaśānaṣṭa vinyaset

tṛtīyāvaraṇe caiva ṣaṭtriṃśatkalaśānnyaset

kṣīreṇa pūrayeddhīnaṃ savastraṃ sāpidhānakam

prathamāvaraṇe caiva vidyeśvarāṃśca vinyaset

tṛtīyāvaraṇe caiva brahmā tatrādhidevatā

tato homaṃ prakartavyaṃ navapaṃcaikameva vā

agnimukhaṃ ca saṃkalpya pūrvoktena krameṇa tu

mudgamāṣasamāyuktaṃ navadravyamihocyate

aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā lājānvāmena caiva hi

tilānvai śiromantreṇa mudgaṃ tu śikhayā yutam

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi paṃcāśatpaṃcaviṃśatiḥ

ghṛtamikṣeti mantreṇa juhuyāttadanantaram

ācāryaṃ pūjayettatra padārthīnpūjayettataḥ

gaurībījena mantreṇa vardhanīṃ snāpayettataḥ

paṃcāśadbhiḥ paṃcaviṃśadbhiḥ snāpayetsnapanoktavat

dadhisnānaṃ prakartavyaṃ ghṛtasnānaṃ tathaiva ca

iti vīratantre kṣīrābhiṣekavidhistripaṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

kuṇḍānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ kiṃ ca tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

sruksruvāṇāṃ vidhiṃ caiva śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

dahatyaśeṣabhāvena tatkuṇḍamiha saṃjñitam

snapane dīkṣākāle tu pavitrārohaṇādiṣu

hastamātra pramāṇena mātrāṃgula samanvitam

yonyādi sarvakuṇḍāni caturaśre prajāyate

yāmyāyāmardhacandraṃ ca nair-ṛtyāṃ tu trikoṇakam

saumyāyāṃ saptakoṇaṃ tu aiśānyāmaṣṭakoṇakam

karṇasūtrasya mokṣāṃśaṃ paścime vinyasettataḥ

dakṣiṇe cottare vṛttaṃ kārayeddeśikottamaḥ

koṇādi tatra sūtraṃ ca vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ

caturaśrasya karṇaṃ tu grāhayettu dvijottamaḥ

bhāgamekaṃ nyasetpūrve madhyātpūrvādi bhrāmayet

aṣṭabhāgakṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ ekabhāgaṃ tu dakṣiṇe

vinyaseccaiva viprendra ! tena mānena sūtrakam

saptabhāgakṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ vinyasetpūrvamekaśaḥ

karṇārdhamaṣṭadhā bhitvā karṇārdhena samanvitam

likeśāṃśaṃ tu kṣetraṃ syātpūrvamekena vinyaset

kṣetraṃ dvātriṃśadaṃśaṃ tu kṣetrārdhena tu yojayet

vasubhāgakṛtaṃ kṣetraṃ dakṣiṇe cārdhabhāgataḥ

tasmānmadhyaṃ gṛhītvā tu kṛtvā ṣaṭkoṇameva ca

dakṣiṇe cottare caikaṃ koṇamityabhidhīyate

aṣṭapatrasamaṃ kṛtvā padmakuṇḍamihocyate

abhyantare tu vinyasya caturdikṣu viśeṣataḥ

lāṃchanaṃ koṇakaṃ gṛhya etadabhyantarasya tu

athavānyaprakāreṇa kuṇḍaṃ kuryāttu deśikaḥ

mekhalāṃ ca prakartavyaṃ vistārotsedhatatsamam

adhastānmekhalā vipra ! dvyaṃgulaṃ tadihocyate

vistāraṃ caturaṃgulyaṃ saptāṃgulaṃ tu dairghyakam

hastamātraṃ khanedbhūmiṃ vistāraṃ talameva tu

madhye nābhiṃ prakalpyātha ekāṃgulaṃ tu viṣṭharam

vajraṃ śaktiṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca khaḍgaṃ pāśaṃ tathāṃkuśam

sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ yājñikaṃ vṛkṣameva vā

vaikaṃkatamayaṃ vāpi kuryādastramanuttamam

vistāraṃ ca ṣaḍaṃgulyaṃ ghanaṃ tasyārdhamucyate

madhye padmamṛjuṃ kṛtvā bahirvakraṃ tu pārśvayoḥ

tadadho gaṇḍikāṃ kuryādardhāṃgulena kārayet

madhyame gaṇḍikāṃ kuryādaṃguladvyamānataḥ

evameva krameṇaiva dvayoragraṃ tu śūlavat

caturaṃgulavistāraṃ dīrghaṃ pūrvavadeva tu

tasyocchrayaṃ ṣaḍaṃgulyaṃ ekāṃgulena gaṇḍikā

tadūrdhve tadadhaścāpi dvābhyāmaṃgulinā bhavet

vajravatphalakā caiva tadūrdhve gaṇḍikāṃguliḥ

ghaṭasyoccaṃ ṣaḍaṃgulyaṃ tadadho gaṇḍikāṃguliḥ

pūrvavaddīrghamevaṃ syān madhye vakraṃ tathaiva ca

mūle tu phalakāṃ kṛtvā vistāraṃ tu ṣaḍaṃgulam

taddīrghaṃ phalakāṃ kṛtvā madhye pāśaṃ tu lekhayet

dīrghaṃ tu pūrvavatkṛtvā agre sūcīmukhaṃ tataḥ

caturaṃguladairghyaṃ tu nāhamekāṃgulaṃ bhavet

gadāṃ tu daṇḍavatkṛtvā agre sūciṃ tu kārayet

pālikā tu catuṣkoṇe vṛṣabhaṃ sthāpayetpunaḥ

cakrasya śūlavanmānaṃ vṛttarūpamihocyate

evaṃ cakraṃ samākhyātaṃ padmaṃ caiva śṛṇuṣvatha

paṃcāṃgulaṃ tu viprendra ! daṇḍanāhamihocyate

ṣoḍaśāṃguladairghyaṃ syādaṣṭāṃgulaṃ tu vistaram

darpaṇaṃ pūrṇakumbhaṃ ca vṛṣabhaṃ yugmacāmaram

vinyasetphalakāmadhye aṣṭamaṃgalamucyate

varṇena lekhayitvā tu lāṃchayecchilpinā tataḥ

sruvasya lakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye caturviṃśatiraṃgulī

karṇaṃ triyaṃgulaṃ proktaṃ mānaṃ pūjyākṣayaṃ bhavet

nāsikāvadbaliṃ kṛtvā sruvalakṣaṇamucyate

ṣaṭtriṃśadaṃgulāyāmaṃ aṣṭāṃgulasuvistaram

agraṃ caiva tribhāgaikaṃ saptāṃgulaṃ tu dairghyakam

rasāṃgula pravistāraṃ karṇaṃ caiva samāhitaḥ

aṣṭāṃgulena vediḥ syād vistāra tāvadeva tu

madhyame caturaṃgalyaṃ baliṃ caiva tu kārayet

dvayoḥ paṭṭikayormadhye yavadvayaṃ tu nimnakam

bilāccaiva mukhāntaṃ syātkhātaṃ kaniṣṭhamānataḥ

gajoṣṭra sadṛśākāraṃ matsyapṛṣṭhākṛtirbhavet

tadadho gaṇḍikā bāhyāṃ tadadho gaṇḍikāṃ śṛṇu

dvayāṃgulapramāṇena ghaṭaṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ

paṃcahastapramāṇena uttamaṃ toraṇocchrayam

utsedhārdhaṃ tu vistāraṃ phalakāṃ caiva vinyaset

evaṃ toraṇamākhyātaṃ darbhamālāṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

adhamaṃ trīṇi darbhāṇi tadagre rajju rucyate

paridhīlakṣaṇaṃ vakṣye avakraṃ nirvraṇaṃ śubham

ṣaṭtriṃśadaṃgulāyāmaṃ tridhā darbhaiḥ paristaret

catvāri viṣṭharaṃ kuryādājyasthālyādikaṃ śṛṇu

vistāraṃ ca ṣaḍaṃgulyaṃ utsedhaṃ tu dvayāṃgulam

ājyaṃ sruvaprapūrṇaṃ ca hastamātrapramāṇataḥ

annamakṣa pramāṇena gulaṃ vai tatpramāṇataḥ

sthalikā caiva kartavyā hastamātra pramāṇataḥ

pīṭhoccaṃ tu tripādīṃśca kārayeddeśikottamaḥ

madhyamaṃ paṃcadarbhaistu saptadarbhaistu cottamam

kūrcānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ vastrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

uttamaṃ dvādaśa proktaṃ vistāraṃ paṃcatālakam

ṣaṭsapta caiva dīrghaṃ syādaṣṭatālaṃ tu vistaram

kuṇḍānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ proktaṃ śāntihomaṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

iti vīratantre kuṇḍādilakṣaṇavidhiścatuḥ paṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

mūrtihomaṃ kathaṃ śaṃbho ! brūhi me parameśvara !

pūrvoktamaṇḍapaṃ vāpi agniśālāmathāpi vā

caturaśraṃ vātha vṛttaṃ agnikuṇḍaṃ tu kārayet

evaṃ tatraiva saṃkalpya darpaṇodaravatkṛtam

mathitvā tu gṛhītvā tu hṛdayena pratiṣṭhitam

upasthānaṃ śirasā kṛtvā paristaraṇameva ca

dakṣiṇe paścime caiva uttare ca paristaret

pātrāṇāṃ sādhanaṃ caiva hūyate kramaśastataḥ

aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā juhuyāttu śatāhutiḥ

śāntihomamitikhyātaṃ diśāhomaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre śāntihomavidhiḥ paṃcaḥ paṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

diśāhomaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva caturānana !

agnimukhaṃ ca saṃkalpya pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

prāsādasya caturdikṣu pūrve tu caturaśrakam

padmaṃ caiva tu saumyāyāṃ kuṇḍaṃ vā sthaṇḍilaṃ tu vā

aśvatthaṃ vāruṇe caiva vaṭaṃ caivottare diśi

sahasraṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā aṣṭottaraśataṃ tu vā

ājyena caruṇā caiva juhuyāttu śatāhutiḥ

dravyānte caiva dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ juhuyāttataḥ

śivāgre maṇṭapaṃ kṛtvā sthaṇḍilaṃ śālibhiḥ kuru

sa vastraṃ sa hiraṇyaṃ ca sakūrcaṃ vāripūritam

ādityaśca tathācāgniḥ somo dharaṇireva ca

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ pūjayedgandhapuṣpakaiḥ

kalaśānbāhyato vipra ! aṣṭadikṣvaṣṭa homayet

ekaikāṣṭaśataṃ caiva īśānādīni hūyate

havirnivedayetpaścāt śivasya parameṣṭhinaḥ

prāsāde maṇṭape caiva liṃgasthāpanakarmaṇi

udyāne vāpi viprendra ! vāstuhomaṃ tu kārayet

samarīya pade kṛtvā sthaṇḍilaṃ sikatena vā

śuddhatoyena saṃpūrya homaṃ tatraiva nikṣipet

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya ullekhanaṃ tu kārayet

aghorāstreṇa cā likhya prokṣayetpuruṣeṇa tu

paristareta darbhaiśca pūrvoktena paristaret

praṇītāmuttare sthāpya brahmāṇaṃ dakṣiṇe yajet

ājyādhiśravaṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

dakṣiṇe cottare caiva uttare paridhīnnyaset

tūṣṇīmadhyaṃ tu dadyāttu aghoraṃ caiva hūyate

caruṃ hutvārthabhasmannājyaṃ vāstoṣpateti ca

sadyādīśānaparyantaṃ ghṛtenaiva tu homayet

pāyasānnaṃgulaṃ yuktaṃ ghṛte naiva pariplutam

jayādirabhyadhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛccaiva hūyate

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāttadanantare

pariṣecanaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā paridhānyāni dhāpayet

praṇītodakena saṃprokṣya pavamānamudīrayan

savatsāṃgāṃ pradadyāttu ācāryāya viśeṣataḥ

kriyāśarīramityuktaṃ dakṣiṇā jīva ucyate

tasmātsarvaprayatnena ācāryaṃ pūjayetsadā

vāstuhomamitikhyātaṃ sakalārādhanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre mūrti vāstuhomaḥ ṣaṭpaṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

sakalārādhanaṃ kiṃ vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

jalasnānaṃ purā kṛtvā bhasmasnānaṃ samācaret

uttarābhimukho bhūtvā pūrvābhimukha eva vā

tasyaiva vāmabhāge tu darbhaiścaiva paristaret

pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ hṛdayena tu vinyaset

tripādamadhyame caiva śaṃkhaṃ caiva tu vinyaset

recakaṃ pūrakaṃ caiva kumbhakaṃ ca samācaret

phaṭkāreṇa samāyuktaṃ daśadikṣu ca śodhayet

īśānamaṃguṣṭhe nyasya tarjanyāṃ caiva deśikaḥ

puruṣaṃ tarjanīṃ nyasya aghoraṃ madhyame nyaset

ṣaḍaṃgaṃ vinyasettatra pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

śikhāyāṃ tu śikhāṃ nyasya kavacaṃ stanamadhyataḥ

īśānamūrtiṃ vinyasya mukhe tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset

sadyojātaṃ nyasetpāde vinyasettu viśeṣataḥ

mukhe viṣṇuṃ nyasedvidvānkarṇayoraśvinau nyaset

ādityaṃ dakṣiṇe bāhau somaṃ vinyasya vāmake

varuṇaṃ dakṣiṇe jānau apare vāyurucyate

vinyasetsṛṣṭimūrtīnāṃ sṛṣṭi nyāsamudāhṛtam

saṃhāramūrtināṃ caiva saṃhāra nyāsamucyate

evaṃ nyāsavidhānena paścātpūjanamārabhet

tālamātrapravistāraṃ tadardhaṃ cocchrayaṃ bhavet

mātrāṃgulena kartavyaṃ mekhaloccaṃ dvayāṃgulam

pratimānāṃ ca sarvāsāṃ pīṭhe karmārcanaṃ yajet

calaṃ caivācalaṃ caiva dvividhaṃ pratimaṃ bhavet

śilābhirmṛṇmayairvāpi bhitticitre paṭe'pi vā

śailajaṃ pratimaṃ caiva abhiṣekaṃ tu kārayet

lohajaṃ pratimaṃ caiva snāpayetprokṣayettu vā

pīṭhe saṃpūjayettatra parivāra samanvitam

kevalaṃ sthāpita idamanyacchṛṇuṣvatha

parivāraṃ na kartavyaṃ śrībilvaṃ ca na kārayet

vīrāsanaṃ tadūrdhve tu kalpayeddeśikottamaḥ

niṣkalāśritaṃ yattu āsanāvaraṇāyutam

pratimānāṃ ca sarvāsāṃ tiṣṭhantadyenameva vā

āsīnastatra sakalaṃ pūjayettu viśeṣataḥ

anyarūpaṃ na kartavyaṃ kartavyaṃ cedvināśanam

vastramābharaṇaṃ caiva kṣālayecchuddhirucyate

vastraṃ datvā tvaghoreṇa gandhaṃ vai śirasā dadet

dīpaṃ sadyena dadyāttu hṛdayena havirdadet

pūrvoktena krameṇaiva garbhāvaraṇakaṃ yajet

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ sarvapāpādvimucyate

sakalārādhanaṃ proktaṃ vighneśasthāpanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre sakalārādhanavidhiḥ saptapaṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

guruvaktrāttu vijñeyaṃ anyathā naiva kathyate

īśādisadyaparyantaṃ uddharettu yathā kramam

cakraṃ trayodaśaṃ grāhya paṃcadaśa samanvitam

vāsavasya caturthaṃ ca śūlādyaṃ ca tataḥ param

vajraṃ tena samāyuktaṃ īśānasya tṛtīyakam

tadeva punaruddhṛtya agnibījena vā yutam

varuṇasya tṛtīyaṃ tu vāsavena samanvitam

vajradvitīyasaṃyuktaṃ varuṇasya tu paṃcamam

tatsarvaṃ śrotumicchāmi bhagavanvaktumarhasi

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

lokadravyādbubhukṣante eteṣāṃ vighnāya pūjayet

prāsādaṃ kārayettatra śāstradaṣṭena karmaṇā

vyomavyāpi japitvā tu karṣayeddeśikottamaḥ

pakṣe santi ca sarvāṇi gogaṇānāṃ nivedayet

darpaṇodaravatkṛtvā gomayenānulepayet

pūrvokte caiva nakṣatre yoge ca karaṇānvite

dvārasya dakṣiṇe pārśve prathameṣṭāṃ tu vinyaset

śilābhiriṣṭakābhirvā dārumṛdbhirathāpi vā

navahastaṃ paṃcahastaṃ vā trihastaṃ vāpi kārayet

prāsādasyavaśādvidvā prākāraṃ kārayettataḥ

paṃcatālottamaṃ viddhi catustālaṃ tu madhyamam

ṣoḍaśāṃśe vibhajyaṃ tu upānamekabhāgikam

ekena paṭṭikāṃ kuryād grīvā caiva triyaṃśakam

pīṭhotsedhaṃ caturbhāgaṃ padmamūrdhve prakalpayet

uttamaṃ lohajaṃ proktaṃ madhyamaṃ tu śilāmayam

sauvarṇaṃ caiva saubhāgyaṃ mokṣārthī rajataṃ bhavet

calitaṃ lohajaṃ caiva ratnajaṃ dārujaṃ tu vā

sthāpitaṃ tu parārthaṃ syādanyadātyārthamucyate

gajānanaṃ gajakarṇaṃ lambodaraṃ tathaiva ca

teṣāṃ caiva tu mūrtīnāṃ svanāmādyakṣarairyutam

tasya yantraṃ vadāmīha sarvāśubhanivāraṇam

likhitvāṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ kesarāsya sakarṇakam

vinyasedaṣṭapatreṣu aṣṭamūrtyakṣarānbudhaḥ

pūjayettu viśeṣeṇa mūlamantreṇa homayet

sarvaroga (haraṃ hyetat) siṃhadṛṣṭagajaṃ yathā

rohiṇyādityapuṣyeṣu hastapauṣṇatriruttarā

sthirarāśau tu kartavyaṃ ubharāśāvathāpi vā

āsīnaṃ vā sthitaṃ vāpi vāhanārūḍhameva vā

nānābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ nāgayajñopavītinam

paṃcatālena kuryāttu pratimāṃ kārayecchubhām

pādoccaṃ tu dvibhāgaṃ tu kuryātpratimamuttamam

vāme pāśasamāyuktaṃ dakṣiṇe cākṣasūtrakam

evaṃ lakṣaṇa saṃyuktaṃ pratimāṃ kārayedbudhaḥ

ṣaḍbhujenaiva kartavyaṃ paracakra jayecchatā

madhyamaṃ caturaṃśena trayaṃśamadhamaṃ bhavet

tāla saṃkhyākramaṃ vakṣye adhamaṃ tālamucyate

yajamānecchayā yuktaṃ śilpinā kārayettataḥ

kāmadaṃ mokṣaphaladaṃ sarvapāpavināśanam

śuddhasnānamanuprāpya śuddhiṃ kṛtvā tato budhaḥ

akārādi kṣakārāntā nyakṣarāṇi vilekhayet

dvivargasya tṛtīyaṃ tu prathamasya caturthakam

mūlamaṃtramidaṃ proktaṃ vighneśvarasya padmaja !

eteṣāṃ sthāpaneṣūktaṃ vibhavasyānurūpataḥ

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam

stambhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryuktaṃ paṃktimānamathocchrayaḥ

kramukaṃ veṇudaṇḍaṃ vā avakraṃ nirvraṇaṃ tathā

madhyame vedikāṃ kuryāt paritaḥ kuṇḍamucyate

vajraṃ pūrve tu vinyasya āgneyyāṃ tu pravālakam

vāruṇyāṃ padmarāgaṃ tu vaiḍūryaṃ vāyugocare

madhyame caiva māṇikyaṃ vinyasettu viśeṣataḥ

tato devaṃ samāyojya hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

evameva prakartavyaṃ lohajānāṃ viśeṣataḥ

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvokte maṇṭape śubhe

aṣṭadroṇaiśca śālībhiḥ tadardhaistaṇḍulairapi

tatraiva pratimāṃ sthāpya pūrvābhimukhameva vā

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vinyasettoyapūritān

gandhapuṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca hṛdinā pūjayettataḥ

akṣihomaṃ tataḥ kuryādājyenaiva tu deśikaḥ

aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā pūrṇāhutimathācaret

padmarekhāṃ samālikhya tanmadhye kṛṣṇamaṇḍalam

hemapūrvaṃ tathā caiva madhvājyaṃ vinyasettataḥ

madhuvāteti mantreṇamadhunā tarpayettataḥ

gaṇānāṃ ceti mantreṇa savatsāṃgāṃ pradarśayet

prokṣayedbrāhmaṇāstatra sākṣataṃ sakuśodakam

prokṣayetkalaśānvipra ! paṃcabrahmeṇa saṃyutam

geyanṛttasamāyuktaṃ jayaśabdaissamanvitam

paṃcamṛtpaṃcagavyaiśca paṃcāṃgaiśca samanvitam

rathevā śibikāyāṃ vā pratimāṃ ropya bhaktitaḥ

pratimāṃ paṃcagavyena snāpayeddhṛdayena tu

mūlamantreṇa viprendra ! jale caivādhivāsanam

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vidyeśvara samanvitān

ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā paṃcarātramathāpi vā

jalāduttīrya pūrvedyuḥ śuddhiṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ

navavastreṇa saṃveṣṭya mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ

śālyardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nyasya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ nyaset

anḍajaṃ muṇḍajaṃ caiva romajaṃ carmajaṃ tathā

tasyopari nyasedbījaṃ kūrcenaiva samanvitam

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vinyasenmūrtisaṃyutān

savastrānsāpidhānāṃśca sahiraṇyānsakūrcakān

vighneśaṃ tatra saṃsthāpya mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ

lokapālāṃśca kumbhāni vinyasetpūrvameva ca

vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā sarvakuṇḍāni kārayet

plakṣāśvatthaṃ ca nyagrodhaṃ bilvaṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ

pālāśaṃ caiva viprendra ! navakuṇḍeṣu homayet

sarveṣāmapyalābhe tu palāśasamidhā yutam

kadalīphalasaṃyuktaṃ homayeddeśikottamaḥ

hṛdayaṃ ca śiraścaiva śikhāmantreṇa hūyate

hutvā hutvā spṛśedbimbaṃ dravyaṃ prati viśeṣataḥ

laḍḍukaṃ nālikeraṃ ca mātuluṃgaphalāni ca

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

snānavedyāṃ samānīya snāpayenmūlamantrataḥ

śilādīnsthāpayedvidvāndeśe'pi tu pade'pi ca

yajanaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva caturānana !

prāṇāyāmaṃ carettatra paścādbrahmāṇi vinyaset

hṛdayātpaṃca vinyasya vighneśaṃ hṛdaye nyaset

pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam

tato lohajaberaṃ cet snātvā parvaṇi parvaṇi

mṛṇmaye bhitticitre vā pīṭhe karmārcanaṃ bhavet

siṃhaṃ padmaṃ ca vimalaṃ yathā krameṇa vinyaset

caturbhujaṃ triṇetraṃ ca prasannavadanānvitam

nirmālyaṃ ca visṛjyātha śikhāmantreṇa deśikaḥ

puṣpaṃ vai śikhayā caiva vāmo dhūpaṃ ca dīpakam

bhakṣyaṃ caiva gaṇānāṃtvā evameva krameṇa tu

puruṣaṃ pūrvadeśe tu aghoraṃ dakṣiṇena tu

īśānyāmīśamantraṃ tu āgneyyāṃ hṛdayaṃ nyaset

īśānyāṃ ca śiraścaiva netramastraṃ diśāsu ca

śukraṃ bṛhaspatiṃ caiva aśvinyau ca tathaiva ca

śāstā caiva tu durgā ca pradakṣiṇe krameṇa tu

vibudhamīśadigbhāge nirmālyaṃ dhārakaṃ nyaset

pūrvoktena vidhānena agnimukhaṃ ca kalpayet

saptāhutiṃ ca vighneśaṃ mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ

nityotsavaṃ tataḥ kuryādvighneśa pratimānvitam

brahmacārī śucirbhūtvā soṣṇīssottarīyakaḥ

pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā ālayaṃ tu praveśayet

utsavaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ kārayettataḥ

mudgamāṣa samāyuktaṃ sarṣapena samanvitam

sugupte sthāpayettatra dhvajārohaṇamārabhet

vistāreṇa samaṃ pucchaṃ tadardhaṃ śikharaṃ bhavet

yaṣṭinā saṃghaṭitvā tu dhvajamāropayettataḥ

yāgasya maṇṭapaṃ kṛtvā īśe vā cāgnigocare

paṃcaṣaṭsaptahastairvā ṣoḍaśastambha saṃyutam

vedibāhye tu paritaḥ paṃcakuṇḍāni kārayet

vedīmadhye nyasecchālīṃ darbhaiścaiva paristaret

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vinyasecchālimadhyame

sa vastrānsāpidhānāṃśca sa hiraṇyānsakūrcakān

samidājyacaruṃ lājān apūpena samanvitam

gaṇānāṃceti hutvā tu pūrṇāhutiṃ yadasya ca

śūlaṃ para"'sukhaḍgaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ padmaṃ ca saṃyutam

prathamaṃ bhūtarātraṃ tu dvitīyaṃ gandhamucyate

siddhānāṃ paṃcamaṃ proktaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ rākṣasamucyate

paṃcāhaṃ ṛṣirātrādi siddhāditryahaṃ bhavet

kautukaṃ balidānānte kuryādgrāma pradakṣiṇam

snapayetkalaśaṃ tatra hṛdayena tu deśikaḥ

sādhitaiḥ kalaśaistatra snāpayenmūlaberakam

prāgagraṃ tu catussūtraṃ udagagraṃ tathaiva ca

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ kalaśānnyasya vittamaḥ

dvitīyāvaraṇe caiva vidyeśvarāṃśca daivatān

pradhāna kumbhe svarṇasya pādyaṃ pūrve tu vinyaset

nair-ṛtyāṃ gandhatoyaṃ ca vāruṇyāṃ tu kuśodakam

īśānyāṃ dadhi saṃyuktaṃ prathamāvaraṇānvite

rajanī cūrṇaṃ tu kadalītilasarṣapa saṃyutam

nāraṃgasya dvayaṃ caiva dvitīyāvaraṇe tathā

evaṃ vai snapanaṃ kuryāt sarvaśāntikaraṃ param

sthāpane paṃcaniṣkaṃ tu utsave tu dviniṣkakam

mūrtipānparivārāṇāṃ yathā śaktyā tu dakṣiṇā

iti vīratantre vighneśasthāpanārcanasnapanotsavavidhiraṣṭapaṃcāśaḥ paṭalaḥ

guruvaktrāttu vijñeyamanyathā naiva kathyate

īśādi sadyaparyantaṃ uddharettu yathākramam

cakraṃ trayodaśaṃ grāhya paṃcādaśa samanvitam

vāsavasya caturthaṃ tu śūlādyaṃ tu yathā tathā

vajrāntena samāyuktaṃ īśānasya tṛtīyakam

tadeva punaruddhṛtya agnibījordhvavāsavam

varuṇasya tṛtīyaṃ tu vāsavena samanvitam

vajrāddvitīyasaṃyuktaṃ varuṇasya tu paṃcamam

vāsavasya caturthaṃ tu śaṃkarānyamataḥ param

somasyaiva dvitīyaṃ tu ṣoḍaśena samanvitam

* * * * * * * * punastadbīja saṃyutam

varuṇāpati saṃgrāhya cakrasyāntena saṃyutam

gadāvāyostukaṃ dvitīya sthānena samanvitam

puruhūtadvitīyaṃ tu pūrvoktena samanvitam

mārutādyaṃ samuddhṛtya cakrāmudrā * * * *

mārutasya dvitīyāntaṃ vahnāvāsava saṃyutam

anilasya paṃcamenaiva vāsavasya dvitīyakam

dhanadasya caturthena yuktaṃ cakratrayodaśam

śaṃkarasya dvitīyāntaṃ varuṇādyena saṃyutam

dvitīyāntavāsavasyāntaṃ vāsavasya trayodaśam

somāntaṃ tu daśāntena pavanasya caturthakam

īśānasya tṛtīyaṃ tu vāsavena samanvitam

vahnibījordhva * * * dvāsavena samanvitam

ekādaśena saṃyuktaṃ mārutasya caturthakam

jalādhipa caturthāntaṃ vāsavennaṃ samanvitam

īśānasya tṛtīyaṃ tu vāsa * * * śāntakam

vahnibīja samāyuktaṃ vāsavena samanvitam

anilaṃ tu samuddhṛtya vāsavaikādaśena tu

ṣoḍaśena samāyuktaṃ ghoraṃ sarvārthakāmadam

dhanadasya ca caturthaṃ tu vajrāntena samanvitam

varuṇāntaṃ samuddhṛtya vāsavaikādaśena ca

gadāyudhaṃ samuddhṛtya vāsavena samanvitam

pavanaṃ gṛhya * * * * * nte naiva bhūṣitam

śūlasyāntaṃ samuddhṛtya khaḍgāntasyādadhastataḥ

varuṇasya paṃcamaṃ gṛhya vāsavena samanvitam

gadāntāntāṃ samuddhṛtya vajrapaṃcamabhūṣitam

vajrāntena tu saṃyuktaṃ gadāvāsava saṃyutam

pavanasya paṃcamaṃ grāhya ṣoḍaśena samāyutam

somāntāntaṃ samuddhṛtya vajrāntena samanvitam

īśasya paṃcamaṃ grāhya vajreṇaiva samanvitam

vahniṃ caiva samuddhṛtya vāsavena samanvitam

kuberasya caturthaṃ tu * va tasya tṛtīyakam

candrāntaṃ tu samuddhṛtya cakreṇaiva samāyutam

kuberāntaṃ samuddhṛtya indrādyena samāyutam

aṃkuśasya tṛtīyaṃ tu vajreṇaiva samāyutam

candrasyaiva caturthaṃ tu vajrāntena samāyutam

kuberāntaṃ vāsavenaiva khaḍgaṃ caiva tu paṃcamam

pāśasya paṃcamaṃ grāhya vāsavena samanvitam

vāsavena samāyuktaṃ vāyupaṃcamameva ca

varuṇasya paṃcamaṃ paścād vajreṇaiva samāyutam

īśānāntaṃ samuddhṛtya vajreṇaiva vibhūṣitam

agnibījaṃ tatordhvaṃ tu vāsavena samāyutam

analasya paṃcamaṃ paścād vajreṇaiva samāyutam

pavanasya caturthaṃ tu vāsavena samanvitam

sadyādīśānāṃ ca dharmakāmārthamokṣadam

brahmoddhāramidaṃ proktaṃ vijayaṃ maṇḍalaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre brahmoddhāravidhirekonaṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

vijayamaṇḍalamevaṃ tu brūhi me śaśibhūṣaṇa !

sarvapūjāphalaṃ caiva sarvayajña pitāmaha !

sṛṣṭuvanāmanuṣṭhābhiḥ brahmaṇaḥ padadāyikā

vijayāpatimanuṣubhiḥ sarvānisya sādhanam

viprakṣatriyaviṭchūdrairanyajātistu varjayet

paṃcagavyena saṃsnāpya mahā havirnivedayet

prāsādasya purobhāge gomayenānulepayet

vaiśyasyāṣṭāṃgulaṃ caiva śūdrāṇāṃ ca ṣaḍaṃgulam

maṇḍalaṃ tatra madhye tu padapuṣpaṃ tu vinyaset

maṇḍalaṃ tatra vinyasya sahasraṃ vā tadardhakam

nityaṃ triṃśapaścatamā vāṣṭottaraṃ śatam

evaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ kṛtvā pūrvarukṣaṃ samuddharet

yathā śraddhānukālasya nityaṃ maṇḍalapūjanam

pālikā ghaṭikā caiva śarāvaṃ ca yathākramam

aṣṭadroṇena śālīnāṃ tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ bhavet

aṃkurānarpayitvā tu jvaladbhirdīpasaṃyutam

adhamaṃ caikasandhyāyāṃ nityaṃ dīpasamāyutam

evaṃ saptadinaṃ kuryād rātrau caivādhivāsayet

ṣoḍaśastambhasaṃyuktaṃ caturdvāra samanvitam

umā maheśakumbhaṃ tu mūla bimbaṃ tu pūjayet

sthaṇḍilaṃ tu tadūrdhve tu kuṃbhaṃ caiva tu vinyaset

uśīraṃ candanaṃ caiva karpūraṃ caiva kuṃkumam

homayettu viśeṣeṇa sarvadravya samāyutam

sa sūtra kūrcasaṃyuktaṃ pūjayettu viśeṣataḥ

prabhāte vimale kuryātsnānaṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ

nṛttageyasamāyuktaṃ stotramaṃgalavācakaiḥ

snapanaṃ tu tataḥ kuryādyathā śāstroktamārgataḥ

devānāṃ svarṇapaṭṭaṃ tu umāhe matsya maṃgalam

sthalikā daṇḍighaṇṭā ca mahāghaṇṭāṃ tu dāpayet

saptamaṇḍalamālikhya saptalokānsupūjayet

maṇḍalaṃ tu pṛthakkuryāt pratyekaṃ prasthaśālinā

svarṇaparvatamevaṃ tu lavaṇaṃ parvameva vā

mudgaparvatamevaṃ tu saptaparvatameva vā

ācāryaṃ pūjayetpūrvaṃ vastrāṃgulīyakādibhiḥ

uttamaṃ tu triniṣkaṃ tu ācāryasya tu dakṣiṇā

sarvaṃ māheśvarairyuktaṃ bāhujānāṃ tu pūjayet

vastraṃ dhānyaṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ candanaṃ dāpayettadā

sarvānamāvṛtaṃ kuryāttadgṛhaṃ praviśettataḥ

ārdrāyāṃ caiva nakṣatre deveśaṃ pūjayettataḥ

kulaikaviṃśamuddhṛtya śivalokaṃ sa gacchati

iti vīratantre vijayamaṇḍalavidhiḥ ṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

tatsarvaṃ brūhi me nātha ! śrotumicchāmyahaṃ prabho

śailajaṃ ratnajaṃ caiva dhātujaṃ dārujaṃ tathā

caturvidhaṃ tu śailaṃ syātsaptadhā ratnajaṃ tathā

mṛṇmayaṃ dvividhaṃ proktaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ saptadhocyate

śailajaṃ liṃgamākhyātaṃ dravyasya lakṣaṇamucyate

suśvetaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ jayadaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam

śilālakṣaṇa saṃyuktaṃ dhanadhānya sukhapradam

bhṛṃgamudgasamākāraṃ pūja * * karī smṛtā

viprakṣatriyavaiśyānāṃ api dhānyasamṛddhidā

śvetā prātipadā proktā brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ

marakataṃ puṣparāgaṃ ca nīlaṃ caiva tu ratnajā

ākarṣaṇe tu vaiḍūryaṃ sphaṭikaṃ putravṛddhidam

nī * * * raṇe kārye ratnajānāṃ phalaṃ bhavet

tāmraṃ putrasamṛddhyarthaṃ kāṃsyaṃ vidveṣakārakam

sīsakaṃ rogakaraṇaṃ * * māyuṣyavardhanam

devadāru śamīvṛkṣaṃ pippalaṃ candanaṃ tathā

madhūkapadmaḍuṇḍūka karṇikārastathaiva ca

ete vargāstu catvāro dvijātikramayogataḥ

saikataṃ gomayaṃ cānnaṃ paiṣṭaṃ gaulaṃ phalaṃ tathā

saikataṃ mokṣakāmānāṃ gomayaṃ roganāśanam

gaulaṃ prītipradaṃ proktaṃ phalamiṣṭārtha siddhidam

kṣaṇikānāṃ tu liṃgānāṃ na kuryātpīṭhabandhanam

prayāṇakāśakra nānāntu śubhāśubhaparīkṣakāḥ

dhvajaṃ ca bherī murajaṃ śubhāni

sarvaśatru patitākṣaya pīḍā yātragaśubhaṃ satatā

sunimittaṃ bhavedgacched durnimittamahe sthitiḥ

yasmindeśe tathā kṛtvā prāṅmukhaḥ sa pathi sthitaḥ

yātatapāsthigandhāṃśca saṃkṣinnākṣara vāriṇi

vakrāścarkarāpyaṃ ca ka ikṣāṃ jharjharīdarīm

sūtra pādavadākāro rekhāmiva tridhā smṛtā

veṇuṃkaravadākāraṃ lūtāpādasamāyutā

kalaṃka kṛṣṇarogābhaṃ kṛṣṇābhūka sannibham

trāsaṃ sphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ śuktibhyānnarasannibhā

jambūphalasamākārā snānacūccakasaṃnibhā

vimalaṃ hemasadṛśaṃ kāṃsyaṃ lohasya saṃnibham

kṛṣṇe kṛṣṇāni naśyanti śvete kṛṣṇa sugandhitā

kharjūrapatra * * * dūrvāstambākṛtiḥ śubhā

kālakaṃ rogadaṃ jñeyaṃ trāsaraṃ rāṣṭravardhanam

darī vināśayordhānyaṃ hanti tanmale * * *

ekavarṇāśilā grāhyā bhuktimuktipradāyikā

strīpuṃ napuṃsakā śailā bālā vṛddhā tu madhyamā

ghaṇṭāśabdāttu pulliṃgāḥ svarahīnā napuṃsakāḥ

dīrghaśabdata yā vṛddhā hṛsvā bālā iti smṛtāḥ

pādādhānaśilāṃ kuryānnapuṃsakena viśeṣataḥ

yasyagarbha gṛhārdhaṃ tu liṃgaṃ kanyasamucyate

paṃcabhāgaṃ tribhāgaṃ tu uttamaṃ liṃgamucyate

śrīpradaṃ caiva sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ rājyadaṃ bhavet

roganāśamidaṃ jñeyaṃ paittalaṃ liṃgamucyate

aṃgulyādīva tasyāntaṃ sarveṣāṃ jātirucyate

sphaṭikaṃ marakataṃ caiva māṇikyaṃ padmarāgakam

indranīlaṃ puṣparāgaṃ vajraṃ caiva tu kārayet

svayoniṃ pauṣṭikaṃ jñeyaṃ anyoktaṃ mokṣakāmadam

ratnānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ dhātujaṃ pīṭhamucyate

liṃgatāratribhāgaṃ tu pīṭhavistāramucyate

vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā ṣoḍaśāṃśena bhājitam

padmatuṇḍaṃ caturthāṃśaṃ dvayaṃśaṃ valayaṃ bhavet

evaṃ pīṭhaṃ samākhyātaṃ stvānāṃ phalamucyate

māṇikyaṃ muktidaṃ jñeyaṃ padmarāgaṃ dhanārthakam

vaiḍūryamāyurvṛddhiḥ syādrāṣṭravaśyaṃ pravālakam

evameva krameṇaiva jñeyaṃ ratnasya lakṣaṇam

evaṃ dvividhamākhyātaṃ calasthāpanakaṃ śṛṇu

hastā ca revatī caiva svātī caiva triruttarā

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam

śālināsthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā tasyordhve pālikā nyaset

navāhairvāpi saptāhaiḥ paṃcāhairvā viśeṣataḥ

pratiṣṭhā maṇṭapaṃ kṛtvā pūrvakalpaṃ tu vittamaḥ

udyāne śuddhadeśe tu gṛhe vāpi viśeṣataḥ

ṣoḍaśastambha saṃyuktaṃ madhyame vedikā bhavet

yāvadaśraṃ bhavetpīṭhaṃ tāvadaśraṃ tu kuṇḍakam

maṇṭapaṃ bhūṣayedvidvān pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

puṣpamālyairalaṃ kṛtya kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam

praviśenmaṇṭapaṃ tatra jale caivādhivāsayet

savastrānsahiraṇyāṃśca vidyeśvara samanvitān

jalāduttīrya pūrvedyuḥ liṃgaṃ prakṣālayettataḥ

śuddhiṃ kṛtvā viśeṣeṇa puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nyasya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ nyaset

śayanaṃ paṃcadhā kalpya yathā pūrvoktamārgataḥ

muṇḍajaṃ tu dukūlaṃ vā kārpāsikamathāpi vā

vṛkṣatvacāca kartavyaṃ carmajaṃ tu vidhīyate

śayane śāyayeddevaṃ prāsādena tu mantrataḥ

prākāraṃ paścime pīṭhaṃ śayane śāyayeddhṛdā

śuddhodakena saṃpūrya śarāveṇa pidhāya ca

dvārakalaśaṃ vinyasya dvārapālānsamarcayet

sthāpayedvidhinā vipra ! svasvanāmnā viśeṣataḥ

vardhanīṃ gauribījena vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ

daśāyudhaṃ prakartavyaṃ svarṇenaiva tu deśikaḥ

vidyeśvarānnyasettatra svasvanāmnā ca mantrataḥ

naivedyaṃ pāyasaṃ dadyād hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

samidājya caruṃ lājānsarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

samidho hīśamantreṇa puruṣeṇājyameva ca

sarṣapaṃ sadya mantreṇa hṛdayena yavaṃ tathā

ghṛtamikṣetiṃ mantreṇa ghṛtaṃ caiva tu hūyate

rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ tatra svapnāni ca parīkṣayet

ekāgniṃ vātha paṃcāgniṃ kuryādvai deśikottamaḥ

homaṃ na kārayedvidvān samidājya caruṃ vinā

sarvadravya samāyuktaṃ havyavāhaṃ samuccaran

hutvānte tu yadasyeti juhuyāttu viśeṣataḥ

mantranyāsoktamārgeṇa nyāsaṃ kṛtvā tato budhaḥ

snāpayecchivakumbhaṃ tu vardhanīmapi piṇḍikām

snapanaṃ kārayettatra śatāṣṭaṃ paṃcāśadeva tu

paṃcavarṇahavirdadyāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayettataḥ

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

calaliṃga sthāpanaṃ proktamumayā sahitaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre calaliṃga pratiṣṭhāṃvidhiḥ eka ṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

sṛṣṭimūrtipratiṣṭhāṃ tu brūhi me parameśvara !

umāskandahitaṃ caiva sukhāsanaṃ tathaiva ca

gaṃgādharaṃ ca paṃcaite sṛṣṭirūpamihocyate

pratirūpasargaṃ pūjā ca tiṣṭhā gati nivṛtti ca

yamarohiṇyāditya puṣyendu hasta pauṣṇa triruttarā

sthirarāśau prakartavyaṃ ubharāśāvathāpi vā

pratiṣṭhā nakṣatra pūrve tu aṃkurānarpayettataḥ

prāsādasyāgrataścaiva maṇḍapaṃ kārayettataḥ

ekādaśa dvādaśakaraṃ trayodaśakaraṃ tathā

ṛṣicchando devatānāṃ vakṣyāmi surasattama !

bhāradvājo bhṛguścaiva tathāṃgirasagautamāḥ

tṛṣṭup cānuṣṭup ca * * * * * prakṛtistathā

uttamottamamevaṃ tu chandasānāṃ vidhīyate

vighneśaśca kuberaśca brahmā viṣṇurmaheśvaraḥ

kramād jñātvā tu viprendra ! kārayedvidhi pūrvakam

dāruṇā ca prakartavyaṃ śilpinā lakṣaṇairyutam

āsanaṃ cāpi vṛkṣaṃ vā kramukaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ tu vā

avakraṃ nirvraṇaṃ caiva puṣakoṭara varjitam

kūṭaṃ vā maṇṭapaṃ vāpi prapāṃ vāpi ca kārayet

evaṃ vai maṇṭapaṃ kṛtvā madhye vediṃ tu kalpayet

caturaśrārdhacandraṃ ca vṛttaṃ padmaṃ tathaiva ca

īśāna indrayormadhye ṣaṭkoṇamiti kathyate

umāsahitamevoktaṃ ekāsanasthite'pi ca

caturaśraṃ ca yoniṃ ca trikoiṇaṃ vṛttameva vā

īśāna indrayormadhye saptakoṇaṃ tu kārayet

pūrvādīśāccaturdikṣu vedāśraṃ kuṇḍamucyate

kalyāṇa sundaraṃ proktaṃ candraśekharamucyate

koṇeṣu vṛttakuṇḍaṃ tu śivāgniṃ cāṣṭakoṇakam

sukhāsanavaddikṣvaṣṭau śivāgniṃ cāṣṭakoṇakam

ekāsanasthitādevyā yonyākāraṃ vidikṣu vai

vitānaiścordhvamācchādya aṣṭadikṣu dhvajānnyaset

dhvajamadhye likhitvā tu aṣṭadikṣu ca vinyaset

tṛtīyaṃ pratimāśuddhiḥ caturthaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇam

saptamaṃ śayanaṃ caiva aṣṭamaṃ homameva ca

daśarimaṃmiha proktaṃ teṣāṃ bhedaṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā śālinā vikirettataḥ

paristarettu darbhaiśca navavastreṇa chāditam

brāhmike tu nyasedvajraṃ indranīlaṃ tu pūrvataḥ

nair-ṛtyāṃ puṣparāgaṃ tu mauktikaṃ vāruṇe nyaset

īśānyāṃ tu mahānīlaṃ evaṃ devasya vinyaset

caturdikṣu ca madhye ca svarṇāni nikṣipettataḥ

pratimāṃ kṣālayettatra āmvenaṃ vāriṇā punaḥ

ādityasya pade caiva netramokṣaṇamucyate

taṇḍulaṃ ca tadardhaṃ ca tadardhaṃ tilameva ca

umā skandasahitaṃ nyasya tasyopari viśeṣataḥ

tāmrapātre'thavā kāṃsye madhvājyaṃ caiva nikṣipet

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau pūrvādīśāntaṃ vinyaset

sasūtrānsāpidhānāṃśca lokapālādhidevatān

kṛṣṇamaṇḍalamālikhya jyotirmaṇḍalamālikhet

suvarṇanakha saṃyuktaṃ hemadūrvā samanvitam

akṣihomaṃ tataḥ kuryāt tasyāgre tu viśeṣataḥ

pūrvoktenaiva mārgeṇa agnimukhaṃ ca kārayet

aṣṭottaraśatenaiva pratyekaṃ hūyate kramāt

madhyame skandamevaṃ tu skandamantreṇa hūyate

svarṇena nakhadūrveṇamadhvājyaṃ tu tarpayet

ghṛtamikṣeti mantreṇa ghṛtaṃ caivaṃ tu tarpayet

brāhmaṇāndarśayettatra brahmajajñānamantrataḥ

darśayedbrahmacārīṃśca skandasyaiva tu deśikaḥ

āpohiṣṭheti saṃprokṣya arghya saṃyutameva ca

paṃcagavya samāyuktaṃ abhiṣekaṃ tu kārayet

śuddhairuttheti mantreṇa śuddhodakena snāpayet

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt sarvālaṃkārasaṃyutam

gītavādya samāyuktaṃ śaṃkhakāhala saṃyutam

nānābhaktajanaissārdhaṃ kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam

nadītaṭākatīre vā hrade gatvā viśeṣataḥ

śālinā sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā pratimāṃ tatra vinyaset

navavastreṇa saṃveṣṭya kūrcenaiva samanvitam

caturdvāra samāyuktaṃ prapāṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ

sa vastrānsāpidhānāṃśca vidyeśvara samanvitān

ekarātraṃ tritātraṃ vā paṃcarātramathāpi vā

vāstuhomaṃ divā kuryāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

ālayaṃ praviśettatra puṣpadantapade sthitaḥ

gaurībījena devyātha guhaṃ vai skandabījataḥ

uśīraṃ candanaṃ caiva elālavaṃga saṃyutam

tato'dhivāsanaṃ kuryāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nyasya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ nyaset

toraṇānarcayettatra dvārakumbhāṃśca vinyaset

savastrānsāpidhānāṃśca lokapālādhidevatān

śayanaṃ paṃcabhiḥ kalpya aṇḍajādyairanukramāt

kṣaumaṃ kārpāsajaṃ vāyaṃ muṇḍajasyeti kīrtitam

vṛkṣatvacānukartavyaṃ carmajaṃ tadvidhīyate

paṃcabrahmeṇa mantreṇa śayanaṃ paṃca kalpayet

pracchādanena saṃyuktaṃ śayane śāyayecchivam

prākśiraḥ paścime pādaṃ vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ

navakumbhānsamādāya trisūtrairveṣṭayettataḥ

paṃcaratna samāyuktaṃ vastrayugmena veṣṭayet

paṃcabrahmeṇa saṃyuktaṃ śivakumbhaṃ ca vinyaset

skandakumbhaṃ nyasenmadhye skandamūlena mantrataḥ

sa vastrānsāpidhānāṃśca sahiraṇyānsakūrcakān

ambikāṃ ca umādevīṃ īśapatnīṃ tathaiva ca

prajñāṃ cāgasutāṃ caiva pārvatī ca tathaiva ca

kumāraṃ ca guhaṃ caiva subrahmaṇyaṃ tathaiva ca

śaravaṇodbhavaṃ caiva kārtikeyaṃ ca vinyaset

gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca dhūpaiśca svasvabījena pūjayet

elālavaṃgakarpūra timbujātīphalaṃ muram

vardhanyāṃ sudakumbheṣu evameva kramānnyaset

tato homaṃ prakurvīta lakṣaṇena samanvitam

paścime cakravṛkṣastu udumbaraṃ tu cottare

śamī caiva apāmārgaṃ bilvavṛkṣa samanvitam

soma īśānayormadhye palāśa samidhā tathā

mudgaṃ māṣaṃ ca viprendra ! navadravyamihocyate

aghoreṇa caruṃ hutvā lājaṃ tatpuruṣeṇa tu

śirasā ca tilaṃ hutvā śivamantreṇa mudgakam

triyambakena mantreṇa caruṃ caiva tu hūyate

evaṃ krameṇa hotavyaṃ tattanmantreṇa deśikaḥ

dravyaṃ prati viśeṣeṇa hotavyaṃ deśikottamaḥ

dravyānte caiva dravyānte pratimāṃ saṃspṛśettataḥ

caturdikṣu caturvedaṃ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

paścime camakaṃ prācyāṃ pavamānaṃ tathottare

māheśvara samāyuktaṃ nṛttaṃ kṛtvā tu bhaktitaḥ

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāttadanantare

tataḥ prabhāte vimale snānaṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ

sṛṣṭirūpa pratiṣṭhāyāṃ sṛṣṭinyāsamudāhṛtam

samidājyacaruṃ lājān datvā paṃcadaśāhutiḥ

ājyaṃ sadyena hotavyaṃ hṛdayena caruṃ hunet

anyairhomaṃ na kartavyaṃ ācāryeṇaiva hūyate

hutvānte tu yadasyeti pūrṇāhuti mathācaret

ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ dadyādyathā śāstrokta mārgataḥ

paṃcāṃgabhūṣaṇaṃ dadyāt śikhākarṇe gale tathā

adhamaṃ daśaniṣkaṃ tu madhyamaṃ dviguṇaṃ bhavet

tadardhamupadeṣṭā ca vāstuhomaṃ tadardhakam

daivajñadviguṇaṃ tasya dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayettataḥ

paryaṣṭi parivārāṇāṃ yathā śaktyā tu dakṣiṇā

sumuhūrte sulagne ca devamutthāpayettataḥ

ghṛtaṃ śirorpaṇaṃ kṛtvā hemadūrvāṃkurākṣataiḥ

mantranyāsoktamārgeṇa paṃcabrahmeṇa saṃyutam

brahmamaṃgena saṃyuktaṃ snāpayecchiva kumbhakam

skandamūlena viprendra ! skandakumbhābhiṣecayet

vardhanīṃ sthāpayeddevyāḥ sahāsanagate yadi

snapanaṃ kārayettatra naivedyaṃ dāpayettataḥ

bhojayetsaptakanyāśca brahmacārīṃśca bhojayet

kāpālaṃ pāṃcarātraṃ ca ṣaḍaite samayāḥ smṛtāḥ

śaivānsaṃbhojayettatra samayānbhojayettataḥ

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

pratiṣṭhākaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ ācāryāya pradāpayet

iti vīratantre sṛṣṭimūrtipratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ dvāṣaṣṭhitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

vaktumarhasi deveśa anugrahārthaṃ prabho ! mama

rohiṇī ca punavasve tiṣye mṛgaśirastathā

sthirarāśau ca kartavyaṃ ubhayorapi kārayet

ṛṣabhaṃ vāhanaṃ caiva ardhanārī tathaiva ca

ete tu paṃcamūrtiśca sthitimūrtirudāhṛtā

paṃcaṣaṭ saptahastaṃ vā aṣṭahastaṃ navaṃ tathā

avakraṃ nirvraṇaṃ kṛtvā paṃktimānamathocchrayam

dhvajamadhye likhettatra lokapālasya vāhanam

darbhamālābhirācchādya muktādāmairalaṃkṛtam

pūrve tu caturaśraṃ tu agneraśvatthapatravat

vṛttaṃ tu vāruṇe caiva vāyavyāṃ paṃcakoṇakam

indraīśānayormadhye aṣṭakoṇaṃ tu kārayet

īśāna indrayormadhye ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ nyaset

pūrvādiṣu caturdikṣu kuṇḍaṃ syāccaturaśrakam

ardhanārīśvaraṃ proktaṃ kirātārjunamucyate

vṛttapaṃcāśrapadmaṃ ca saptakoṇāṣṭakoṇakam

sahāsane tu viprendra ! āgneyādiṣu yonikam

kirātārjunamevoktaṃ vīreśaṃ śṛṇu tatvataḥ

aśvatthaṃ ca trikoṇaṃ ca paṃcāśraṃ cāṣṭakoṇakam

saptakoṇaṃ bhavetkuṇḍaṃ vīreśamadhunocyate

soma īśānayormadhye hariṇāṃ vṛttakuṇḍakam

evameva krameṇaiva kuṇḍaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ

ratnanyāsaṃ prakartavyaṃ satyakasya pade'thavā

navavastra samāyuktāṃ pratimāṃ tatra vinyaset

indranīlaṃ ca vaiḍūryaṃ sphaṭikaṃ marakataṃ tu vā

pūrvādiṣu nyasedetān paṃcabrahma samuccaran

ardhanārīśvaraṃ caiva śivabhāge tu vinyaset

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kuryān netrayugmayute dine

śālyopari nyased devaṃ pracchannapaṭasaṃyutam

śilpinaṃ pūjayet tatra śilpinaṃ tu visarjayet

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ kalaśānaṣṭa vinyaset

hiraṇya nakhadūrvā ca ghṛtapātramapātrakam

paṃcāṃgabhūṣaṇairyuktaḥ prokṣayet pratimāṃ tataḥ

ājyena caruṇā caiva netreṇaiva śatāhutiḥ

madhvājyaṃ tarpayed dhīmān netramantrasamanvitam

ghṛtamikṣe ghṛtaṃ tarpya savatsāṃgāṃ pradarśayet

pracchannapaṭamāvarjya janān sarvān pradarśayet

pavamānaṃ samuccārya paṃcagavyābhiṣecanam

grāmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryād rathe vā śibike'pi vā

nadītaṭākatīre vā gomayenānulepayet

snāpayet paṃcagavyena vyomavyāpi samuccaran

sa vastra paridhānena kūrcenaivacchādayet

prapāṃ kṛtvā tu viprendra ! kalaśānaṣṭa vinyaset

ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā paṃcarātramathāpi vā

vāstuhomaṃ divā kuryāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

vyomavyāpi samuccārya snāpayedgandhatoyakaiḥ

sarvagandhaṃ samāloḍhya lepayet pratimāṃ tataḥ

śālinā vikiredbediṃ bhāradvaya samanvitam

lājairdarbhaiśca vikired aṇḍajādīni kalpayet

vastreṇaivordhvamācchādya vyomavyāpi ṣaḍaṃgakaiḥ

sthāpayecchivakumbhaṃ tu vardhanīṃ sthāpayecchivam

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ aṣṭakumbhānnyasettataḥ

tasya bāhye tu parito lokapāla ghaṭānnyaset

ekāsanastha devyāśca antarālāśca vardhanī

aśvatthodumbaraplakṣavaṭaṃ pūrvādidikṣu ca

gaurī vṛṣabha kuṇḍābhyāṃ palāśenaiva hūyate

yavasarṣapa saṃyuktaṃ navadravyaṃ ca hūyate

puruṣeṇa caruṃ hutvā lājānghoreṇa caiva hi

tilānvai śiromantreṇa aṣṭottaraśatāhutiḥ

ādyākṣaraṃ tu hṛsvaṃ ca dīrghaṃ brahmāṃga mucyate

dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā sparśayetpratimāṃ tataḥ

sthiti nyāya prakāreṇa nyāsaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ

pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā sarvadravya samanvitam

īśānena tu mantreṇa kuryāt pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ

upadeṣṭā mūrtipāṃścaiva padārthīn sarvāṃśca pūjayet

sthitimārga prakāreṇa aṣṭatriṃśatkalaśān nyaset

geyanṛttādisarvāṃśca prāsādaṃ ca pradakṣiṇam

prāsādena pratiṣṭhāpya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya vastrairābharaṇairapi

brāhmaṇānbhojayettatra śaivānsaṃbhojayettataḥ

sthitimūrtiridaṃ proktaṃ saṃhārasthāpanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre sthitimūrtipratiṣṭhā triṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

kiṃ vā saṃhāramūrtīnāṃ sthāpanaṃ parameśvara !

nṛttaṃ caiva tu kāmāri kāladāhaṃ tathaiva ca

ṣaṣṭhe saṃhāramūrtīnāṃ sthāpanaṃ tviha cocyate

haste ca revatī caiva svātī caiva triruttarā

aṃkurānarpayet pūrvaṃ navāhe saptame'thavā

dakṣiṇe cottare cāpi kārayellakṣaṇānvitam

maṇṭapaṃ caiva viprendra ! ekāśītipadaṃ kuru

tasya bāhye tu parito mānuṣe kuṇḍamucyate

vṛttaṃ caiva ṣaḍaśraṃ ca padmaṃ saptāśrameva ca

īśāna indrayormadhye aṣṭakoṇaṃ ca kārayet

caturaśraṃ ca ṣaṭkoṇaṃ trikoṇaṃ paṃcakoṇakam

īśāna indrayormadhye saptakoṇaṃ tu kārayet

tripuraghnaṃ tathā dikṣu catuṣkoṇaṃ trikoṇakam

īśāna indrayormadhye saptāśraṃ kārayettataḥ

satyakasya pade caiva ratnanyāsaṃ tu kārayet

tasyoparinyaseddevaṃ ṣaṇṇavatyaṃ viśeṣataḥ

puṣṭikaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca puṣparāgaṃ tathaiva ca

māṇikyaṃ madhyame sthāpya śivamantreṇa deśikaḥ

susnigdhaśālinā kuryātpuṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ

ratnanyāsamidaṃ proktaṃ netramokṣaṇamucyate

nayanadvayayukte tu dine lagne śubhe tathā

nṛttarūpaṃ tadūrdhve tu vinyasecchiva mantrakaiḥ

sthāpayet kalaśānaṣṭau lokapālādhidevatān

suvarṇasūcinā vipra ! śilpinā praharettataḥ

jyotirmaṇḍalamālikhya netramantra samāyutam

netramantreṇa viprendra ! aṣṭottaraśataṃ pṛthak

suvarṇadūrvayā vipra ! madhvājyaṃ caiva tarpayet

tāmrapātre'thavā kāṃsye ājyaṃ caiva tu vinyaset

ghṛtamikṣeti mantreṇa ghṛtaṃ caiva tu tarpayet

gordhenurbhavyamantreṇa darśayedgāṃ viśeṣataḥ

ṣaṇṇavatyena mantreṇa prokṣayet tu viśeṣataḥ

pracchannapaṭamāvarjya darśayenmānuṣāṃstataḥ

śuddhiṃ kṛtvā viśeṣeṇa tantrīṇāṃ ca saṃyutam

sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktaṃ kuryādgrāma pradakṣiṇam

prapāṃ kṛtvā viśeṣeṇa kalaśān vinyaset tataḥ

ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā paṃca vā saptarātrakam

maṇḍapasyaiśadigbhāge snānavedyupari nyaset

ghṛtaṃ śirorpaṇaṃ kuryāt svarṇadūrvāṃkurākṣataiḥ

kṣālayecchuddhatoyena * * * nantu kalpayet

aṣṭatriṃśat kalānyāsaṃ ṣaḍaṃgenaiva vinyaset

sumūhūrte sulagne ca nakṣatrakaraṇānvite

ṣaṇṇavatyena mantreṇa abhiṣekaṃ tu kārayet

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ kalaśe vā bhiṣecayet

pūrvokta lakṣaṇairyuktaṃ arcayet tu viśeṣataḥ

pratiṣṭhopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ ācāryāya pradāpayet

śaivān saṃbhojayet tatra yathā śaktyādi dakṣiṇā

yāvad rudrapuraṃ sarve dadhyākṣaye naraḥ

ādyeṣu tritayeṣveṣu na dhvajārohaṇa kriyā

evaṃ kṛte mahāśānti rājarāṣṭrasukhāvaham

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokaṃ sa gacchati

iti vīratantre saṃhāramūrtipratiṣṭhavidhiḥ catuṣṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

kathaṃ gaurī pratiṣṭhā ca brūhi me parameśvara !

rohiṇī ca punarvasvo puṣye mṛgaśirastathā

sthirarāśau tu kartavyaṃ aṣṭamaṃ grahavarjitam

dvinetrāṃ cāruvadanāṃ sustanāṃ ca suyauvanām

karaṇḍamakuṭāṃ caiva sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam

pādanūpura saṃyuktāṃ devyā rūpamihocyate

pūrvoktalakṣaṇairyuktaṃ maṇṭapaṃ kārayettataḥ

tasyā vṛtapadaṃ trīṇi madhyame kuṇḍamucyate

lakṣaṇena prakartavyaṃ pūrvoktavidhinā saha

hemaṃ tatraiva nikṣipya aṣṭadikṣu krameṇa tu

dvitīyavarge viprendra ! tṛtīyākṣara saṃyutam

bindunāda samāyuktaṃ mūlamantramihocyate

hṛsvā brahma samākhyātā dīrghāhyaṃgāni cocyate

ādityasya pade caiva gomayenānulepayet

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nyasya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ nyaset

navavastraṃ nyasettatra pratimāṃ vinyaset tataḥ

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vāmādyādīni vinyaset

kṛṣṇamaṇḍalamālikhya jyotirmaṇḍalamālikhet

vastreṇāveṣṭya tad devyāḥ pracchanna paṭasaṃyutam

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vāmādyādīni vinyaset

agnimukhaṃ ca saṃkalpya paṃcāśatsaṃkhyayā hunet

tāmrapātre'thavā kāṃsye ghṛtaṃ madhu ca pūrayet

madhuvātā ṛtāyeti madhunā tarpayettataḥ

gaurdhenu bhavyamantreṇa savatsāṃgāṃ pradarśayet

netramantrasamāyuktaṃ dhānyarāśīṃ ca darśayet

paṃcagavyena viprendra ! abhiṣekaṃ samācaret

nadītaṭākakatīre vā gomayenānulepite

snāpayet paṃcagavyena jalādhivāsanaṃ kuru

jalamadhye nyased devīṃ gaurībījena deśikaḥ

savastraṃ sāpidhānaṃ ca vāmādyāścaiva vinyaset

ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā paṃcarātramathāpi vā

snāpayet paṃcagavyena navavastreṇa veṣṭayet

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nyasya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ nyaset

tasyo parinyaseddevīṃ gaurīmūlena deśikaḥ

pracchanna paṭasaṃyuktaṃ puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tataḥ

śuddhodakena saṃpūrṇāḥ pratyekaṃ vastraveṣṭitāḥ

gaurībījena mantreṇa sa kūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam

pratyekaṃ vastrasaṃyuktaṃ pradhāne hema vinyaset

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ manonmanīṃ tvīśagocare

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya gaurīmūlena mantrataḥ

tato homaṃ prakurvīta nava vā paṃca eva vā

gaurīmūlena mantreṇa samidhā hūyate kramāt

śiromantreṇa lājaśca mudgaṃ vāmena hūyate

evameva krameṇaiva pratyekaṃ tu śatāhutiḥ

devasyatveti mantreṇa annāhutimathācaret

havyavāheti mantreṇa sarvadravya samanvitam

nṛttageya samāyuktaṃ rātrau jāgaramucyate

gaurīmūlaṃ japettatra āgneyādi caturdiśi

mantranyāsoktamārgeṇa sṛṣṭi nyāsaṃ tu kārayet

samidājyacarūṇāṃ tu homayed deśikottamaḥ

hutvānte tu yadasyeti sviṣṭakṛddhūyate kramāt

vastrairābharaṇairyuktaṃ gobhūmīkāṃcanādibhiḥ

tato muhūrtamudvīkṣya devīmutthāpayet kramāt

sṛṣṭinyāsa krameṇaiva devyā aṃgeṣu vinyaset

snāpayet tu viśeṣeṇa gaurīmūlamanusmaran

snapanaṃ kārayetpaścād yathā vibhavavistaram

brāhmaṇān bhojayettatra saptakanyāśca bhojayet

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā śālinā vikirettataḥ

sthaṇḍile sthāpayeddevīṃ diśi dakṣiṇato nyaset

tatra viṣṇuṃ samāhūya dadyādudaka pūrvakam

hṛdayenātiṣṭha mantreṇa aśmānaṃ sparśayet tataḥ

īśānena tu mantreṇa lājahomaṃ tu kārayet

triḥkṛtvā tu viśeṣeṇa hutvā cittādibhistathā

bhinnāsanamidaṃ proktaṃ sahāsanamataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre gaurī sthāpanavidhiḥ paṃcaṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

sauvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ lohajaṃ vāpi kārayet

lakṣaṇaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathā vadanupūrvaśaḥ

adhamaṃ saptāṃgulaṃ proktaṃ pādukasya tu dairghyakam

aṃgulyādi trayāṃgulyaṃ utsedhaṃ * * * * *

evaṃ lakṣaṇa saṃyuktāṃ pādukāṃ kārayet tataḥ

ratnānāmapya lābhe tu suvarṇādīni kārayet

maṇṭapasya tu saṃskāraṃ vibhavena samanvitam

darbhamālābhirāveṣṭya catustoraṇa saṃyutam

muktādāmairalaṃkṛtya puṣpamālopaśobhitam

aṣṭadroṇena śālīṃśca tanmadhye vinyasettataḥ

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhya darbhaiścaiva paristaret

jalāduttīrya deveśaṃ śuddhiṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ

ācāryaśca śucirbhūtvā navavastreṇa veṣṭayet

ācāryasya ca bījena sarvāṃgeṣu ca vinyaset

upadeṣṭā śarīraṃ tad ācāryo jīva ucyate

mantranyāsaṃ prakartavyaṃ lakṣaṇena samanvitam

aṃguṣṭhādi kaniṣṭhāntaṃ paṃcabrahma nyaset kramāt

astraṃ karatale caiva vinyaset tu viśeṣataḥ

prākṛtaṃ bhāvamutsṛjya śivohamiti bhāvayet

svarṇasūtreṇa saṃyuktaṃ kautukaṃ bandhayet tataḥ

samāloḍhya ca sarvaṃ tu lepayet tu samantataḥ

pratimaṃ navavastreṇa pidhāya sakalaṃ tataḥ

navakumbhaṃ samādāya sūtreṇāveṣṭayet tataḥ

sa vastraṃ sa hiraṇyaṃ ca paṃcaratna samanvitam

pratimasyaiśa digbhāge sthāpayecchiva kumbhakam

vardhanīṃ tasya vāme tu gaurī bījena sthāpayet

* * * * * * * * tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

tato homaṃ prakartavyaṃ lakṣaṇena samanvitam

khadiraṃ ca śamī caiva apāmārgaṃ tathaiva ca

sarveṣāmeva homānāṃ madhye pālāśamucyate

mudgamāṣaṃ ca viprendra ! navadravyamihocyate

caruhomaṃ śikhā caiva lājānvai kavacena tu

īśānena tilaṃ hutvā mudgaṃ sadyena hūyate

sahasraṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhārdhamathāpi vā

ugro'tidhāśrīmo ete homasya daivatāḥ

balavikaraṇī balapramathinī sarvabhūta manonmanī

dravyānte pratimāṃ spṛṣṭvā paṃcabrahma ṣaḍaṃgakaiḥ

vyomaṃ prāsādamantraṃ ca brahmamaṃgaṃ tathaiva ca

trāṇi parvāṇi te yaṃ nṛtta samākulaiḥ

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāttadanantare

hūyate kramaśo vipra ! vyāhṛtiṃ juhuyāttataḥ

paryaṣṭi paricārāṃśca śivadīkṣāvaho viduḥ

athā vasānahomaṃ tu pradhānāgnau tu hūyate

prabhāte dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād ācāryādi ca sarvaśaḥ

paṃcāṃgabhūṣaṇaṃ dadyād ācāryasya viśeṣataḥ

uttamaṃ triguṇaṃ proktaṃ ācāryasya tu dakṣiṇā

stotrādadhvayīnādīnāṃ tadardhaṃ dakṣiṇā śubhe

padmasya pūrvadigbhāge vedāśraṃ kuṇḍamucyate

pavamānena saṃprokṣya śuddhairutthai tato druvat

pādukau vinyasenmadhye navavastrasya copari

bhūtavargānta vargeṣu agniṃ saṃyojayet tataḥ

śivaviṣṇusamārūḍhaṃ pādukābījamucyate

śrībhūmī dakṣiṇā diśi tathā devau prakīrtitau

pūrayecchuddhato yena sa kūrcānsāpidhānakān

pādukādīśa digbhāge vardhanīṃ vinyasettataḥ

manonmanīṃ pradhānasya vinyasettu viśeṣataḥ

tato homaṃ prakartavyaṃ pūrvoktavidhinā saha

sumuhūrte sulagne ca vardhanīṃ vinyasettataḥ

gandhapuṣpādinā''pūjya vinyasecchivamantrataḥ

ācāryaṃ pūjayetpūrvaṃ paryaṣṭi paricārakān

nityotsavaṃ tu kāle tu pradakṣiṇamathācaret

brahmacārī vahitvā tu pādukāvarcayet tataḥ

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

pādukā sthāpanaṃ proktaṃ citrasthāpanakaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre pādukāsthāpanavidhiḥ ṣaṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

citrapratiṣṭhā kiṃ devabrūhi me parameśvara !

citraṃ ca trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ teṣāṃ bhedaṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

mṛṇmayaṃ śūlasaṃyuktaṃ bhitticitre paṭe'pi vā

citrasya saṃnidhau caiva pratiṣṭhāmaṇṭapaṃ kuru

ratnanyāsaṃ ca kartavyaṃ umādevyā viśeṣataḥ

vedikā brahmabhāge tu mānuṣe kuṇḍamucyate

devyāśceddikṣu viprendra ! yoniraśvattha patravat

nayanamokṣaṇaṃ kuryān netrayugmayute dine

jalādhivāsanaṃ noktaṃ śayane śāyayet tataḥ

bhāraśāli samāyuktaṃ vedimadhye paristaret

paristaret tu darbhaiśca aṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇakam

darpaṇaṃ śāyayettatra pracchanna paṭasaṃyutam

devyāścaiva tu vāmādi vinyaset tu viśeṣataḥ

* * * * * * * devaṃ devyā vardhanīṃ nyaset

gandhapuṣpādinā'bhyarcya pāyasānnaṃ nivedayet

samidājya caruṃ lājān sarṣapā * gameva ca

sadyena samidhā hutvā hṛdayenājyameva ca

sarṣarpaṃ netramantreṇa mudgamīśānamantrataḥ

sarvadravya samāyuktaṃ havyavāhaṃ samuccaran

tataḥ prabhāte vimale snānaṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ

tato vāsāna homaṃ tu ājyena caruṇāyutam

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryād adhyayanaṃ tataḥ

hutvānte tu yadasyeti juhuyāt tadanantaram

homīnāṃ tu padārthīnāṃ pūjayet tu viśeṣataḥ

tasyordhve darpaṇaṃ sthāpya mūlamantreṇa saṃyutam

pradhāna kalaśaṃ caiva snāpayet tu viśeṣataḥ

havirnivedayet paścāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ ca sthāpana kramameva hi

pratiṣṭhopakaraṇaṃ sarvaṃ ācāryāya pradāpayet

iti vīratantre citrapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ saptaṣaṣṭhitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

vimānasthāpanaṃ kiṃ vā brūhi me parameśvara !

rohiṇī ca punavasvā puṣyo mṛgaśirastathā

dvitīyā paṃcamī caiva dvādaśī vā trayodaśī

evamādiṣu kāleṣu vimānasthāpanaṃ kuru

prāsādasyaiva purataḥ prapāṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ

madhyame vedikāṃ kuryād hastamātrocchrayaṃ bhavet

nāgaraṃ drāviḍaṃ caiva vesaraṃ tu śṛṇuṣvatha

vṛttaṃ ca paṃcakoṇaṃ ca padmaṃ caivāṣṭakoṇakam

caturdikṣu ca īśānyāṃ yathoktaṃ kuṇḍamucyate

vitānadhvajasaṃyuktaṃ puṣpamālyairalaṃkṛtam

kuberasya vidhiḥ prokto drāviḍasya tu vṛttakam

tasya pūrve jalāvartaṃ vastramālyairalaṃkṛtam

caturdikṣu nyasettatra hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

ācāryaśca śucirbhūtvā navavastreṇa veṣṭitaḥ

prāsādaṃ darbhamālābhirveṣṭayet tu viśeṣataḥ

vāstudevabaliṃ dadyāt pūrvokta vidhinā saha

lambayennavavastreṇa prāsādena tu mantrataḥ

prāsādahomaṃ kartavyaṃ garbhagehe viśeṣataḥ

samidhāmīśamantreṇa ājyaṃ vāmena hūyate

śāntihomakrameṇaiva caturdikṣu ca homayet

prāsādameva homena śivāsanamatha śṛṇu

śālinā vikirettatra bhāradvayena saṃyutam

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhya darbhaiścaiva paristaret

śuddhodakena saṃpūrya vastrakūrcasamanvitān

navakumbhān samādāya sūtreṇa pariveṣṭayet

caturdaśasvarasaṃyuktaṃ bindunādasamanvitam

pradhānakalaśe naiva * * * * * vinyaset

pūrve tu viṣṇukumbhāsyu dakṣiṇāmūrtidakṣiṇe

pūrvādiṣu caturdikṣu śivavidyeśvarasya tu

uttare caiva durgāyāṃ vinyaset tu viśeṣataḥ

havirnivedayet paścāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

samidājya caruṃ lājān sarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

īśānyādīni sadyāntaṃ hṛdayena trayaṃ yutam

vimānabījaṃ sarveṣu hutvā caiva śatāhutiḥ

mantranyāsoktamārgeṇa nyāsaṃ kṛtvā viśeṣataḥ

tato vāsana homaṃ ca kartavyaṃ deśikottamaḥ

vimānabījasaṃyuktaṃ paṃcaviṃśatirucyate

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāttadanantare

hutvānte tu yadasyeti pūrṇāhutimathācaret

śirasopari viprendra ! kumbhamudvāhayet tataḥ

vimānamūlasaṃyuktaṃ vyomavyāpi samuccaran

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ snāpayet svasvamantrataḥ

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya havirdadyādviśeṣataḥ

saptakanyāśca viprendra ! vastrādīni ca dāpayet

vimānasthāpanaṃ proktaṃ mānuṣasthāpanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre vimānasthāpanavidhiraṣṭaṣaṣṭitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

mānuṣasthāpanaṃ ko vā śivajñānābhipūritān

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva caturānana !

śivaprasāda siddhāṃśca pratimādīni kārayet

śailajaṃ dārujaṃ vāpi mṛṇmayaṃ citrameva ca

catvāri paṃcaṣaṭtālaṃ mātrāṃgulena kārayet

śivaprasādakāle tu yadrūpaṃ saṃbhavanti vai

ṛṣīṇāmṛṣirūpaṃ tu nṛpāṇāṃ nṛpavadbhavet

ilā ca piṃgalā caiva suṣumnā ca tathaiva ca

alaṃpuruṣā ruhrī ca śaṃkhinī daśamaḥ smṛtaḥ

vimalā ghoṣaṇī caiva pṛthivī āvinī tathā

śodhanī vyādhinī caiva rasāvatī ca mudvatī

sarvagā caiva nāḍyastā viṃśatissahitāṣṭabhiḥ

tasya mantraṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

saptamasya dvitīyaṃ tu prathamasya caturdaśa

hṛsvā brahma samākhyātā dīrghāhyaṃgāni vinyaset

devatānāṃ vāhanānāṃ sthāpayedevameva tu

navaratnaṃ paṃcaratnaṃ vā vinyaseddeśitottamaḥ

madhyame kalpayeddevīṃ paritaḥ kuṇḍamucyate

pūrvādiṣu caturdikṣu īśānyādyaṣṭakoṇakam

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā adhivāsya tato budhaḥ

vāstu homaṃ tataḥ kuryāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

śayanaṃ paṃcabhiḥ kalpya kārpāsairvastraveṣṭitam

kāye tu vinyasedvipra ! paṃcaviṃśatikaṃ tathā

śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca rasogandhaśca paṃcamaḥ

śrotratvakcakṣurjihvā ca ghrāṇaṃ caiva tu paṃcamam

paṃcaviṃśati tatvāni śarīre vinyaset tataḥ

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya paścātkumbhāni vinyaset

pratimā dakṣiṇe bhāge kuṃbhaṃ mūlena sthāpayet

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ sthāpayet kumbhamaṣṭakam

nāgaḥ kūrmaśca kṛkaro kumbhe pūrvādi vinyaset

dhanaṃjayottare nyasya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

tato homaṃ prakurvīta palāśasamidhā saha

samidājya carūnlājānsarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

paṃcabrahma ṣaḍaṃgena pratyekaṃ tu śatāhutiḥ

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

tataḥ prabhāte vimale snānaṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ

tato vāsanahomaṃ tu samidājya carūnsaha

hutvānte tu yadasyeti sviṣṭakṛddhūyate kramāt

upadeṣṭāraṃ saṃpūjya yathā śāstrokta mārgataḥ

mantranyāsokta mārgeṇa sṛṣṭi nyāsaṃ tu kārayet

paṃcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgena prāṇādatyaya kumbhakān

naivedyaṃ pāyasaṃ dadyāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

hāṭakaṃ vastra saṃyuktaṃ sarveṣāṃ dāpayet tataḥ

mānuṣaṃ sthāpanaṃ proktaṃ piṇḍikā sthāpanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre mānuṣasthāpanavidhiḥ ekona saptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

piṇḍikā sthāpanaṃ kiṃcit tatsarvaṃ me vada prabho !

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryān maṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam

stambhaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiryuktaṃ caturdvārasamanvitam

vitānadhvajamācchādya digdhvajaiśca pralambitam

śālibhirvikirettatra taṇḍulaiśca tilaistathā

pīṭhaṃ prakṣālayedvipra ! prāsādena tu mantrataḥ

valmīke ca taṭāke ca kuśamūle tathaiva ca

śuddhakṣetre tu vai brahma mṛdbhissaṃkṣālayedbudhaḥ

puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tatra svastimaṃgalavācakaiḥ

śuddhodakena saṃpūrya vinyaset pīṭhabāhyataḥ

vardhanyāṃ nikṣipet tatra pūrvādīśāntameva ca

sakūrcaṃ ca suvarṇaṃ ca vastrayugmaṃ pṛthak pṛthak

dakṣiṇe caiva raudrī ca kālī nir-ṛti gocare

balapramathinīṃ saumye vinyaset tu viśeṣataḥ

madhye manonmanīṃ devīṃ nyastvā gandhādinā yajet

tato homaṃ prakurvīta kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile'pi vā

vṛttapīṭhasya he brahman vṛttakuṇḍamihocyate

samidhassadyamantreṇa ājyaṃ vāmena caiva hi

veṇumīśānamantreṇa mudgaṃ ca śirasā tathā

gaurībījena mantreṇa pratyekaṃ tu śatāhutiḥ

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāt tadanantare

upānaṃ pādamityuktaṃ jagatī jaṃghonujaṃ tathā

kaṇṭhaśca hṛdayaṃ proktaṃ nābhiścaiva tu paṭṭikā

kaṇṭhaśca hṛdayaṃ proktaṃ bāhūcaivordhva vāmanam

evaṃ vai padmapīṭhe tu teṣāṃ rūpaṃ prakalpayet

tataḥ prabhāte (vimale) sumuhūrte sulagnake

paścādgarbhagṛhaṃ gatvā ācāryaḥ śilpibhissaha

vṛṣagāyatrimantreṇa prāsādena samanvitam

pīṭhaṃ visarjayet tatra jalamadhye tu nikṣipet

ṣaṇṇavatyakṣarairyuktaṃ vyomavyāpi samuccaran

aṣṭabandhaṃ prakartavyaṃ lakṣaṇena samanvitam

vāyuke neṣṭakā cūrṇaṃ sadṛśaṃ ghanatailakam

tāmrapātre viśeṣeṇa pācayecchilpinā saha

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivaloke mahīyate

tat tadaṃśāni vinyasya sūtrāgramupakalpanam

tanmadhye vedikāṃ kuryādaratnimātramucchritām

gātrasaṃveṣṭanaṃ kuryād vitānenordhvaṃ veṣṭayet

vedibāhye tu parito navakuṇḍāni kārayet

tat tat pīṭhākṛtiṃ brahman tat tat kuṇḍamihocyate

tadardhaṃ taṇḍulān nyasya taṇḍulārdhatilān nyaset

bālasthānasya cāgre tu yātrāhomaṃ tu kārayet

sa sūtraṃ sāpidhānaṃ ca savastraṃ hema saṃyutam

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vidyeśvara samanvitān

ācāryaśca śucirbhūtvā śirasā dhārayed ghaṭam

vimāna pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryānvedimadhye nyaset tataḥ

gandhapuṣpādinā pūjya pāyasānnaṃ nivedayet

samāloḍhya viśeṣeṇa liṃgamāloḍyayet tataḥ

tato homaṃ prakurvīta lakṣaṇena samanvitam

udumbaramapāmārgaṃ khadiraṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

īśāna indrayormadhye śamīṃ caiva tu vinyaset

sarveṣāṃ caiva homānāṃ palāśedhyamihocyate

samidhaṃ sadyamantreṇa vaktreṇājyaṃ tathaiva ca

netreṇaiva tu mudgaṃ syān māṣaṃ hṛdayamucyate

prāsādena tu mantreṇa pratyekaṃ paṃcaviṃśatiḥ

sarvadravya samāyuktaṃ havyavāhaṃ samuccaran

jayādirabhyadhānaṃ ca rāṣṭrabhṛccaiva hūyate

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

puruṣasūktaṃ japitvā ca stotreṇaiva samanvitam

navavastraparīdhānaḥ soṣṇīṣassottarīyakaḥ

praśāntamānaso bhūtvā snāpayelliṃga mūrdhani

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ vidyeśān snāpayet tataḥ

snapanaṃ kārayet tatra yathāvibhavavistaram

havirnivedayet paścāt tāmbūlaṃ dapayet tataḥ

pīṭhasthāpanamevoktaṃ jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre piṇḍikāsthāpanavidhiḥ saptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

jalasaṃprokṣaṇaṃ kiṃ vā brūhi me parameśvara

prāsādakaraṇe caiva śilpinā sparśayet tataḥ

patane dūṣite spṛṣṭe pūjāhīne tathaiva ca

trirātre paṃca saptāhe aṃkurārpaṇamārabhet

pālikā ghaṭikā caiva śarābaṃ ca tridhā bhavet

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam

ṣoḍaśastambhasaṃyuktaṃ caturdvāra vibhūṣitam

samakhaṇḍaṃ vartamānaṃ ca śivādhikyaṃ tathaiva ca

kuṇḍānāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ lakṣye samakhaṇḍasya padmaja !

indra īśānayormadhye aṣṭakoṇaṃ tu kārayet

caturaśraṃ ca yoniṃ ca ardhacandraṃ trikoṇakam

pūrvādīni kramadṛddhiṃ īśāne saptakoṇakam

trairāśikasya kuṇḍāni śrūyatāṃ caturānana !

svayamudbhūtaliṃgasya sthāpitasya maharṣibhiḥ

eteṣāṃ prokṣaṇe kāle tat tat pīṭhākṛtiṃ yathā

pūrvoktena pramāṇena kuṇḍaṃ kuryāt tu deśikaḥ

gomayenopalipyātha toraṇa dhvajasaṃyutam

vedyopari nyasecchāliṃ bhāradvayasamanvitam

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhya darbhaiścaiva paristaret

candanāgarukarpūraṃ uśīraṃ kuṃkumairyutam

puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tatra paṃcagavyena snāpayet

kautukaṃ bandhayet paścāt hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

tasyāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā śālibhistaṇḍulaistilaiḥ

vardhanīṃ tasya vāme tu gaurībījena sthāpayet

tasyāgre sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā yātrāhomaṃ tu kārayet

samidājya carūṇāṃ tu pratyekaṃ tu śatāhutiḥ

īśānena tilaṃ hutvā spṛṣṭvā liṃgaṃ tato budhaḥ

daśamudrā ca dātavyaṃ lakṣaṇena samanvitam

praviśya maṇṭapaṃ vidvān - yedvedi madhyame

bāle sthāne kṛte hyevaṃ kartavyaṃ deśikottamaḥ

navakumbhānsamādāya sūtreṇa pariveṣṭitān

paṃcaratna samāyuktaṃ vastrayugmena veṣṭitam

paṃcabrahmaśivāṃgaiśca śivamantreṇa sthāpayet

sa vastrān sahiraṇyāṃśca sakūrcān sāpidhānakān

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya pāyasānnaṃ nivedayet

samidājya carūnlājānsarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

palāśaṃ pūrvato bhāge udumbaraṃ cāgnigocare

khadiraṃ paścime nyasya vāyavye bilvameva ca

palāśasamidhā caiva pradhānāgnau tu homayet

stotramantraṃ japitvā tu pavamānaṃ japet tataḥ

caruṃ tat puruṣeṇaiva lājānghoreṇa caiva hi

tilān hutvā tu śikhayā mudgaṃ tu kavacena tu

dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ hutvā spṛṣṭvā liṃgaṃ tu sarvaśaḥ

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāt tadanantare

āpo rājā idaṃ viṣṇoḥ pratyekaṃ caru hūyate

mantranyāsokta mārgeṇa svadehe caiva vinyaset

sarvadravya samāyuktaṃ pūrṇāhutimathācaret

pūjāparyuṣito liṃgaṃ vyapohya kalaśaṃ tataḥ

paṃcāsanaṃ tadākalpya pūrvoktavidhinā saha

vyomavyāpi mahāmantraṃ vinyasya liṃgamūrdhani

vimāna pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryād garbhagehaṃ praveśayet

snāpayecchiva kumbhaṃ tu gaurībījena vardhanīm

snapanaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ yathā vibhavavistaram

paṃcavarṇahavirdadyāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

viprādīṃśca dadecchaivān paryaṣṭi paricārakān

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

iti vīratantre jalasaṃprokṣaṇavidhireka saptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

vṛṣabha sthāpanaṃ kiṃ vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

pūjāṃśocca pramāṇena adhamaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ bhavet

pūjāṃśena samāyuktaṃ madhyamaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ bhavet

athavā garbhamānena yathāvad garbhamucyate

athavā tālamānena tritālamadhamaṃ bhavet

sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vā śilā bhavet

galamaṃśena kartavyaṃ ṣaḍaṃśaṃ pṛṣṭhadairghikam

dvandvayoradhamāṃśaṃ tu gomukhākṛtireva ca

karṇadvayasamāyuktaṃ kiṃkiṇīrupaśobhitam

pādāccatvāramaṃśaṃ tu caturaṃśaṃ tu bālakam

ūrumūlasya vistāraṃ dvayaṃśamiti kathyate

śailaje mṛṇmaye lohe ratnanyāsaṃ tu kārayet

indranīlaṃ ca ratnādi pūrvādika caturdiśi

vṛṣabhaṃ kṣālayed vidvān puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tataḥ

pūrvokta vidhinā kuryād akṣihomādikārayet

vṛṣagāyatrimantreṇa jale caivādhivāsayet

jalāduttīrya pūrvedyuḥ praviśedālayaṃ prati (tataḥ)

madhyame vedikāṃ kuryāt paritaḥ kuṇḍamucyate

saptavargāccaturthaṃ tu tadvargāttu tṛtīyakam

vṛṣabhamūlamidaṃ jñeyaṃ hutvā brahmamihocyate

tadūrdhve vinyasedvipra paṃcabhiḥ kambalairyutam

pracchanna paṭa saṃyuktaṃ abhitaḥ kalaśān nyaset

vṛṣagāyatrimantreṇa vinyased deśikottamaḥ

aṣṭavidyeśvarān bāhya kalaśasyādhi daivatān

tato homaṃ prakurvīta palāśasamidhā saha

īśānādīni sadyāntaṃ kramān mantramihocyate

vṛṣaṃ spṛṣṭvā tu dravyānte vyāhṛtiṃ juhuyāt tataḥ

havyavāhādimantreṇa pūrṇāhutimathācaret

gāyatryā paṃcabrahmaṃ tu snāpayet kumbhameva ca

ācārya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt padārthīn pūjayet tataḥ

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokaṃ sa gacchati

vṛṣabhasthāpanaṃ proktaṃ athāgnisthāpanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre vṛṣabhasthāpanavidhirdvāsaptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

agnipratiṣṭhāṃ deveśa ! tat sarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

prāsādasyāgni digbhāge agnivimānamucyate

athavā garbhamānaṃ vā navadhā bhājite tathā

dvitālamadhamaṃ jñeyaṃ tritālaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet

eka hṛdayaṃ tripādaṃ ca catuśśṛṃgaṃdvayaṃśiraḥ

padmāsanasamārūḍhaṃ ūrdhvakeśa samanvitam

sruvākṣatomaraṃ caivetyete vāmakareṣu ca

evaṃ samāsataḥ proktaṃ pratiṣṭhālakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu

marakataṃ dakṣiṇe nyasya paścime tu pravālakam

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kuryān netradvayayute dine

bindunāda samāyuktaṃ agnimūla mudāhṛtam

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvoktavidhinā saha

jalādhivāsanaṃ kuryāt tasya mūlena mantrataḥ

prāsādasyāgrataścaiva pūrvoktamaṇḍapaṃ kuru

caturdikṣu ca īśānyāṃ trikoṇaṃ kuṇḍamucyate

pratisthaika digbhāge agnimūlena vinyaset

śayanaṃ paṃcabhiḥ kalpyaḥ pratimāṃ vinyaset tataḥ

tasyaiva vāmabhāge tu svāhā bījena vardhanam

hutāśanaṃ pāvakaṃ ca analaṃ havyavāhanam

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ vinyaset tu viśeṣataḥ

tato homaṃ prakartavyaṃ palāśasamidhāyutam

mudgamāṣaṃ ca nīvāraṃ bilvaṃ caikādaśaṃ bhavet

tataḥ prabhāte vimale snānaṃ kṛtvā tu deśikaḥ

tato vāsana homaṃ ca samidājya carūn yutam

vimāne sthāpayedvidvān pratimaṃ paścimaṃ mukham

svasvanāmnaiva mantreṇa snāpayet kumbhameva ca

brāhmaṇān bhojayet tatra paryaṣṭiparicārakān

agnisthāpanamevoktaṃ mātṝṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre agnisthāpanavidhistrisaptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

mātṝṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ kiṃ vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā

vighneśaṃ pratimaṃ kṛtvā sthāpayed vidhipūrvakam

caturbhujāṃ dvinetrāṃ ca padmāsanopari sthitam

vīrabhadraṃ mayā proktaṃ brāhmī caiva śṛṇuṣvatha

dakṣiṇe'dakṣiṇe haste akṣasūtrakamaṇḍalum

trinetrāṃ caturbhujāṃ caiva jaṭāmukuṭa dhāriṇīm

padmāsanopariṣṭāt tu māheśvarīṃ ca kārayet

dakṣiṇe'dakṣiṇe haste akṣamālākamaṇḍulum

dvinetrāṃ caturbhujāṃ devīṃ śaṃkhacakragadādharām

kārayed vaiṣṇavīṃ devīṃ vārāhīṃ ca tataḥ śṛṇu

makuṭena samāyuktāṃ varāha mukha saṃyutām

caturbhujāṃ dvinetrāṃ ca śyāmavarṇa samāyutām

caturbhujaṃ triṇetraṃ ca vighneśaṃ kārayet tataḥ

māyānala samāyuktaṃ bindunāda samāyutam

hṛsvā brahma samākhyātā dīrghāhyaṃgāni cocyate

nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvokte naiva mārgataḥ

jalādhivāsanaṃ kuryāt pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

pūrvavanmaṇḍapaṃ kuryāt pūrvokte naiva mārgataḥ

caturaśraṃ yonikuṇḍaṃ ardhacandraṃ trikoṇakam

pūrvādīni kramān nyasya kuṇḍānyetāni vinyaset

puṇyāhaṃ vācayettatra pavamānena prokṣayet

śayanaṃ paṃcabhiḥ kuryāt tasyopari nyaset tataḥ

kaumārīṃ vahnideśe tu vaiṣṇavīṃ dakṣiṇe nyaset

cāmuṇḍīṃ vāyudeśe tu uttare vīrabhadrakam

tasyaiva vāmabhāge tu kumbhānvinyasya deśikaḥ

vastreṇa veṣṭayed vidvān suvarṇe naiva saṃyutān

tato homaṃ prakurvīta lakṣaṇena samanvitam

samidājya carūnlājān sarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

paṃcabrahma ṣaḍaṃgaiśca pratyekaṃ tu śatāhutiḥ

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

tato vāsanahomaṃ tu samidājyena saṃyutam

hutvānte tu spṛśedbimbaṃ dravyaṃ prati viśeṣataḥ

uttarābhimukhīrdevīḥ sthāpayed deśikottamaḥ

pratiṣṭhākumbhamādāya snāpayet svasvamantrataḥ

brāhmaṇānbhojayet tatra saptakanyāśca bhojayet

mātṝṇāṃ sthāpanaṃ proktaṃ kumārasthāpanaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre mātṛsthāpanavidhiścatuḥ saptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

kumārasthāpanaṃ kiṃ vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

pūjābhāgasamutsedhaṃ pratimaṃ socchrayaṃ bhavet

catvāraḥ paṃcatālaṃ vā ṣaṭtālaṃ vāpi kārayet

varadābhayahastaṃ tu akṣamālākamaṇḍalum

jayā ca vijayā caiva devyau pārśva sthite sadā

ebhistu lakṣaṇairyuktaṃ pratimāṃ kārayet tataḥ

prathamasya caturthaṃ tu bindunāda samanvitam

hṛsvā brahma samākhyātā dīrghābhyaṃgāni cocyate

vajraṃ marakataṃ caiva vaiḍūryaṃ ca pravālakam

susnigdhaṃ kārayettatra nayanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ kuru

akṣihomaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā netreṇaiva śatāhutiḥ

ghṛtamikṣeti santarpya savatsāṃgāṃ pradarśayet

* * * * tṛtīyaṃ tu māyānala samanvitam

saptavargāccaturthaṃ tu māyātatva samanvitam

brāhmaṇān darśayet tatra puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tataḥ

navavastrasamādhānaḥ kuryādgrāmapradakṣiṇam

mūlamantreṇa viprendra ! pracchanna paṭasaṃyutam

jalāduttīrya pūrvedyuḥ snāpayet paṃcagavyakaiḥ

madhyame vedikāṃ kuryātparitaḥ kuṇḍamucyate

indra īśānayormadhye kuryātkuṇḍaṃ trikoṇakam

śālinā vikiredvedīṃ taṇḍulaṃ ca tilaṃ nyaset

śāyayed vedikāmadhye prākśiraskā vidhīyate

kautukaṃ bandhayet pūrve hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

jayā ca vijayā caiva dvau pārśvau sthāpayet kramāt

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ aṣṭakumbhān kramānyaset

senāpatiṃ surāgraṃ ca harasūnuṃ tathaiva ca

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya svasvanāmnā ca mantrataḥ

tato homaṃ prakurvīta pālāśasamidhā saha

śālinīvāragodhūma mudgaiścaiva samanvitam

skandamūlena hotavyamājye naiva śatāhutiḥ

tato vāsanahomaṃ tu samidājyacarūyutam

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

sviṣṭamagneti mantreṇa juhuyāttadanantare

utthāpya śayanāddevīṃ sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ tu kārayet

snāpayet skandamūlena skandakumbhaṃ viśeṣataḥ

āvāhayet tu vāmena sadyena sthāpanaṃ bhavet

gandhādyā hṛdayā caiva puṣpaṃ tu śirasā tathā

naivedyaṃ hṛdaye naiva nityotsavaṃ tu kārayet

pratimāṃ pūrvato gatvā paścāt tu kṣaṇikaṃ bhavet

utsavaṃ kārayet tatra lakṣaṇena samanvitam

skandasthāpanamevoktaṃ jyeṣṭhā sthāpanakaṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre skandasthāpanavidhiḥ paṃcasaptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

jyeṣṭhāyā sthāpanaṃ kiṃ vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

pūjāṃśocca pramāṇena pratimotsedhamucyate

catvāraḥ paṃcaṣaṭtālaṃ pratimasyocchrayaṃ bhavet

makuṭena samāyuktāṃ raktavastra samanvitām

dakṣiṇe putrasaṃyuktāṃ sūkarānanasaṃyutām

evaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ devyā rūpaṃ prakalpayet

mūlamantraṃ pravakṣyāmi dvitīyasya tṛtīyakam

bindunādasamāyuktaṃ jyeṣṭhāmūlamiti smṛtam

prathamasyaikādaśaṃ yuktaṃ bindunāda samanvitam

paṃcamāt paṃcamaṃ gṛhya bindunāda samanvitam

ratnanyāsaṃ tu kartavyaṃ paṃcaratnaṃ tu mūlataḥ

akṣihomaṃ tataḥ kuryād ājyenaiva śatāhutiḥ

jalādhivāsanaṃ kuryān mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ

ekarātraṃ trirātraṃ vā paṃcarātramathāpi vā

pūrvoktamaṇḍape ramye madhye vedikayā yutam

vṛttaṃ vā caturaśraṃ vā sarvakuṇḍāni kārayet

vāstu homaṃ divā kuryāllakṣaṇena samanvitam

śayanaṃ paṃcabhiḥ kalpya gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

dakṣiṇe vāmapārśve tu putraṃ putrī samanvitam

vāmādi śakti saṃyuktaṃ kākaṃ ca kramān nyaset

savastrāṃ sāpidhānāṃ ca sahiraṇyaṃ sa kūrcakam

tato homaṃ prakurvīta pālāśasamidhā saha

śālinīvāragodhūma mudge naiva samanvitam

mūlamantreṇa viprendra ! aṣṭottaraśatāhutiḥ

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

mūlamantreṇa hotavyaṃ pratyekaṃ tu śatāhutiḥ

utthāpya śayanāddevīṃ sthāpayen mānuṣe pade

paṃcabrahma śivāṃgaiśca snāpayecchakti kumbhakān

brāhmaṇān bhojayet tatra śaivān saṃbhojayet tataḥ

aṃganyāsakaranyāsaṃ paṃcabrahmeṇa kārayet

āvāhayet tu sadyena vāmena sthāpanaṃ bhavet

hṛdayena caruṃ dadyān nityotsavaṃ tu kārayet

prāsādasya samotsedhaṃ dhvajadaṇḍaṃ vidhīyate

bherītāḍanakaṃ kṛtvā aṃkurārpaṇameva ca

tato grāmabaliṃ dadyāt śuddhānnaṃ pratisamāyutam

tṛtīyamṛṣirātraṃ tu caturthaṃ gandhamucyate

jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ saptamaṃ rātramityetā rātridevatāḥ

snapanaṃ kārayet tatra lakṣaṇena samanvitam

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ sa puṇyāṃ gatimāpnuyāt

iti vīratantre jyeṣṭhā sthāpanavidhiḥ ṣaṭsaptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

snapanaṃ vividhaṃ kiṃ vā tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

grahaṇe viṣuve caiva ayane vyatipādake

adbhutānāṃ samutpanne prāyaścittādi saṃbhave

prāsādasyāgrataścaiva maṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam

ekādaśakaraṃ vātha trayodaśakaraṃ tu vā

adhamaṃ pūrvatrayaṃ siddhaṃ madhyamaṃ cāparaṃ bhavet

ṛṣicchando devatā vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kamalāsana !

ādityena samāyuktaṃ uttamatrayamucyate

vighneśena samāyuktaṃ madhyamaṃ trayamucyate

chandenaiva samāyuktaṃ adhamatrayamucyate

maṇṭapaṃ navaṣaṭtriṃśat * * * gātra saṃyutam

pūrvoktena vidhānena maṇṭapaṃ kārayet tataḥ

proktaṃ tu navabhedena procyate tu mayā dvija !

vitānenordhvamācchādya muktādāmairalaṃkṛtam

caturdvāra samāyuktaṃ catustoraṇa saṃyutam

sṛkpaṭṭadevāṃgaiḥ netradīpairalaṃkṛtam

sahasrakalaśaṃ caiva uttamottamamucyate

paṃcāśadadhika dviśataṃ uttamādhamamucyate

ekāśītistu madhyaṃ syān madhyamasya viśeṣataḥ

sumuhūrte ca sulagne ca sūtrapādaṃ tu kārayet

aṣṭādhikaṃ ca viprendra ! catvāriṃśacca saṃyutam

uttarāgraṃ tathaiva syādevameva krameṇa tu

madhyame paṃcaviṃśattu taddvayāvṛta vīthikān

bāhye padadvayaṃ nyastvā paṃca paṃca padāntare

evameva krameṇaiva vidhīnāṃ lopayet kramāt

vyūhāntare prakartavyaṃ vyūhasthāpanamākramāt

puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tatra pavamānena prokṣayet

kalaśānprati nikṣipya ardhakaṃ cottamaṃ bhavet

evameva prakāreṇa hīnaṃ cāpi na kārayet

śālyardha taṇḍulaṃ nyasya taṇḍulārdhatilaṃ nyaset

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ caiva darbhaiścaiva paristaret

kalaśānāṃ vidhiṃ vakṣye uttamaṃ droṇapūritam

apakvamati pakvaṃ ca hīnaṃ kālaṃ ca varjayet

triguṇī kṛtamoraṃ vā veṣṭayet kuṃjarākṣavat

śuddhodakena saṃpūrṇaṃ kūrcamadhye vinikṣipet

paṃcāsanopariṣṭāt tu sthāpayet kumbhameva ca

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya vyomavyāpi samuccaran

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ aṣṭakumbhān nyaset kramāt

vidyeśānāṃ suvarṇaṃ ca vinyaseddeśikottamaḥ

uttamaṃ daśahastaṃ tu madhyamaṃ cāṣṭahastakam

vistāraṃ tu catustālaṃ kalaśānveṣṭayet kramāt

cūrṇitaṃ hṛdaye naiva vinyasedeśikumbhake

vastrairdhūpaiśca dīpaiśca gandhaiścaiva tu pūjayet

madhya vyūhaṃ tu vinyasya bāhya vyūhaṃ padaṃ nyaset

ājyaṃ caiva tu pūrve tu āgneyyāṃ dadhi vinyaset

vāruṇyāṃ tu madhunyasya vāyavyāṃ tu nalaṃ nyaset

evameva prakāreṇa vyūhaṃ prati nyaset tataḥ

sarṣapaṃ śālinā caiva vidikṣu vinyaset tataḥ

vibhūtiravyayaḥ śāstā pinākī pramathādhipaḥ

pūrvādi vinyased vidvān pradakṣiṇakrameṇa tu

alābhe caiva viprendra ! yathālābhaṃ vinikṣipet

kalaśānpaṃcaviṃśāṃstu vyūhaṃ prati nyaset kramāt

kuṣṭhaṃ ca kuṃkumaṃ caiva raghumāṃci tathaiva ca

uśīraṃ candanaṃ caiva tṛṇakaccolajambakam

lodhraṃ ca kuṃkumaṃ caiva caturviṃśati saṃyutam

agnirudrodbhutāśī ca piṃgalaḥ khātato haraḥ

śālinīvāragodhūma priyaṃguṃ caiva saṃyutam

lohaṃ ca kuṃkumaṃ caiva caturviṃśati saṃyutam

kālarudraśca vikhyātaḥ sarvadharma patistathā

lokāntaraḥ sphuṭā lokaḥ tryambako nāgabhūṣaṇaḥ

eteṣāṃ devatānāṃ tu vyūhe vyūhe prakīrtitāḥ

pūrvābhimukhaṃ saṃsthāpya śivomāṃ caiva deśikaḥ

gandhapuṣpādinā caiva kalaśānpratipūjayet

tato homaṃ prakurvīta lakṣaṇena samanvitam

samidājya carūnlājān sarvadravya samāyutam

cittādīni jayādīni homayet tu viśeṣataḥ

gandhapuṣpādinābhyarcya śivakuṃbhaṃ ca vardhanīm

caturdikṣu caturvedaiḥ kuryādadhyayanaṃ tataḥ

mūlaberamanuprāpya nirmālyaṃ ca visarjayet

paṃcāsano pariṣṭāt tu śivaṃ dhyāyed vicakṣaṇaḥ

tato garbhagṛhaṃ gatvā prāsādena samanvitam

sthāpayecchivakumbhaṃ tu vardhanyāṃ pīṭhakaṃ nyaset

yathā sthāpanamārgeṇa sthāpayet parameśvaram

vastraṃ dadyād hṛdā caiva gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dāpayet

ācārya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt padārthīn pūjayet tataḥ

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

iti vīratantre sahasrakalaśasthāpanavidhiḥ saptasaptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

ko vā paṃcaśataṃ snānaṃ brūhi me parameśvara !

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryān maṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam

catustoraṇa saṃyuktaṃ darbhamālopaśobhitam

muktādāmairalaṃkṛtya maṇḍapaṃ maṇḍayet tataḥ

triṃśat sūtra samāyuktaṃ prāgagrāṇi vinyaset

madhye navapadaṃ padmaṃ aṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam

tadbāhye tu paritaḥ padaṃ paṃca sthitāni tu

śālinā vikiret tatra āḍhakaṃ kalaśaṃ prati

padmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhya darbhaiścaiva paristaret

śuddhodakena saṃpūrya kumbhamādāya maṇḍape

vardhanīṃ tasya vāme tu gaurībījena sthāpayet

kūrcaṃ tatraiva nikṣipya navaratnāni vinyaset

śivakumbhe śivaṃ sthāpya vardhanyāṃ ca manonmanīm

vastrayugmena saṃveṣṭya svarṇaṃ tatraiva vinyaset

dvitīyāvaraṇe caiva ṣaṭsaptati ca vinyaset

aśītikalaśaṃ caiva vinyased deśikottamaḥ

tṛtīyāvaraṇe caiva aṣṭāviṃśati vinyaset

saptamāvaraṇe caiva śatakumbhāṃśca vinyaset

kūrcān kalaśa saṃyuktān śuddhodakena pūritān

utkūrcaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ cāhuradhaḥ kūrcaṃ tu śāntikam

elālavaṃgatakkola nimbujātī phalairyutam

gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ caiva dadhikṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ tathā

sarṣapaṃ mudgamāṣaṃ ca priyaṃguṃ ca samanvitam

ajaḥ kāpāli buddhī ca vajradehaḥ pramardanaḥ

agnīrudro hutāśaśca dvitīyāvaraṇe nyaset

tṛtīyāvaraṇe nyasya devatānāṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu kramādvinyasya devatāḥ

caturthāvaraṇe nyasya devatānāṃ śṛṇuṣvatha

saṃyoktā ca samāyuktaṃ paṃcamāvaraṇe nyaset

vyomavyāpiṃ japet tatra brahmāṃgaiśca samanvitam

yathā sthāpitamārgeṇa snāpayet kalaśāṃstataḥ

ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt paṃcāṃgabhūṣaṇaissaha

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

iti vīratantre paṃcaśatasnapanavidhiḥ aṣṭasaptatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

snapanaṃ kīdṛśaṃ deva ! tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

prāsādasyāgrataścaiva maṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam

dvāviṃśatiśca sūtrāṇi pūrvābhimukhameva ca

navapādeṣu padmaṃ syād aṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikam

yadbāhye varaṇādbāhye padadvayaṃ ca hrāsayet

śatadvayaṃ ca paṃcāśat padānyatra sthitāni tu

darbhaiḥ paristaret tatra gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

triguṇī kṛtasūtreṇa veṣṭayet kuṃjarākṣavat

jalabhāṇḍaṃ samādāya secayedudakaṃ hṛdā

elālavaṃga takkolairuśīra candanairyutam

vinyasecchivakuṃbhaṃ tu vyomavyāpiṃ samuccaran

abhitaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau vidyeśvara samanvitam

vajraṃ marakataṃ caiva vaiḍūryaṃ ca pravālakam

vinyasecchiva kumbhe tu vardhanyāṃ paṃcaratnakam

bāhyāvaraṇakaṃ tatra ṣaṭtriṃśatkalaśān nyaset

kapālīśo vajrapāṇiḥ pinākī tridaśādhipaḥ

tṛtīyāvaraṇe caiva kalaśān ṣaṣṭi vinyaset

panasaṃ kadalī caiva mātuluṃgaphalāni ca

yāmyaṃ mṛtyuharaṃ caiva dhātā dharmapatistathā

paṃcamāvaraṇaṃ caiva ṣaṭsaptatiṃ nyaset tataḥ

kālarudro hutāśaśca jvalano dahanastathā

dravyaṃ ca devatā yuktaṃ koṇe koṇe ca vinyaset

hṛdā ca samidho hutvā hṛtaṃ tu śirasā tathā

sarṣapaṃ * * * * * netramantreṇa caiva hi

sarvadravya samāyuktaṃ havyavāhaṃ samuccaran

diśāsvadhyayanaṃ kuryāt stotramantraṃ japet tataḥ

snāpayecchivakumbhaṃ tu prāsādena tu mantrataḥ

tṛtīyāvaraṇaṃ paścāt caturthāvaraṇaṃ punaḥ

paṃcavarṇa havirdadyāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

paṃcāśad dviśataṃ proktaṃ uttama trayamucyate

madhyama trayake vipra ! aṣṭottaraśataṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre paṃcāśad dviśatasnapanavidhiḥ ekonāśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

etat sarvaṃ samāsena aṣṭottaraśataṃ tvatha

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇḍapaṃ caturaśrakam

catustoraṇasaṃyuktaṃ darbhamālābhirāvṛtam

stambhāni veṣṭayet tatra gomayenānu lepayet

dvividhaṃ sūtrapādaṃ tu śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikaṃ tathā

uttarāgraṃ tathaiva syād sūtrasyaitāni nirdiśet

caturdikṣu ca viprendra ! dvārārthaṃ tu padaṃ tataḥ

tasya bāhye tu paritaḥ padadvaya sthitāni tu

idamanyat pravakṣyāmi cakrasthāpanamucyate

uttarāgraṃ tathaiva syāt tālamātreṇa cāntaram

tāsāṃ pūrve diśo vipra ! tālamātreṇa cāntaram

caturbhiḥ kalaśānnyasya pauṣṭikaṃ tviha cocyate

śālinā vikiret tatra adhikaṃ kalaśānprati

paristarecca darbhaiśca puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tataḥ

astramantreṇa kṛtvā tu vāmena śoṣaṇaṃ bhavet

āḍhakena tu saṃpūrṇān adhamaṃ tu vidhīyate

bhinnaṃ kālaṃ ca suṣiraṃ bhedacchedaṃ ca varjayet

śarāvaṃ caiva viprendra sūtreṇa veṣṭayet tataḥ

pradhāna kalaśaṃ vipra ! śuddhodakena pūrayet

kalaśasya mukhe rudraṃ viṣṇuṃ madhyamake nyaset

madhyame śivakumbhaṃ tu prāsādena tu vinyaset

śivakumbhe śivaṃ pūjya vardhanyāṃ gaurimeva ca

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ aṣṭakumbhāṃśca vinyaset

prathamāvaraṇaṃ hyetad dvitīyāvaraṇaṃ śṛṇu

pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ payaḥ

tṛtīyāvaraṇe caiva caturviṃśat kalā nyaset

lājācūrṇa śamī caiva nīvāraṃ nālikerakam

karpūraṃ hariberaṃ ca viṃśatiṃ kalaśān nyaset

agnīrudro hutāśaśca piṃgalaḥ khātako haraḥ

ete'pi daivatāḥ proktāḥ kalaśeṣūdake nyaset

catuścatvāri kalaśān caturthāvaraṇe nyaset

dāntadūrvā ca takkolaṃ puṃnāgaṃ jāti pāṭalī

vyāghranakhīśatāverī maṃjiṣṭhā nāgakesaram

bhītā ca sahadevī ca viṣṇukrāntiḥ suvarcalā

balabhadrī mahābhadrī apāmārgendu vallikā

caturthāvaraṇe caiva vinyased deśikottamaḥ

kālarudraśca vikhyāto dharmādharmapatistathā

hantā ca krūradṛṣṭiśca ūrdhvaśepho mahābhujaḥ

atiścalaḥ pāśahasto mahābala eva ca

vibhīṣaṇo mahāvego laghuvegastathaiva ca

kapardī meghavāhaśca jaṭāmakuṭadhārakaḥ

dhanuścaiva tu viprendra ! kalaśeṣūdake nyaset

itareṣāṃ tu devānāṃ śivābhimukhameva ca

prathamāvaraṇe cāṣṭau dvitīye dvādaśa nyaset

devatānāṃ ca viprendra ! catvāriṃśadghaṭān nyaset

tato homaṃ prakartavyaṃ caturdikṣu palāśakaiḥ

sadyādīśānaparyantaṃ kramād hutvā śivavrataiḥ

prāsādena tu mantreṇa paṃcāśaddhūyate kramāt

hutvānte tu yadasyeti juhuyāt tadanantare

ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt padārthīn pūjayet tataḥ

paścāt paṃcāsanaṃ kalpya sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ prakalpayet

vardhanīṃ tasya vāme tu snāpayed gauri mantrataḥ

paṃcabrahma ṣaḍaṃgena snāpayet kalaśaṃ tataḥ

caturthaṃ paṃcamaṃ snātvā evamevaṃ krameṇa tu

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

iti vīratantre aṣṭottaraśata snapanavidhiḥ aśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

ekāśītiḥ kathaṃ deva ! tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

tatsarvamakhilaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva caturānana !

pūrvokte naiva mārgeṇa kārayed deśikottamaḥ

śuddhodakena saṃpūrya śarāveṇa vidhāyate

śālyardhaṃ taṇḍulaṃ nyasya taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ nyaset

prāsādena tu mantreṇa gaurībījena vardhanīm

vinyaseccaiva viprendra ! abhitaḥ kalaśairyutam

adhamaṃ tvaṣṭahastaṃ syān madhyamaṃ navahastakam

purāṇaṃ putranāśaḥ syāt keśayuktaṃ dhanakṣayaḥ

pūrve tu nava kumbhaṃ tu āgneyyāṃ navakumbhakam

vāruṇyāṃ navakaṃ vidvān vāyavyāṃ navakaṃ nyaset

sa vastrān sāpidhānāṃśca sakūrcān vāripūritān

lohaṃ priyaṃgupuṣpaṃ ca lavaṃgaṃ phalamallikā

parṇakā śatapatraṃ ca tailaṃ kastūri rocanā

rajanī bhadrakoṣṭhaṃ ca saralaṃ kṛṣṇamālikā

siṃhī ca śaṃkhapuṣpī ca lakṣmī sthalāravindakam

rudraparṇī mahālakṣmīrdhūrdhūreṇa samanvitam

nadyāstīre ca goṣṭhe ca balmīke vṛṣaśṛṃgake

vidyeśakuṃbhe nikṣipya paṃcagavyena saṃyutam

vāruṇyāṃ navake caiva * * saṃ vinyaset kramāt

saumyāyāṃ viṣṇumāvāhya * * caṇḍīśagocare

samidājya carūnlājānsarṣapena samanvitam

vyomavyāpiṃ japitvā tu ājyena paṃcaviṃśatiḥ

snāpayecchivakumbhaṃ tu vyomavyāpiṃ samuccaran

paṃcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgena snāpayet kalaśān budhaḥ

ācārya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt padārthīn pūjayet tataḥ

ekāśītiriti proktā ekonapaṃcāśat śṛṇu

iti vīratantre ekāśītisnapanavidhiḥ ekāśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

ekona paṃcāśatkovā tat sarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam

darbhamālābhirāveṣṭya vitāno parisobhitam

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā sūtrapātaṃ tu kārayet

madhye navapadaṃ sthitvā tadbāhyekaṃ tu vīthikā

śālinā sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ

madhyame'ṣṭa padaṃ kṛtvā gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

vastradvaya samāyuktaṃ sa kūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam

kalaśān vinyaset tatra savastraṃ sāpidhānakam

candanaṃ kuṃkumaṃ caiva meva māṃjī muraṃgulam

śatapuṣpaṃ tathā vyāghrī dhātrī pūrvā harītakī

haṃsapuṣpā gandhapuṣpā mahādroṇī samanvitam

ajaḥ kapālīṃ buddhiśca vajradehaḥ pramardanaḥ

agnīrudro hutāśī ca piṃgalaḥ pādako hariḥ

yāmyo mṛtyuharo dhātā (vidhātā) karṇakārikā

saṃyoktā ca viyoktā ca niyoktā ca prakīrtitaḥ

kālarudraśca vikhyāto dhātā dharmapatistathā

virūpākṣo dhūmravarṇo lohito daṃṣṭra vā budhaḥ

bhūtapālo lokapālaḥ kṣetrapālastathaiva ca

etepi daivatāḥ proktāḥ kalaśeṣūdake nyaset

aiśānyāmeka homaṃ vā palāśasamidhā saha

śālinī vāragodhūma mudge naiva samanvitam

hutvānte tu yadasyeti pūrṇāhutimathācaret

gaurībījena vardhanyāṃ snāpayecchivakumbhakam

paṃcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgena snāpayet sarvakumbhakam

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivaloke mahīyate

iti vīratantre ekonapaṃcāśat snapanavidhiḥ dvayaśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

snapanaṃ paṃcaviṃśacca brūhi me parameśvara !

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam

vitānopari saṃchannaṃ digdhvajaiśca pralambitam

gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā sūtrapātaṃ tu kārayet

madhye navapadaṃ sthitvā tadbāhyeka padaṃ tyajet

āḍhakaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā prasthaṃ vā śāli vinyaset

taṇḍulārdhaṃ tilaṃ nyasya darbhāṃścaiva paristaret
