paṃcaratna samāyuktaṃ sa kūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ aṣṭakumbhāṃśca vinyaset

paṃcagavya samāyuktaṃ savastraṃ sāpidhānakam

āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu catvāraḥ kumbha ucyate

elālavaṃga takkolaṃ timbujātiphalaṃgulam

dāntā dūrvā ca takkolaṃ puṃnāgaṃ jātipāṭalī

yathā krameṇa vinyasya pūrvādīśānamantakam

vibhūtiravyayaḥ śāstā pinākī tridaśādhipaḥ

jvalano dahano babhruḥ bhasmāntakaḥ kṣayāntakaḥ

kālarudraśca viprendra ! kalaśasyādhidevatāḥ

tato homaṃ prakartavyaṃ pālāśasamidhā saha

samidājyacarūnlājān sarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān

paṃcabrahma ṣaḍaṃgena yathā krameṇa hūyate

ācārya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād vastrahemāṃgulīyakaiḥ

gaurībījena vardhanyāṃ snāpayet tu viśeṣataḥ

paṃcavarṇa havirdadyāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

paṃcaviṃśatirevoktā ṣoḍaśaṃ ca tataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre paṃcaviṃśati snapanavidhiḥ tryaśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

kalaśān ṣoḍaśān kiṃ vā brūhi me parameśvara

prāsāda syāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam

vitāne nordhvamācchādya digdhajaiśca pralambitam

sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktaṃ sūtrapātaṃ tu kārayet

madhye navapadaṃ sthitvā tasyāvṛta padaṃ tyajet

śālinā vikiret tatra pūrvoktavidhinā saha

paristarecca darbhaiśca gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

gaurī bījena vardhanyāṃ sthāpayed deśikottamaḥ

āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu caturaḥ kalaśān nyaset

vastrayugmena saṃveṣṭya viṇikābhiralaṃkṛtam

gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ caiva kṣīraṃ dadhighṛtaṃ tathā

sadyādīśānaparyantaṃ yathā krameṇa vinyaset

snāpayecchivakumbhaṃ tu vyomavyāpi samuccaran

paṃcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgena sthāpayet kalaśāṃstataḥ

ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ dadyād vastrahemāṃgulīyakaiḥ

iti vīratantre ṣoḍaśasnapanavidhiḥ caturaśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

navakumbhāḥ kathaṃ deva ! brūhi me parameśvara !

prāsādasyāgrataḥ kuryānmaṇṭapaṃ caturaśrakam

vitānoparisaṃchannaṃ digdhvajaiśca pralambitam

sarvālaṃkāra saṃyuktaṃ gomayenopalepayet

sūtrapātaḥ prakartavyaḥ ṣaṭsūtraṃ prākśiro bhavet

śālinā vikiret tatra āḍhakaṃ kalaśaṃ prati

paristaret tu darbhaiśca padmamaṣṭadalaṃ likhet

savastraṃ paṃcaratnaṃ ca sakūrcaṃ sāpidhānakam

savastrānsahiraṇyāṃśca gandhodakena pūritān

pūrve gomūtrakaṃ caiva gomayaṃ cāgnideśake

ājyaṃ cottarataḥ sthāpya nair-ṛte ca kuśāmbujam

pūrvādīśāna paryantaṃ aṣṭavidyeśvarān nyaset

tato homaṃ prakurvīta palāśasamidhā saha

saptadravyeṇa saṃyuktaṃ paṃcabrahmasamāyutam

hutvānte tu yadasyeti sviṣṭakṛddhūyate kramāt

ācārya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād vastrahemāṃgulīyakaiḥ

gaurībījena vardhanyāmanantādi svamantrakaiḥ

adhamasyādhamaṃ proktaṃ śītakumbhaṃ tataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre navakumbha snapanavidhiḥ paṃcāśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

etat sarvaṃ samāsena bhagavanvaktumarhasi

jvaramārī vināśāya sarvāriṣṭavināśanam

triṃśat tanmadhyamaṃ proktaṃ aṣṭāviṃśati cādhamam

bimbaprabhānvabhāyuktaṃ bhinnaṃ kālaṃ ca varjayet

kṣālayed dhṛdaye naiva darbhairupari vinyaset

śālyardhataṇḍulān nyasya taṇḍulārdha tilān nyaset

vastrapūtena toyena pūrayedghaṭameva ca

uśīraṃ candanaṃ caiva kumbhamadhye hṛdā nyaset

vajraṃ ca padmarāgaṃ ca vaiḍūryaṃ mauktikaṃ tathā

sakūrcaṃ sāpidhānaṃ ca puṣpamālā samanvitam

paṃcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgena arcayed gandhapuṣpakaiḥ

pūrvarātre'dhivāsyātha nṛttageya samanvitam

evameva nyaset kuṃbhaṃ śivastatrādhidevatā

kumbhasya saṃnidhau vipra ! śānti homaṃ tu kārayet

evameva prakāreṇa upakumbhaṃ ca vinyaset

mantranyāsoktamārgeṇa sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ tu kārayet

liṃgasyopari viprendra ! hastamātroccamucyate

abakraṃ nirvraṇaṃ kṛtvā viṣṭharatrayasaṃyutam

dvau pārśvau pṛṣṭhataścaiva vinyaset tu tripādikān

nālaṃ tatraiva vinyasya caturaṃguladairghyakam

vyomavyāpiṃ samuccārya kumbhamāropayet tataḥ

aho rātraṃ sravan mūrdhni sarvadoṣanikṛntanam

sandhyāyāṃ pūjayet paścāt pūrvoktavidhinā saha

dinānte'pi ca viprendra ! kumbhāvarohaṇaṃ kuru

ācārya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād vastrahemāṃgulīyakaiḥ

śaivān saṃbhojayet tatra samayān bhojayet tataḥ

śītakumbhavidhiḥ prokta adbhutānāṃ vidhiṃ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre śītakumbhavidhiḥ ṣaḍaśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

adbhutānāṃ vidhiḥ ko vā tat sarvaṃ brūhi me prabho

bhūmikampe diśādāhe dhūmaketānupasthite

anāvṛṣṭyādikāle tu grahaśāntiṃ samācaret

śālinā sthaṇḍilaṃ kṛtvā taṇḍulān vinyaset tataḥ

sthāpayeccaiva viprendra ! trayaṃ caiva tu madhyame

trayāṇāṃ madhyame caiva grahāṇāṃ caiva vinyaset

candramāgneya digbhāge aṃgārakaṃ tu dakṣiṇe

ketuṃ ca vāyudigbhāge uttare tu bṛhaspatim

bhāskarasyeśvaro devaḥ pūrvābhimukha eva ca

rāhoḥ kāliṃga devastu yamodevaśśanestathā

budhaśca viṣṇudaivatya ityetā grahadevatāḥ

indrāṇī ca prajāpatyaḥ sarpā brahmādhidevatāḥ

teṣāṃ pūrve tu viprendra ! sikataiḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ kuru

ādityasya ca vṛttaṃ syāśukrasya paṃcakoṇakam

rāhoḥ śūrpākṛtiścaiva dhanuścaiva śanaiścare

bṛhaspatestu vijñeyaṃ budhasya bhagavān bhavet

tattadvarṇāni puṣpāṇi pūjayed deśikottamaḥ

aṃgārakasya khadiraṃ apāmārgaṃ budhasya ca

śamīṃ śanaiścare vidyād rāhoḥ dūrvāhutirbhavet

aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi aṣṭāviṃśatireva vā

evaṃ lakṣaṇa saṃyuktaṃ homayecchivadīkṣitaḥ

eṣāmīśeti mantreṇa ādityasyaiva hūyate

śukrahomamiti proktaṃ āpyāyassomamabravīt

agnirmūrdhā ca sometā aṃgārakasya hūyate

śanno devī prajāpate śanihomaṃ prakīrtitam

ādiyasyo bṛhatsāmajñānamācāryaṃ homamucyate

paṃcabrahmaṣaḍaṃgena prāsādena samanvitam

kṣīrasnānaṃ prakartavyaṃ śrīrudreṇa viśeṣataḥ

ativṛṣṭirbhavet tatra subhikṣaṃ kurute tataḥ

mahāmārī pravṛtte tu snapanaṃ tu sahasrakam

raktakanyā samutpanne śṛṇu tatvaṃ pitāmaha

rājñāṃ grāmaṃ kṣetraṃ vā vināśaṃ vrajati dhruvam

vāhanānāṃ kṣayo yāmye bhāryānāśastu nair-ṛte

jvarāpasmāraṃ vāyavyāṃ saumye vyādhimavāpnuyāt

nirmālyakuṇḍe dṛśyeta rājarāṣṭravināśanam

dhānyaśālāya nāśaḥ syādvāre dveśmagṛnyakam

brāhmaṇakṣatriyāṇāṃ ca antarmaṇḍalake bhavet

madhyahāre'pi dṛśyante śūdrāṇāṃ kṣayamāpnuyāt

agnijādikṣayaṃ caiva mahāmaryādibhittike

evameva prakāreṇa adbhutaṃ dṛśyate bhayam

vyapohya sakalaṃ tatra paṃcagavyena prokṣayet

ghanaṃ kṛtvā tu taddeśe śāntihomaṃ tadūrdhvataḥ

pūrvavadvidhinā yuktaṃ homaṃ kuryāt tu deśikaḥ

evaṃ kṛtvā mahāśāntiṃ sarvadoṣaharāya ca

vyapohya sakalaṃ tatra diśāhomaṃ tu kārayet

snāpayed devadeveśaṃ mahāhavirnivedayet

puṇyāhaṃ kārayet tatra pavamānena prokṣayet

anyevācādbhutotpanne snapanaṃ kārayet tataḥ

adbhutānāṃ vidhiḥ proktaḥ kṛttikādīpa ucyate

iti vīratantre adbhutaśāntividhiḥ saptāśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

ko vā tu kṛttikādīpaḥ tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

kārtike tu tathā māse nakṣatre kṛttikāyute

kṛttikādīpahīne tu jvaramārī pravartate

navavastreṇa kṛtvā tu vartiṃ kārpāsa saṃyutam

śiva prapūrite pātre vinyased dhṛdayena tu

prāsādamūrdhni vinyasya hṛdayena tu mantrataḥ

jātī vā campakaṃ vāpi kramukaṃ vaiṇavaṃ tu vā

prāsādasadṛśaṃ vāpi galāntaṃ vāpi kārayet

etaddīpena saṃyojya paṃcāśatpaṃcaviṃśatiḥ

vastreṇa veṣṭayet tatra ghṛtena ca pariplutam

dīpaṃ saṃyojya viprendra ! sadyojātena mantrataḥ

vimāna pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryādgarbhagehaṃ praveśayet

ācārya dakṣiṇāṃ dadyād vastrahemāṃgulīyakaiḥ

dīpadaṇḍaṃ tu viprendra ! viṣṇudīpena yojayet

prāsādena tu mantreṇa dīpān sarvatra yojayet

nānādīpa samāyuktaṃ rātrau mahotsavaṃ kuru

anādiveśyā saṃyuktaṃ gaṇikādāsibhissaha

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martyaḥ śivalokamavāpnuyāt

kṛttikādīpa evoktaḥ agrāśanamataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre kṛttikādīpavidhiḥ aṣṭāśītitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

agrāśanaṃ kathaṃ deva ! tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

śubhe muhūrte maṃgalye bhūmi saṃgrahaṇaṃ bhavet

caitre cāryāpabharaṇī kuryāt bhūmipraveśanam

dvitīyā daśamī grāhyā vṛṣavṛścika karkaṭāḥ

bhūmipraveśanaṃ proktaṃ kṛṣikarma tvathocyate

mūlā punarvasuścaiva rohiṇī cottaratrayam

navamīṃ caiva viprendra ! varjayet kṛṣi karmasu

vṛṣabhe karkaṭe mīne mithune siṃhalagnake

śvetau raktau ca kapilau nāgacchinnau vṛṣau tathā

gocarmarajjunā caiva halaṃ caiva tu bandhayet

prāgukta vasayedbhūmiṃ sadyojātena mantrataḥ

mūlānurādhā hastā ca maghārauhiṇi revatī

śatabhiṣak ca nakṣatraṃ praśastaṃ bījakarmasu

ekādaśī tṛtīyā ca praśastā bījakarmaṇi

yamakumbha tulā madhyāḥ śeṣāṃstu parivarjayet

evameva prakāreṇa bījāvāpanamucyate

bhūmi praveśanaṃ kṛtvā pūrvābhimukhamāsthitaḥ

mṛdena bhūmirūpaṃ tu prākśiraḥ kārayed budhaḥ

puṇyāhaṃ vācayet tatra tāmbūlaṃ dāpayejjanān

bindunāda samāyuktaṃ mūlamantramihocyate

naivedyaṃ pāyasaṃ dadyāllājāpūpa samanvitam

aṃjaliṃ śālicūrṇaṃ tu mūlamantra samanvitam

namo'stu subhage ! nityaṃ kṛṣikarmedhyatāmiha

ācāryaṃ pūjayet paścād daivajñaṃ pūjayet tataḥ

tato bhaktajanaissārdhaṃ praviśedālayaṃ prati

caṇḍeśa pratimāyuktaṃ sarvātodya samanvitam

hastatrayottarāyāṃ ca rohiṇyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ

śukaṃ grāhyāstramantreṇa śirasā dhārayet tataḥ

raviraśmisusaṃtaptaṃ prabhinnaṃ kṣipalūkhale

hastaṃ ca śravaṇaṃ caiva revatyaśvatireva ca

śraviṣṭhā śatabhiṣak caiva anūrādhā triruttarā

meṣavṛścikamīnāstu varjyāḥ śeṣāstu pūjitāḥ

taṇḍulaṃ śarkarāyuktaṃ nālikeraphalairyutam

pratimānāṃ ca sarvāsāṃ evamevākṣamaṃ dadet

evaṃ nivedayet paścāt tāmbūlaṃ dāpayet tataḥ

agrāśanamidaṃ proktaṃ bālasthāpanamataḥ śṛṇu

iti vīratantre agrāśanavidhirekonanavatitamaḥ paṭalaḥ

bālasthānaṃ kathaṃ deva ! tatsarvaṃ brūhi me prabho !

dvāramānena vā ku * bhittimānena vā punaḥ

śailajaṃ dārujaṃ vāpi bālaliṃgaṃ karoti hi

pūjāṃśoccaṃ nava kuryādviṣva? māgānvitaṃ tu ?

tritālaṃ madhyamoktaṃ adhamaṃ tu dvitāla * * ?

tena mānena kartavyaṃ liṃgasya bhāgamuttamam

adhamo brahmabhāgaḥ syān madhyamo * ṇu? ucyate

liṃgasūtraṃ tu kartavyaṃ nālaṃ caiva tu kalpa * ?

bālaliṃgaṃ prakartavyaṃ evameva krameṇa tu

vāruṇe vāpi kartavyaṃ bālasthānamihocyate

trihastaṃ paṃcahastaṃ vā saptahastamathāpi vā

bālasthānasya vāme tu prapālitvā * * * *?

vṛttaṃ vā caturarthaṃ vā a * *?ni * * * *?

sthā *?lopari vinyasya śivakumbhaṃ ca vardhanīm

āvāhya devadeveśaṃ gandhapuṣpādinārcayet

pūrvavat sthaṇḍile vipra ! atrāhyeva? tu kārayet
